  <!-- JAVASCRIPT — BAGIAN B                                        -->
  <!-- =========================================================== -->
  <script>
    /* =========================================================
       1. HELPER UMUM
       ========================================================= */

    function refreshIcons() {
      if (window.lucide) window.lucide.createIcons();
    }

    function showBanner(text, type, durasi) {
      type = type || 'info';
      durasi = durasi || 3500;

      var b = document.getElementById('status-banner');
      var t = document.getElementById('status-text');
      if (!b) return;

      if (_bannerTimeout) {
        clearTimeout(_bannerTimeout);
        _bannerTimeout = null;
      }

      // Hapus semua class warna
      b.classList.remove('hidden',
        'bg-emerald-600','border-emerald-400','text-white',
        'bg-red-600','border-red-400',
        'bg-blue-600','border-blue-400',
        'bg-slate-800','border-slate-600'
      );

      // Tambah class warna solid
      if (type === 'success') b.classList.add('bg-emerald-600','border-emerald-400','text-white');
      else if (type === 'error') b.classList.add('bg-red-600','border-red-400','text-white');
      else if (type === 'warning') b.classList.add('bg-slate-800','border-slate-600','text-white');
      else b.classList.add('bg-slate-800','border-slate-600','text-slate-100');

      t.innerText = text;
      b.classList.remove('hidden');

      _bannerTimeout = setTimeout(function() {
        b.classList.add('hidden');
        _bannerTimeout = null;
      }, durasi);
    }

    function openModal(id) {
      var el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (el) el.classList.remove('hidden');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function closeModal(id) {
      var el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (el) el.classList.add('hidden');
    }

    /**
     * Potong angka ke 2 desimal TANPA pembulatan (versi client-side)
     * 90       → "90"
     * 90.5     → "90.5"
     * 90.256   → "90.25"
     */
    function potongDuaDesimalJS(angka) {
      var s = String(angka);
      var match = s.match(/^(-?\d+)(?:\.(\d+))?/);
      if (!match) return s;
      var intPart = match[1];
      var decPart = match[2];
      if (!decPart) return intPart;
      decPart = decPart.substring(0, 2);
      return intPart + '.' + decPart;
    }


    /* =========================================================
       2. THEME TOGGLE
       ========================================================= */

    function initTheme() {
      var saved = localStorage.getItem('simak_theme');
      var html = document.documentElement;
      if (saved === 'dark') { html.classList.add('dark'); html.classList.remove('light'); }
      else { html.classList.remove('dark'); html.classList.add('light'); }
      updateThemeButton();
    }

    function toggleTheme() {
      var html = document.documentElement;
      var isDark = html.classList.contains('dark');
      if (isDark) {
        html.classList.remove('dark'); html.classList.add('light');
        localStorage.setItem('simak_theme', 'light');
      } else {
        html.classList.add('dark'); html.classList.remove('light');
        localStorage.setItem('simak_theme', 'dark');
      }
      updateThemeButton();
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function updateThemeButton() {
      var html = document.documentElement;
      var isDark = html.classList.contains('dark');
      var icon = document.getElementById('theme-icon');
      var label = document.getElementById('theme-label');
      var btn = document.getElementById('btn-theme-toggle');
      if (!icon || !label) return;

      if (isDark) {
        icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'sun');
        label.textContent = 'Terang';
        if (btn) {
          btn.classList.remove('bg-amber-100','text-amber-900','border-amber-300');
          btn.classList.add('bg-slate-800','text-slate-300','border-slate-700');
        }
      } else {
        icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'moon');
        label.textContent = 'Gelap';
        if (btn) {
          btn.classList.remove('bg-slate-800','text-slate-300','border-slate-700');
          btn.classList.add('bg-amber-100','text-amber-900','border-amber-300');
        }
      }
    }

    /* =========================================================
       4. AUTH
       ========================================================= */

    function handleLoginSubmit(e) {
      if (e) e.preventDefault();

      var u = document.getElementById('login-username').value.trim();
      var p = document.getElementById('login-password').value.trim();

      if (!u || !p) {
        document.getElementById('login-error-msg').classList.remove('hidden');
        document.getElementById('login-error-text').innerText = 'Isi username dan password!';
        return;
      }

      var btnSubmit = document.getElementById('btn-login-submit');
      var btnOriginalText = btnSubmit.innerHTML;
      btnSubmit.disabled = true;
      btnSubmit.innerHTML = '<span>Memuat data...</span><i data-lucide="loader" class="w-4 h-4 animate-spin"></i>';
      refreshIcons();

      document.getElementById('login-error-msg').classList.add('hidden');

      var loginScreen = document.getElementById('screen-login');
      var loadingScreen = document.getElementById('screen-loading');
      if (loginScreen) loginScreen.classList.add('hidden');
      if (loadingScreen) loadingScreen.classList.remove('hidden');

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
            btnSubmit.disabled = false;
            btnSubmit.innerHTML = btnOriginalText;
            refreshIcons();

            if (res && res.status === 'success') {
              currentUser = { username: res.user.username, name: res.user.nama, role: res.user.role };
              localStorage.setItem('simak_user', JSON.stringify(currentUser));

              applyDataFromServer_(res);
              showMainApp();
            } else {
              if (loadingScreen) loadingScreen.classList.add('hidden');
              if (loginScreen) loginScreen.classList.remove('hidden');
              document.getElementById('login-error-msg').classList.remove('hidden');
              document.getElementById('login-error-text').innerText = res.message || 'Akses anda telah dihapus!';
            }
          })
          .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
            btnSubmit.disabled = false;
            btnSubmit.innerHTML = btnOriginalText;
            refreshIcons();
            if (loadingScreen) loadingScreen.classList.add('hidden');
            if (loginScreen) loginScreen.classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('login-error-msg').classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('login-error-text').innerText = 'Error: ' + err.message;
          })
          .loginDanAmbilData(u, p);
      } else {
        btnSubmit.disabled = false;
        btnSubmit.innerHTML = btnOriginalText;
        if (loadingScreen) loadingScreen.classList.add('hidden');
        if (loginScreen) loginScreen.classList.remove('hidden');
        document.getElementById('login-error-msg').classList.remove('hidden');
        document.getElementById('login-error-text').innerText = 'Mode offline: Tidak dapat login.';
      }
    }

    function handleLogout() {
      currentUser = null;
      localStorage.removeItem('simak_user');
      document.getElementById('main-app-container').classList.add('hidden');

      _pkksFolderPath = [];
      _pkksCurrentFolder = null;
      _pkksFolderCache = {};
      _pkksFileCache = {};
      _pkksFolderLoaded = false;
      _pkksCurrentFolderLoaded = false;

      var loadingScreen = document.getElementById('screen-loading');
      var loginScreen = document.getElementById('screen-login');
      if (loadingScreen) loadingScreen.classList.add('hidden');
      if (loginScreen) loginScreen.classList.remove('hidden');

      var lu = document.getElementById('login-username');
      var lp = document.getElementById('login-password');
      var le = document.getElementById('login-error-msg');
      if (lu) lu.value = '';
      if (lp) lp.value = '';
      if (le) le.classList.add('hidden');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function showMainApp() {
      var loadingScreen = document.getElementById('screen-loading');
      var loginScreen = document.getElementById('screen-login');
      if (loadingScreen) loadingScreen.classList.add('hidden');
      if (loginScreen) loginScreen.classList.add('hidden');

      document.getElementById('main-app-container').classList.remove('hidden');
      document.getElementById('header-username').innerText = currentUser.name;
      document.getElementById('header-role').innerText = currentUser.role.toUpperCase();

      renderAllViews();
      showBanner('✅ Selamat datang, ' + currentUser.name + '! Data berhasil dimuat.', 'success', 15000);
      refreshIcons();
    }

    /**
     * Helper: pindahkan data dari response server ke appState
     * Dipakai oleh handleLoginSubmit() dan getInitialDataDirect()
     */
    function applyDataFromServer_(res) {
      if (!res) return;
      if (res.dataSiswa) appState.siswa = res.dataSiswa;
      if (res.dataGuru) appState.guru = res.dataGuru;
      if (res.sorogan) appState.sorogan = res.sorogan;
      if (res.nabil) appState.nabil = res.nabil;
      if (res.nilai) appState.nilai = res.nilai;
      if (res.pengguna) appState.pengguna = res.pengguna;
      if (res.mappingRaport) appState.mapping = res.mappingRaport;
      if (res.mappingIjazah) appState.mappingIjazah = res.mappingIjazah;
      if (res.jadwal) appState.jadwal = res.jadwal;
      window.appState = appState;
    }


    /* =========================================================
       5. ROUTING TAB
       ========================================================= */

    function switchTab(tabId) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-view').forEach(function(el) { el.classList.add('hidden'); });
      document.querySelectorAll('.nav-btn').forEach(function(btn) {
        btn.classList.remove('bg-emerald-600','text-white','shadow-md');
        btn.classList.add('text-slate-400');
      });

      var activeView = document.getElementById('view-' + tabId);
      var activeNav = document.getElementById('nav-' + tabId);
      if (activeView) activeView.classList.remove('hidden');
      if (activeNav) {
        activeNav.classList.remove('text-slate-400');
        activeNav.classList.add('bg-emerald-600','text-white','shadow-md');
      }

      if (tabId === 'nilai') {
        setTimeout(function() {
          updateFilterNilaiAngkatan();
          renderNilaiTable();
        }, 100);
      }

      if (tabId === 'muadalah') {
        setTimeout(function() { initMuadalahTab(); }, 100);
      }

      if (tabId === 'absensi') {
        setTimeout(function() {
          populateFilterKelasManual();
          populateFilterNabil();
          populateFilterSorogan();
          renderAbsenSiswaTable();
        }, 100);
      }

      if (tabId === 'jadwal') {
        setTimeout(function() {
          loadJadwalIframe();
          setTimeout(function() {
            var iframeJadwal = document.getElementById('iframeJadwal');
            if (iframeJadwal && typeof kirimDataKeIframeJadwal === 'function') {
              kirimDataKeIframeJadwal(iframeJadwal);
            }
          }, 500);
        }, 100);
      }

      if (tabId === 'pkks') {
        setTimeout(function() {
          kembaliKeMenuPKKS();
          updateViewToggleButtons();
          if (!_pkksFolderLoaded) {
            muatDaftarFolderPKKS();
          }
        }, 100);
      }

      refreshIcons();
    }

    // Listener pesan dari iframe jadwal
    window.addEventListener('message', function(event) {
      var data = event.data;
      if (!data) return;

      if (data.type === 'JADWAL_UPDATED') {
        if (Array.isArray(data.jadwal) && window.appState) {
          window.appState.jadwal = data.jadwal;
        }
        return;
      }

      if (data.type === 'JADWAL_SAVE_REQUEST') {
        if (!Array.isArray(data.jadwal)) return;
        if (window.appState) window.appState.jadwal = data.jadwal;

        if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
          google.script.run
            .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
              if (res && res.status === 'success') {
                showBanner('✅ Jadwal tersimpan ke sheet (' + data.jadwal.length + ' baris).', 'success');
              } else {
                showBanner('⚠️ Gagal simpan ke sheet: ' + (res.message || 'Unknown'), 'error');
              }
            })
            .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
              showBanner('❌ Gagal simpan jadwal: ' + (err.message || err), 'error');
            })
            .saveJadwalBatchDirect(data.jadwal);
        } else {
          showBanner('⚠️ Mode offline — gagal simpan ke sheet.', 'error');
        }
        return;
      }
    });

    function renderAllViews(keepPage) {
      document.getElementById('dash-stat-siswa').innerText = (appState.siswa || []).length;
      document.getElementById('dash-stat-guru').innerText = (appState.guru || []).length;
      document.getElementById('dash-stat-sorogan').innerText = (appState.sorogan || []).length;
      document.getElementById('dash-stat-nabil').innerText = (appState.nabil || []).length;

      var jumlahLulus = (appState.siswa || []).filter(function(s) {
        return String(s.status || '').trim().toLowerCase() === 'lulus';
      }).length;
      document.getElementById('dash-stat-lulus').innerText = jumlahLulus;

      muadalahState.konfigAktif = null;
      muadalahState.siswa = [];
      var wrapperM = document.getElementById('muadalah-table-wrapper');
      var emptyM = document.getElementById('muadalah-empty');
      if (wrapperM) wrapperM.classList.add('hidden');
      if (emptyM) emptyM.classList.remove('hidden');

      // ★ Siswa — hanya reset kalau bukan keepPage
      if (!keepPage) {
        resetSiswaPagination();
        resetNilaiPagination();
      }
      updateFilterKelas();
      updateFilterAngkatan();
      updateFilterStatus();
      renderSiswaTable();

      updateFilterNilaiKelas();
      updateFilterNilaiAngkatan();
      renderNilaiTable();

      renderGuruTable();

      populateAbsenKelas();
      populateFilterKelasManual();
      populateFilterNabil();
      populateFilterSorogan();
      renderAbsenSiswaTable();
      populateCetakFilterNabil();
      populateCetakFilterSorogan();
    }


    /* =========================================================
       7. MODUL SISWA
       ========================================================= */

    /* ----- Helper filter ----- */

    function getUniqueKelas() {
      var set = new Set();
      (appState.siswa || []).forEach(function(s) {
        if (s.kelas && s.kelas.trim() !== '') set.add(s.kelas.trim());
      });
      var arr = Array.from(set);
      var urutan = { 'X': 1, 'XI': 2, 'XII': 3 };
      arr.sort(function(a, b) {
        var ap = (a.match(/^(X|XI|XII)/) || [''])[0];
        var bp = (b.match(/^(X|XI|XII)/) || [''])[0];
        if (urutan[ap] && urutan[bp]) {
          if (urutan[ap] !== urutan[bp]) return urutan[ap] - urutan[bp];
          return a.localeCompare(b);
        }
        return a.localeCompare(b);
      });
      return arr;
    }

    function getUniqueStatus() {
      var set = new Set();
      (appState.siswa || []).forEach(function(s) {
        if (s.status && s.status.trim() !== '') set.add(s.status.trim());
      });
      var arr = Array.from(set);
      var urutan = { 'Aktif': 1, 'Lulus': 2, 'Mutasi': 3, 'Keluar': 4, 'Pindah': 5 };
      arr.sort(function(a, b) {
        var ao = urutan[a] || 999, bo = urutan[b] || 999;
        if (ao !== bo) return ao - bo;
        return a.localeCompare(b);
      });
      return arr;
    }

    function getStatusBadgeClass(status) {
      if (!status || status.trim() === '') return 'bg-slate-500/20 text-slate-400';
      var s = status.toLowerCase().trim();
      var map = {
        'aktif': 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300',
        'lulus': 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-300',
        'mutasi': 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300',
        'pindah': 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300',
        'keluar': 'bg-red-500/20 text-red-300',
        'drop out': 'bg-red-500/20 text-red-300',
        'do': 'bg-red-500/20 text-red-300',
        'cuti': 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-300',
        'pindahan': 'bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300',
        'baru': 'bg-teal-500/20 text-teal-300'
      };
      for (var k in map) {
        if (s.includes(k)) return map[k];
      }
      return 'bg-slate-500/20 text-slate-300';
    }

    /* ----- Update dropdown filter ----- */

    function updateFilterKelas() {
      var select = document.getElementById('filter-siswa-kelas');
      if (!select) return;
      var kelasList = getUniqueKelas();
      var currentValue = select.value;

      select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Semua Kelas</option><option value="TANPA">Tanpa Kelas</option>';
      kelasList.forEach(function(k) {
        var opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = k; opt.textContent = k;
        select.appendChild(opt);
      });
      if (currentValue && select.querySelector('option[value="' + currentValue + '"]')) select.value = currentValue;
    }

    function updateFilterStatus() {
      var select = document.getElementById('filter-siswa-status');
      if (!select) return;
      var statusList = getUniqueStatus();
      var currentValue = select.value;
      select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Semua Status</option>';
      statusList.forEach(function(s) {
        var opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = s; opt.textContent = s;
        select.appendChild(opt);
      });
      if (currentValue && select.querySelector('option[value="' + currentValue + '"]')) select.value = currentValue;
    }

    function updateFilterAngkatan() {
      var select = document.getElementById('filter-siswa-angkatan');
      if (!select) return;
      var currentValue = select.value;
      var set = new Set();
      (appState.siswa || []).forEach(function(s) {
        var m = String(s.masuk || '').trim();
        var match = m.match(/\d{4}/);
        if (match) set.add(match[0]);
      });
      var arr = Array.from(set);
      arr.sort(function(a, b) { return parseInt(b, 10) - parseInt(a, 10); });

      select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Semua Angkatan</option>';
      arr.forEach(function(a) {
        var opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = a; opt.textContent = a;
        select.appendChild(opt);
      });
      if (currentValue && select.querySelector('option[value="' + currentValue + '"]')) select.value = currentValue;
    }

    /* ----- Tabel siswa ----- */

    function renderSiswaTable() {
      var search = (document.getElementById('search-siswa') && document.getElementById('search-siswa').value || '').toLowerCase();
      var kFilter = (document.getElementById('filter-siswa-kelas') || {}).value || '';
      var aFilter = (document.getElementById('filter-siswa-angkatan') || {}).value || '';
      var sFilter = (document.getElementById('filter-siswa-status') || {}).value || '';
      var pageSize = parseInt((document.getElementById('siswa-page-size') || {}).value) || 25;

      var tbody = document.getElementById('table-siswa-body');
      if (!tbody) return;

      var filtered = (appState.siswa || []).filter(function(s) {
        var matchText = (s.nama || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
                        (s.noInduk || '').includes(search) ||
                        (s.alamat || '').toLowerCase().includes(search);

        var matchKelas = true;
        if (kFilter === 'TANPA') matchKelas = !s.kelas || s.kelas.trim() === '';
        else if (kFilter) matchKelas = s.kelas && (s.kelas === kFilter || s.kelas.startsWith(kFilter + '-') || s.kelas.startsWith(kFilter + ' '));

        var matchStatus = true;
        if (sFilter) matchStatus = s.status === sFilter;

        var matchAngkatan = true;
        if (aFilter) {
          var mMatch = String(s.masuk || '').match(/\d{4}/);
          matchAngkatan = (mMatch && mMatch[0] === aFilter);
        }

        return matchText && matchKelas && matchStatus && matchAngkatan;
      });

      var state = paginationState.siswa;
      state.filteredData = filtered;
      state.pageSize = pageSize === 0 ? filtered.length : pageSize;
      state.totalPages = pageSize === 0 ? 1 : Math.ceil(filtered.length / pageSize);
      if (state.currentPage > state.totalPages) state.currentPage = 1;
      if (state.currentPage < 1) state.currentPage = 1;

      var startIdx = (state.currentPage - 1) * state.pageSize;
      var endIdx = state.pageSize === 0 ? filtered.length : Math.min(startIdx + state.pageSize, filtered.length);
      var pageData = filtered.slice(startIdx, endIdx);

      if (pageData.length === 0) {
        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" class="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-400">Tidak ada data santri yang cocok.</td></tr>';
      } else {
        tbody.innerHTML = pageData.map(function(s, idx) {
          var globalIndex = startIdx + idx + 1;
          var isBerhenti = s.kelas && s.kelas.toLowerCase().includes('berhenti');
          var statusDisplay = s.status || 'Aktif';
          if (isBerhenti && statusDisplay === 'Aktif') statusDisplay = 'Berhenti';

          var ket = String(s.keterangan || '').trim().toLowerCase();
          var peringatan = '';
          if (ket !== 'lengkap') {
            peringatan = ' <span class="text-[10px] font-bold text-red-400 whitespace-nowrap">(Data Tidak Lengkap)</span>';
          }

          return '<tr class="hover:bg-slate-800/60 transition">' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center text-white">' + globalIndex + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 font-mono-code font-bold text-white">' + (s.noInduk || '-') + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-white">' + (s.nama || '-') + peringatan + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-white">' + (s.kelas && s.kelas.trim() !== '' ?
              '<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-700 text-white font-medium">' + s.kelas + '</span>' :
              '<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-700/50 text-slate-300 italic text-[10px]">Tanpa Kelas</span>') + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-white">' + (s.tetala || '-') + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-white max-w-[180px] truncate">' + (s.alamat || '-') + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center"><span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold ' + getStatusBadgeClass(statusDisplay) + '">' + statusDisplay + '</span></td>' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center"><div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">' +
              '<button onclick="bukaDetailSiswa(\'' + s.noInduk + '\')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-blue-400" title="Lihat Detail"><i data-lucide="eye" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i></button>' +
              '<button onclick="editSiswaModal(\'' + s.noInduk + '\')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-amber-400" title="Edit Santri"><i data-lucide="edit-3" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i></button>' +
              '<button onclick="hapusSiswa(\'' + s.noInduk + '\')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-red-950 text-red-400" title="Hapus Santri"><i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i></button>' +
            '</div></td></tr>';
        }).join('');
      }
      updatePaginationControls();
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function changeSiswaPage(direction) {
      var newPage = paginationState.siswa.currentPage + direction;
      if (newPage < 1 || newPage > paginationState.siswa.totalPages) return;
      paginationState.siswa.currentPage = newPage;
      renderSiswaTable();
    }

    function updatePaginationControls() {
      var state = paginationState.siswa;
      var totalPages = state.totalPages, currentPage = state.currentPage;

      var prevBtn = document.getElementById('siswa-prev-btn');
      var nextBtn = document.getElementById('siswa-next-btn');
      var pageDisp = document.getElementById('siswa-current-page-display');
      if (prevBtn) prevBtn.disabled = currentPage <= 1;
      if (nextBtn) nextBtn.disabled = currentPage >= totalPages || totalPages === 0;
      if (pageDisp) pageDisp.textContent = 'Halaman ' + currentPage;

      var prevB = document.getElementById('siswa-prev-btn-bottom');
      var nextB = document.getElementById('siswa-next-btn-bottom');
      var infoB = document.getElementById('siswa-page-info-bottom');
      if (prevB) prevB.disabled = currentPage <= 1;
      if (nextB) nextB.disabled = currentPage >= totalPages || totalPages === 0;
      if (infoB) infoB.textContent = 'Halaman ' + currentPage + ' dari ' + (totalPages || 1);

      var totalData = state.filteredData.length;
      var startIdx = (currentPage - 1) * state.pageSize;
      var endIdx = state.pageSize === 0 ? totalData : Math.min(startIdx + state.pageSize, totalData);

      var startEl = document.getElementById('siswa-start-index');
      var endEl = document.getElementById('siswa-end-index');
      var totalEl = document.getElementById('siswa-total-data');
      var totalBottomEl = document.getElementById('siswa-total-data-bottom');
      if (startEl) startEl.textContent = totalData === 0 ? 0 : startIdx + 1;
      if (endEl) endEl.textContent = totalData === 0 ? 0 : endIdx;
      if (totalEl) totalEl.textContent = totalData;
      if (totalBottomEl) totalBottomEl.textContent = totalData;
    }

    function resetSiswaPagination() { paginationState.siswa.currentPage = 1; }

    /* ----- CRUD form siswa ----- */

    function openModalTambahSiswa() {
      document.getElementById('modal-siswa-title').innerText = 'Tambah Santri Baru (38 Kolom)';

      document.getElementById('f-noinduk').value        = '202300' + (appState.siswa.length + 1);
      document.getElementById('f-nama').value           = '';
      document.getElementById('f-nisn').value           = '';
      document.getElementById('f-nik').value            = '';

      document.getElementById('f-kelamin').value        = 'Laki-laki';

      document.getElementById('f-anakke').value         = '';
      document.getElementById('f-alamatsingkat').value  = '';
      document.getElementById('f-alamat').value         = '';
      document.getElementById('f-sd').value             = '';
      document.getElementById('f-mts').value            = '';
      document.getElementById('f-alamatmts').value      = '';
      document.getElementById('f-npsn').value           = '';
      document.getElementById('f-kk').value             = '';
      document.getElementById('f-notlp').value          = '';
      document.getElementById('f-ayah').value           = '';
      document.getElementById('f-tetalaayah').value     = '';
      document.getElementById('f-sekolahayah').value    = '';
      document.getElementById('f-pekerjaanayah').value  = '';
      document.getElementById('f-ibu').value            = '';

      document.getElementById('f-keluar').value         = '';

    function simpanDataSiswaDariForm() {
      var noInduk = document.getElementById('f-noinduk').value.trim();
      var nama = document.getElementById('f-nama').value.trim();
      if (!noInduk || !nama) { alert('Mohon isi Nomor Induk dan Nama Santri!'); return; }

      var fNoIndukEl = document.getElementById('f-noinduk');
      var isEdit = (fNoIndukEl.dataset.editMode === 'true');
      var oldNoInduk = fNoIndukEl.dataset.oldNoInduk || '';

      var newSiswa = {
        noInduk: noInduk, nama: nama,
        nisn: document.getElementById('f-nisn').value || '',
        nik: document.getElementById('f-nik').value || '',
        kelas: document.getElementById('f-kelas').value || '',
        tetala: document.getElementById('f-tetala').value || '',
        kelamin: document.getElementById('f-kelamin').value || 'Laki-laki',
        agama: document.getElementById('f-agama').value || 'Islam',
        anak_ke: document.getElementById('f-anakke').value || '',
        alamat_singkat: document.getElementById('f-alamatsingkat').value || '',
        alamat: document.getElementById('f-alamat').value || '',
        sd: document.getElementById('f-sd').value || '',
        mts: document.getElementById('f-mts').value || '',
        alamat_mts: document.getElementById('f-alamatmts').value || '',
        npsn: document.getElementById('f-npsn').value || '',
        kk: document.getElementById('f-kk').value || '',
        notlp: document.getElementById('f-notlp').value || '',
        ayah: document.getElementById('f-ayah').value || '',
        tetala_ayah: document.getElementById('f-tetalaayah').value || '',
        sekolah_ayah: document.getElementById('f-sekolahayah').value || '',
        pekerjaanayah: document.getElementById('f-pekerjaanayah').value || '',
        ibu: document.getElementById('f-ibu').value || '',
        tetala_ibu: document.getElementById('f-tetalaibu').value || '',
        sekolah_ibu: document.getElementById('f-sekolahibu').value || '',
        masuk: document.getElementById('f-masuk').value || '',
        keluar: document.getElementById('f-keluar').value || '',
        mutasi: document.getElementById('f-mutasi').value || '',
        alamat_mutasi: document.getElementById('f-alamatmutasi').value || '',
        status: document.getElementById('f-status').value || 'Aktif',
        prestasi_kab: document.getElementById('f-prestasikab').value || '',
        prestasi_prov: document.getElementById('f-prestasiprov').value || '',
        prestasi_nas: document.getElementById('f-prestasinas').value || '',
        lanjut_studi: document.getElementById('f-lanjutstudi').value || '',
        alamat_lanjut: document.getElementById('f-alamatlanjut').value || '',
        no_ijazah: document.getElementById('f-noijazah').value || '',
        tgl_kelulusan: document.getElementById('f-tglkelulusan').value || '',
        keterangan: document.getElementById('f-keterangan').value || '',
        foto: document.getElementById('f-foto').value || '',
        cap_waktu: new Date().toISOString()
      };

      if (isEdit) {
        var idx = appState.siswa.findIndex(function(s) { return s.noInduk === oldNoInduk; });
        if (idx >= 0) appState.siswa[idx] = newSiswa;
        else appState.siswa.push(newSiswa);
      } else {
        if (appState.siswa.some(function(s) { return s.noInduk === noInduk; })) {
          alert('No Induk ' + noInduk + ' sudah ada!'); return;
        }
        appState.siswa.push(newSiswa);
      }

      closeModal('modal-siswa-form');
      renderAllViews(true);

      // ★ Kalau sedang di halaman detail siswa, refresh isinya
      if (currentDetailSiswaNoInduk === noInduk || currentDetailSiswaNoInduk === oldNoInduk) {
        var updatedSiswa = appState.siswa.find(function(x) { return x.noInduk === noInduk; });
        if (updatedSiswa) {
          currentDetailSiswaNoInduk = noInduk;
          document.getElementById('ds-title').textContent = 'Detail: ' + (updatedSiswa.nama || 'Siswa');
          document.getElementById('ds-subtitle').textContent = 'No Induk: ' + (updatedSiswa.noInduk || '-');
          populateDetailSiswa(updatedSiswa);
        }
      }

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
        var runner = google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
            if (res && res.status === 'success') showBanner('✅ ' + res.message, 'success');
            else { showBanner('⚠️ ' + (res.message || 'Gagal simpan'), 'error'); refreshDataFromSheets(); }
          })
          .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
            showBanner('❌ Gagal simpan: ' + (err.message || err), 'error');
          });
        if (isEdit) runner.updateSiswaDirect(newSiswa, oldNoInduk);
        else runner.tambahSiswaDirect(newSiswa);
      } else {
        showBanner('⚠️ Mode offline — data hanya tersimpan lokal', 'info');
      }

      fNoIndukEl.dataset.editMode = 'false';
      fNoIndukEl.dataset.oldNoInduk = '';
    }

    function editSiswaModal(noInduk) {
      var s = appState.siswa.find(function(x) { return x.noInduk === noInduk; });
      if (!s) return;

      document.getElementById('modal-siswa-title').innerText = 'Edit Data Santri: ' + s.nama;

      document.getElementById('f-noinduk').value        = s.noInduk || '';
      document.getElementById('f-nama').value           = s.nama || '';
      document.getElementById('f-nisn').value           = s.nisn || '';
      document.getElementById('f-nik').value            = s.nik || '';
      document.getElementById('f-kelas').value          = s.kelas || '';

      document.getElementById('f-mts').value            = s.mts || '';
      document.getElementById('f-alamatmts').value      = s.alamat_mts || '';
      document.getElementById('f-npsn').value           = s.npsn || '';
      document.getElementById('f-kk').value             = s.kk || '';
      document.getElementById('f-notlp').value          = s.notlp || '';
      document.getElementById('f-ayah').value           = s.ayah || '';
      document.getElementById('f-tetalaayah').value     = s.tetala_ayah || '';
      document.getElementById('f-sekolahayah').value    = s.sekolah_ayah || '';
      document.getElementById('f-pekerjaanayah').value  = s.pekerjaanayah || '';
      document.getElementById('f-ibu').value            = s.ibu || '';
      document.getElementById('f-tetalaibu').value      = s.tetala_ibu || '';
      document.getElementById('f-sekolahibu').value     = s.sekolah_ibu || '';
      document.getElementById('f-masuk').value          = s.masuk || '';
      document.getElementById('f-keluar').value         = s.keluar || '';
      document.getElementById('f-mutasi').value         = s.mutasi || '';
