      var fNoIndukEl = document.getElementById('f-noinduk');
      fNoIndukEl.dataset.editMode = 'true';
      fNoIndukEl.dataset.oldNoInduk = noInduk;

      switchFormTab('tab-identitas');
      openModal('modal-siswa-form');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function hapusSiswa(noInduk) {
      if (!confirm('Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus data santri ' + noInduk + '?')) return;
      appState.siswa = appState.siswa.filter(function(s) { return s.noInduk !== noInduk; });
      renderAllViews(true);

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
            if (res && res.status === 'success') showBanner('✅ ' + res.message, 'success');
            else { showBanner('⚠️ ' + (res.message || 'Gagal hapus'), 'error'); refreshDataFromSheets(); }
          })
          .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
            showBanner('❌ Gagal hapus: ' + (err.message || err), 'error');
            refreshDataFromSheets();
          })
          .hapusSiswaDirect(noInduk);
      }
    }

    /* ----- Detail & kartu siswa ----- */

        function bukaDetailSiswa(noInduk) {
      var s = appState.siswa.find(function(x) { return x.noInduk === noInduk; });
      if (!s) {
        showBanner('Data siswa tidak ditemukan!', 'error');
        return;
      }

      currentDetailSiswaNoInduk = noInduk;

      // Set judul
      document.getElementById('ds-title').textContent = 'Detail: ' + (s.nama || 'Siswa');
      document.getElementById('ds-subtitle').textContent = 'No Induk: ' + (s.noInduk || '-');

      // Isi semua field
      populateDetailSiswa(s);

      // Buka halaman
      document.getElementById('view-siswa').classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById('view-detail-siswa').classList.remove('hidden');

      // Reset ke tab bio
      switchTabDetailSiswa('bio');
      window.scrollTo(0, 0);
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function kembaliKeSiswa() {
      currentDetailSiswaNoInduk = null;
      document.getElementById('view-detail-siswa').classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById('view-siswa').classList.remove('hidden');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function switchTabDetailSiswa(tabId) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.ds-panel').forEach(function(el) { el.classList.add('hidden'); });

      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-ds-btn').forEach(function(btn) {
        btn.classList.remove('text-emerald-400','border-emerald-400','bg-emerald-500/5');
        btn.classList.add('text-slate-400','border-transparent');
      });

      var panel = document.getElementById('ds-panel-' + tabId);
      if (panel) panel.classList.remove('hidden');

      var btn = document.getElementById('tab-ds-' + tabId);
      if (btn) {
        btn.classList.remove('text-slate-400','border-transparent');
        btn.classList.add('text-emerald-400','border-emerald-400','bg-emerald-500/5');
      }

      // Kalau tab nilai → refresh angka
      if (tabId === 'nilai' && currentDetailSiswaNoInduk) {
        populateNilaiDiDetailSiswa(currentDetailSiswaNoInduk);
      }

      refreshIcons();
    }

    function populateDetailSiswa(s) {
      function set(id, val) {
        var el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (el) el.textContent = (val === null || val === undefined || val === '') ? '-' : val;
      }

      // Foto
      var fotoImg = document.getElementById('ds-foto');
      var fotoPh = document.getElementById('ds-foto-placeholder');
      if (s.foto && String(s.foto).trim() !== '') {
        fotoImg.src = s.foto;
        fotoImg.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (fotoPh) fotoPh.classList.add('hidden');
      } else {
        fotoImg.src = '';
        fotoImg.classList.add('hidden');
        if (fotoPh) fotoPh.classList.remove('hidden');
      }

      // Status badge
      var badge = document.getElementById('ds-status-badge');
      if (badge) {
        var status = s.status || 'Aktif';
        badge.textContent = status;
        badge.className = 'inline-block px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold ' + getStatusBadgeClass(status);
      }

      // Biodata
      set('ds-nama', s.nama);
      set('ds-noinduk', s.noInduk);
      set('ds-nisn', s.nisn);
      set('ds-nik', s.nik);
      set('ds-kk', s.kk);
      set('ds-kelamin', s.kelamin);
      set('ds-tetala', s.tetala);
      set('ds-agama', s.agama);
      set('ds-anakke', s.anak_ke);
      set('ds-kelas', s.kelas);
      set('ds-notlp', s.notlp);
      set('ds-alamatsingkat', s.alamat_singkat);
      set('ds-alamat', s.alamat);

      // Ortu
      set('ds-ayah', s.ayah);
      set('ds-tetalaayah', s.tetala_ayah);
      set('ds-sekolahayah', s.sekolah_ayah);
      set('ds-pekerjaanayah', s.pekerjaanayah);
      set('ds-ibu', s.ibu);
      set('ds-tetalaibu', s.tetala_ibu);
      set('ds-sekolahibu', s.sekolah_ibu);

      // Sekolah
      set('ds-sd', s.sd);
      set('ds-mts', s.mts);
      set('ds-alamatmts', s.alamat_mts);
      set('ds-npsn', s.npsn);

      // Lain
      set('ds-masuk', s.masuk);
      set('ds-keluar', s.keluar);
      set('ds-mutasi', s.mutasi);
      set('ds-alamatmutasi', s.alamat_mutasi);
      set('ds-lanjutstudi', s.lanjut_studi);
      set('ds-alamatlanjut', s.alamat_lanjut);
      set('ds-prestasikab', s.prestasi_kab);
      set('ds-prestasiprov', s.prestasi_prov);
      set('ds-prestasinas', s.prestasi_nas);
      set('ds-noijazah', s.no_ijazah);
      set('ds-tglkelulusan', s.tgl_kelulusan);

      var ketEl = document.getElementById('ds-keterangan');
      if (ketEl) ketEl.textContent = s.keterangan || 'Tidak ada keterangan';
    }

    function populateNilaiDiDetailSiswa(noInduk) {
      var n = appState.nilai.find(function(x) { return x.noInduk === noInduk; });
      if (!n) {
        ['smt1','smt2','smt3','smt4','smt5','smt6','avg','ijazah'].forEach(function(id) {
          var el = document.getElementById('ds-nilai-' + id);
          if (el) el.textContent = '-';
        });
        return;
      }

      ['smt1','smt2','smt3','smt4','smt5','smt6'].forEach(function(id) {
        var el = document.getElementById('ds-nilai-' + id);
        if (el) el.textContent = n[id] && n[id] !== '' ? n[id] : '-';
      });

      var avgEl = document.getElementById('ds-nilai-avg');
      if (avgEl) avgEl.textContent = n.avg && n.avg !== '' ? n.avg : '-';

      var ijazahEl = document.getElementById('ds-nilai-ijazah');
      if (ijazahEl) ijazahEl.textContent = '...';

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function(data) {
            if (ijazahEl) {
              ijazahEl.textContent = (data && data.rataRataIjazah) ? data.rataRataIjazah : '-';
            }
          })
          .withFailureHandler(function() {
            if (ijazahEl) ijazahEl.textContent = '-';
          })
          .getDetailNilaiData(noInduk);
      } else {
        if (ijazahEl) ijazahEl.textContent = '-';
      }
    }

    function editSiswaDariDetail() {
      if (!currentDetailSiswaNoInduk) return;
      editSiswaModal(currentDetailSiswaNoInduk);
    }

    function bukaDetailNilaiDariDetailSiswa() {
      if (!currentDetailSiswaNoInduk) return;
      var noInduk = currentDetailSiswaNoInduk;

      // Reset detail siswa state
      currentDetailSiswaNoInduk = null;

      // Sembunyikan semua view, tampilkan view-nilai
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-view').forEach(function(el) { el.classList.add('hidden'); });
      document.querySelectorAll('.nav-btn').forEach(function(btn) {
        btn.classList.remove('bg-emerald-600','text-white','shadow-md');
        btn.classList.add('text-slate-400');
      });

      var navNilai = document.getElementById('nav-nilai');
      if (navNilai) {
        navNilai.classList.remove('text-slate-400');
        navNilai.classList.add('bg-emerald-600','text-white','shadow-md');
      }

      // Buka halaman detail nilai (yang sudah ada di menu Nilai)
      bukaDetailNilai(noInduk);

      refreshIcons();
    }

    /* ----- Tab form siswa ----- */

    function switchFormTab(tabId) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.form-tab-content').forEach(function(el) { el.classList.add('hidden'); });
      document.querySelectorAll('.form-tab-btn').forEach(function(btn) {
        btn.classList.remove('text-emerald-400','bg-emerald-500/10');
        btn.classList.add('text-slate-400');
      });
      var content = document.getElementById('form-' + tabId);
      var btn = document.getElementById('btn-' + tabId);
      if (content) content.classList.remove('hidden');
      if (btn) {
        btn.classList.remove('text-slate-400');
        btn.classList.add('text-emerald-400','bg-emerald-500/10');
      }
    }


    /* =========================================================
       8. MODUL NILAI (Tabel + Detail + Edit)
       ========================================================= */

    /* ----- Pagination & sorting ----- */

    function resetNilaiPagination() { nilaiPaginationState.currentPage = 1; }

    function changeNilaiPage(direction) {
      var newPage = nilaiPaginationState.currentPage + direction;
      if (newPage < 1 || newPage > nilaiPaginationState.totalPages) return;
      nilaiPaginationState.currentPage = newPage;
      renderNilaiTable();
    }

    function sortNilaiTable(field) {
      if (nilaiPaginationState.sortField === field) {
        nilaiPaginationState.sortOrder = nilaiPaginationState.sortOrder === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc';
      } else {
        nilaiPaginationState.sortField = field;
        nilaiPaginationState.sortOrder = 'asc';
      }
      nilaiPaginationState.currentPage = 1;
      renderNilaiTable();
    }

    /* ----- Filter dropdown ----- */

    function updateFilterNilaiKelas() {
      var select = document.getElementById('filter-nilai-kelas');
      if (!select) return;
      var kelasList = getUniqueKelas();
      var currentValue = select.value;
      select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Semua Kelas</option>';
      kelasList.forEach(function(k) {
        var opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = k; opt.textContent = k;
        select.appendChild(opt);
      });
      if (currentValue && select.querySelector('option[value="' + currentValue + '"]')) select.value = currentValue;
    }

    function updateFilterNilaiAngkatan() {
      var select = document.getElementById('filter-nilai-angkatan');
      if (!select) return;
      var currentValue = select.value;
      var set = new Set();
      (appState.siswa || []).forEach(function(s) {
        var m = String(s.masuk || '').trim();
        var match = m.match(/\d{4}/);
        if (match) set.add(match[0]);
      });
      var arr = Array.from(set);
      arr.sort(function(a, b) { return parseInt(b, 10) - parseInt(a, 10); });

      select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Semua Angkatan</option>';
      arr.forEach(function(a) {
        var opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = a; opt.textContent = a;
        select.appendChild(opt);
      });
      if (currentValue && select.querySelector('option[value="' + currentValue + '"]')) select.value = currentValue;
    }

    /* ----- Tabel nilai ----- */

    function renderNilaiTable() {
      var search = (document.getElementById('search-nilai') && document.getElementById('search-nilai').value || '').toLowerCase();
      var smtFilter = (document.getElementById('filter-nilai-smt') || {}).value || 'all';
      var kelasFilter = (document.getElementById('filter-nilai-kelas') || {}).value || '';
      var angkatanFilter = (document.getElementById('filter-nilai-angkatan') || {}).value || '';
      var pageSize = parseInt((document.getElementById('nilai-page-size') || {}).value) || 25;

      var tbody = document.getElementById('table-nilai-body');
      if (!tbody) return;

      var siswaMap = {};
      (appState.siswa || []).forEach(function(s) { siswaMap[s.noInduk] = s; });

      var dataWithInfo = (appState.nilai || []).map(function(n) {
        var santri = siswaMap[n.noInduk];
        return {
          noInduk: n.noInduk, nama: n.nama,
          smt1: n.smt1, smt2: n.smt2, smt3: n.smt3, smt4: n.smt4, smt5: n.smt5, smt6: n.smt6, avg: n.avg,
          kelas: santri ? (santri.kelas || '-') : '-',
          masuk: santri ? (santri.masuk || '') : '',
          existsInSiswa: !!santri
        };
      });
      dataWithInfo = dataWithInfo.filter(function(n) {
        if (!n.existsInSiswa) return false;
        if ((n.nama || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('angkatan') !== -1) return false;
        if ((n.noInduk || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('angkatan') !== -1) return false;
        return true;
      });

      var filtered = dataWithInfo.filter(function(n) {
        var matchText = (n.nama || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) || (n.noInduk || '').includes(search);

        var matchKelas = true;
        if (kelasFilter) matchKelas = n.kelas === kelasFilter || n.kelas.startsWith(kelasFilter + '-');

        var matchAngkatan = true;
        if (angkatanFilter) {
          var mMatch = String(n.masuk || '').match(/\d{4}/);
          matchAngkatan = mMatch && mMatch[0] === angkatanFilter;
        }

        var matchSmt = true;
        if (smtFilter !== 'all') {
          var smt = parseInt(smtFilter);
          matchSmt = n['smt' + smt] > 0;
        }

        return matchText && matchKelas && matchSmt && matchAngkatan;
      });

      if (nilaiPaginationState.sortField) {
        var field = nilaiPaginationState.sortField;
        var order = nilaiPaginationState.sortOrder;
        filtered.sort(function(a, b) {
          var aVal = a[field] || 0, bVal = b[field] || 0;
          return order === 'asc' ? aVal - bVal : bVal - aVal;
        });
      }

      var state = nilaiPaginationState;
      state.filteredData = filtered;
      state.pageSize = pageSize === 0 ? filtered.length : pageSize;
      state.totalPages = pageSize === 0 ? 1 : Math.ceil(filtered.length / pageSize);
      if (state.currentPage > state.totalPages) state.currentPage = 1;
      if (state.currentPage < 1) state.currentPage = 1;

      var startIdx = (state.currentPage - 1) * state.pageSize;
      var endIdx = state.pageSize === 0 ? filtered.length : Math.min(startIdx + state.pageSize, filtered.length);
      var pageData = filtered.slice(startIdx, endIdx);

      var getColorClass = function(val) {
        if (val >= 85) return 'text-emerald-400';
        if (val >= 70) return 'text-blue-400';
        if (val >= 60) return 'text-amber-400';
        return 'text-red-400';
      };

      if (pageData.length === 0) {
        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="12" class="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">Tidak ada data nilai yang cocok.</td></tr>';
      } else {
        tbody.innerHTML = pageData.map(function(n, idx) {
          var gi = startIdx + idx + 1;
          return '<tr class="hover:bg-slate-800/60 transition">' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center text-slate-400">' + gi + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 font-mono-code font-bold text-white">' + n.noInduk + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-slate-100">' + n.nama + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center">' + (n.kelas && n.kelas !== '-' ?
              '<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 font-medium">' + n.kelas + '</span>' :
              '<span class="text-slate-500">-</span>') + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center font-mono-code font-bold ' + getColorClass(n.smt1) + '">' + (n.smt1 || '-') + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center font-mono-code font-bold ' + getColorClass(n.smt2) + '">' + (n.smt2 || '-') + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center font-mono-code font-bold ' + getColorClass(n.smt3) + '">' + (n.smt3 || '-') + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center font-mono-code font-bold ' + getColorClass(n.smt4) + '">' + (n.smt4 || '-') + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center font-mono-code font-bold ' + getColorClass(n.smt5) + '">' + (n.smt5 || '-') + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center font-mono-code font-bold ' + getColorClass(n.smt6) + '">' + (n.smt6 || '-') + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center font-mono-code font-bold text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-950/20">' + (n.avg || '0.00') + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center"><div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">' +
              '<button onclick="bukaDetailNilai(\'' + n.noInduk + '\')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-blue-400" title="Detail"><i data-lucide="eye" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i></button>' +
              '<button onclick="editNilaiDetailModal(\'' + n.noInduk + '\')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-amber-400" title="Edit"><i data-lucide="edit-3" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i></button>' +
              '<button onclick="hapusNilai(\'' + n.noInduk + '\')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-red-950 text-red-400" title="Hapus"><i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i></button>' +
            '</div></td></tr>';
        }).join('');
      }

      updateNilaiPaginationControls();
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function updateNilaiPaginationControls() {
      var state = nilaiPaginationState;
      var totalPages = state.totalPages, currentPage = state.currentPage;

      var prevBtn = document.getElementById('nilai-prev-btn');
      var nextBtn = document.getElementById('nilai-next-btn');
      var pageDisp = document.getElementById('nilai-current-page-display');
      if (prevBtn) prevBtn.disabled = currentPage <= 1;
      if (nextBtn) nextBtn.disabled = currentPage >= totalPages || totalPages === 0;
      if (pageDisp) pageDisp.textContent = 'Halaman ' + currentPage;

      var prevB = document.getElementById('nilai-prev-btn-bottom');
      var nextB = document.getElementById('nilai-next-btn-bottom');
      var infoB = document.getElementById('nilai-page-info-bottom');
      if (prevB) prevB.disabled = currentPage <= 1;
      if (nextB) nextB.disabled = currentPage >= totalPages || totalPages === 0;
      if (infoB) infoB.textContent = 'Halaman ' + currentPage + ' dari ' + (totalPages || 1);

      var totalData = state.filteredData.length;
      var startIdx = (currentPage - 1) * state.pageSize;
      var endIdx = state.pageSize === 0 ? totalData : Math.min(startIdx + state.pageSize, totalData);

      var startEl = document.getElementById('nilai-start-index');
      var endEl = document.getElementById('nilai-end-index');
      var totalEl = document.getElementById('nilai-total-data');
      var totalBottomEl = document.getElementById('nilai-total-data-bottom');
      if (startEl) startEl.textContent = totalData === 0 ? 0 : startIdx + 1;
      if (endEl) endEl.textContent = totalData === 0 ? 0 : endIdx;
      if (totalEl) totalEl.textContent = totalData;
      if (totalBottomEl) totalBottomEl.textContent = totalData;
    }

    /* ----- Detail nilai per mapel ----- */

    function bukaDetailNilai(noInduk) {
      // ★ 1. Buka halaman DULU
      document.getElementById('view-nilai').classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById('view-detail-nilai').classList.remove('hidden');
      window.scrollTo(0, 0);

      // ★ 2. Tampilkan placeholder loading
      var santri = appState.siswa.find(function(s) { return s.noInduk === noInduk; });
      var nilaiLocal = appState.nilai.find(function(n) { return n.noInduk === noInduk; });
      var namaDisplay = (nilaiLocal && nilaiLocal.nama) || (santri && santri.nama) || 'Santri';

      document.getElementById('detail-nilai-title').textContent = 'Detail Nilai: ' + namaDisplay;
      document.getElementById('detail-nilai-subtitle').textContent = 'Memuat data...';
      document.getElementById('dn-nama').textContent = namaDisplay;
      document.getElementById('dn-noinduk').textContent = noInduk;
      document.getElementById('dn-kelas').textContent = santri ? (santri.kelas || '-') : '-';
      document.getElementById('dn-rata-rata-ijazah').textContent = '...';

      // Reset ke tab Smt 1
      switchTabDetailNilai('smt1');

      // Kosongkan isi tabel & tampilkan "Memuat..."
      var loadingHtml = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="px-3 py-2 text-center text-slate-500 text-[10px] italic">Memuat...</td></tr>';
      var loadingDkn = '<tr><td colspan="3" class="px-3 py-2 text-center text-slate-500 text-[10px] italic">Memuat...</td></tr>';
      ['smt1','smt2','smt3','smt4','smt5','smt6'].forEach(function(id) {
        var bodyEl = document.getElementById('dn-' + id + '-body');
        if (bodyEl) bodyEl.innerHTML = loadingHtml;
        var avgEl = document.getElementById('dn-' + id + '-avg');
        if (avgEl) avgEl.textContent = '...';
      });
      var dknBody = document.getElementById('dn-dkn-body');
      if (dknBody) dknBody.innerHTML = loadingDkn;

      refreshIcons();

      // ★ 3. Fetch data di belakang
      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function(data) {
            if (data) renderDetailNilai(data);
            else {
              if (nilaiLocal) renderDetailNilaiFromLocal(nilaiLocal);
              else showBanner('Data nilai tidak ditemukan!', 'error');
            }
          })
          .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
            if (nilaiLocal) renderDetailNilaiFromLocal(nilaiLocal);
            else showBanner('Gagal mengambil data: ' + err.message, 'error');
          })
          .getDetailNilaiData(noInduk);
      } else {
        if (nilaiLocal) renderDetailNilaiFromLocal(nilaiLocal);
        else showBanner('Data nilai tidak ditemukan!', 'error');
      }
    }

    function kembaliKeNilai() {
      document.getElementById('view-detail-nilai').classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById('view-nilai').classList.remove('hidden');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function switchTabDetailNilai(tabId) {
      // Sembunyikan semua panel
      document.querySelectorAll('.dn-panel').forEach(function(el) { el.classList.add('hidden'); });

      // Reset semua tombol tab
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-dn-btn').forEach(function(btn) {
        btn.classList.remove('text-emerald-400','border-emerald-400','bg-emerald-500/5');
        btn.classList.remove('text-purple-400','border-purple-400','bg-purple-500/5');
        btn.classList.add('text-slate-400','border-transparent');
      });

      // Tampilkan panel terpilih
      var panel = document.getElementById('dn-panel-' + tabId);
      if (panel) panel.classList.remove('hidden');

      // Aktifkan tombol tab
      var btn = document.getElementById('tab-dn-' + tabId);
      if (btn) {
        btn.classList.remove('text-slate-400','border-transparent');
        if (tabId === 'dkn') {
          btn.classList.add('text-purple-400','border-purple-400','bg-purple-500/5');
        } else {
          btn.classList.add('text-emerald-400','border-emerald-400','bg-emerald-500/5');
        }
      }

      refreshIcons();
    }

    function renderDetailNilaiFromLocal(localData) {
      if (!localData) return;
      var converted = {
        noInduk: localData.noInduk, nama: localData.nama,
        dkn: [], rataRataIjazah: 0,
        semesters: [
          { id: 'smt1', label: 'Semester 1', rataRata: localData.smt1 || 0, mapel: [] },
          { id: 'smt2', label: 'Semester 2', rataRata: localData.smt2 || 0, mapel: [] },
          { id: 'smt3', label: 'Semester 3', rataRata: localData.smt3 || 0, mapel: [] },
          { id: 'smt4', label: 'Semester 4', rataRata: localData.smt4 || 0, mapel: [] },
          { id: 'smt5', label: 'Semester 5', rataRata: localData.smt5 || 0, mapel: [] },
          { id: 'smt6', label: 'Semester 6', rataRata: localData.smt6 || 0, mapel: [] }
        ]
      };
      currentDetailNilaiData = converted;

      document.getElementById('dn-nama').textContent = localData.nama || '-';
      document.getElementById('dn-noinduk').textContent = localData.noInduk || '-';
      var santri = appState.siswa.find(function(s) { return s.noInduk === localData.noInduk; });
      document.getElementById('dn-kelas').textContent = santri ? (santri.kelas || '-') : '-';

      ['smt1','smt2','smt3','smt4','smt5','smt6'].forEach(function(id) {
        var avgEl = document.getElementById('dn-' + id + '-avg');
        if (avgEl) avgEl.textContent = localData[id] || 0;
        var bodyEl = document.getElementById('dn-' + id + '-body');
        if (bodyEl) bodyEl.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="px-3 py-2 text-center text-slate-500 text-[10px] italic">Data detail hanya tersedia saat sinkron dengan Google Sheets</td></tr>';
      });

      var dknBody = document.getElementById('dn-dkn-body');
      if (dknBody) dknBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="3" class="px-3 py-2 text-center text-slate-500 text-[10px] italic">Data DKN tidak tersedia</td></tr>';

      document.getElementById('dn-rata-rata-ijazah').textContent = '-';
      document.getElementById('detail-nilai-title').textContent = 'Detail Nilai: ' + (localData.nama || 'Santri');
      document.getElementById('detail-nilai-subtitle').textContent = 'Rata-rata per semester (data lokal)';
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function renderDetailNilai(data) {
      if (!data) return;
      currentDetailNilaiData = data;

      document.getElementById('dn-nama').textContent = data.nama || '-';
      document.getElementById('dn-noinduk').textContent = data.noInduk || '-';
      var santri = appState.siswa.find(function(s) { return s.noInduk === data.noInduk; });
      document.getElementById('dn-kelas').textContent = santri ? (santri.kelas || '-') : '-';

      if (!data.semesters || !Array.isArray(data.semesters)) return;

      function displayNilai(val) {
        if (val === null || val === undefined || val === '') return '-';
        var num = parseFloat(val);
        if (isNaN(num)) return String(val);
        return String(potongDuaDesimalJS(num));
      }

      function getColor(val) {
        if (val === null || val === undefined || val === '') return 'text-slate-500';
        var num = parseFloat(val);
        if (isNaN(num)) return 'text-slate-300';
        if (num >= 85) return 'text-emerald-400';
        if (num >= 70) return 'text-blue-400';
        if (num >= 60) return 'text-amber-400';
        return 'text-red-400';
      }

      data.semesters.forEach(function(sem) {
        if (!sem || !sem.id) return;

        var avgEl = document.getElementById('dn-' + sem.id + '-avg');
        if (avgEl) avgEl.textContent = displayNilai(sem.rataRata);

        var bodyEl = document.getElementById('dn-' + sem.id + '-body');
        if (!bodyEl) return;

        if (!sem.mapel || sem.mapel.length === 0) {
          bodyEl.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="px-3 py-2 text-center text-slate-500 text-[10px] italic">Tidak ada data mapel</td></tr>';
          return;
        }

        bodyEl.innerHTML = sem.mapel.map(function(item, index) {
          return '<tr class="hover:bg-slate-800/40">' +
            '<td class="px-3 py-1.5 text-center text-slate-400">' + (index + 1) + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-3 py-1.5 font-medium text-slate-200">' + (item.mapel || ('Mapel ' + (index + 1))) + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-3 py-1.5 text-center font-mono-code font-bold ' + getColor(item.teori) + '">' + displayNilai(item.teori) + '</td>' +
            '<td class="px-3 py-1.5 text-center font-mono-code font-bold ' + getColor(item.praktek) + '">' + displayNilai(item.praktek) + '</td>' +
          '</tr>';
        }).join('');
      });

      var dknBody = document.getElementById('dn-dkn-body');
      if (dknBody) {
        if (data.dkn && Array.isArray(data.dkn) && data.dkn.length > 0) {
          dknBody.innerHTML = data.dkn.map(function(item, index) {
            return '<tr class="hover:bg-slate-800/40">' +
              '<td class="px-3 py-1.5 text-center text-slate-400">' + (index + 1) + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-3 py-1.5 font-medium text-slate-200">' + item.nama + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-3 py-1.5 text-center font-mono-code font-bold ' + getColor(item.nilai) + '">' + displayNilai(item.nilai) + '</td>' +
            '</tr>';
          }).join('');
        } else {
          dknBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="3" class="px-3 py-2 text-center text-slate-500 text-[10px] italic">Tidak ada data DKN</td></tr>';
        }
      }

      var rrEl = document.getElementById('dn-rata-rata-ijazah');
      if (rrEl) rrEl.textContent = displayNilai(data.rataRataIjazah);

      document.getElementById('detail-nilai-title').textContent = 'Detail Nilai: ' + (data.nama || 'Santri');
      document.getElementById('detail-nilai-subtitle').textContent = 'Semua mata pelajaran per semester';
      refreshIcons();
    }

    /* ----- Edit nilai detail ----- */

    function editNilaiDetailModal(noInduk) {
      if (!noInduk) { showBanner('No Induk tidak valid!', 'error'); return; }

      showBanner('Mengambil data nilai dari Google Sheets...', 'info');

      if (typeof google === 'undefined' || !google.script || !google.script.run) {
        showBanner('❌ Mode offline: tidak bisa edit', 'error');
        return;
      }

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(data) {
          if (!data) {
            showBanner('Data nilai tidak ditemukan!', 'error');
            return;
          }
          bukaModalEditNilai(data);
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          showBanner('Gagal mengambil data: ' + (err.message || err), 'error');
        })
        .getDetailNilaiData(noInduk);
    }

    function bukaModalEditNilai(data) {
      editNilaiState.noInduk = data.noInduk;
      editNilaiState.activeTab = 'smt1';

      (data.semesters || []).forEach(function(sem) {
        if (!sem || !sem.id) return;
        editNilaiState.semesters[sem.id] = (sem.mapel || []).map(function(m) {
          return {
            mapel: m.mapel || '',
            teori: (m.teori === null || m.teori === undefined) ? '' : String(m.teori),
            praktek: (m.praktek === null || m.praktek === undefined) ? '' : String(m.praktek)
          };
        });
      });

      editNilaiState.dkn = (data.dkn || []).map(function(d) {
        return {
          nama: d.nama || '',
          nilai: (d.nilai === null || d.nilai === undefined) ? '' : String(d.nilai)
        };
      });

      document.getElementById('en-nama').textContent = data.nama || '-';
      document.getElementById('en-noinduk').textContent = data.noInduk || '-';
      var santri = appState.siswa.find(function(s) { return s.noInduk === data.noInduk; });
      document.getElementById('en-kelas').textContent = santri ? (santri.kelas || '-') : '-';

      document.getElementById('editNilaiTitle').textContent = 'Edit Nilai: ' + (data.nama || 'Santri');
      document.getElementById('editNilaiSubtitle').textContent = 'Klik tab semester untuk edit, atau tab DKN';

      switchEditNilaiTab('smt1');
      openModal('modalEditNilaiDetail');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function switchEditNilaiTab(tabId) {
      editNilaiState.activeTab = tabId;

      document.querySelectorAll('.edit-nilai-tab').forEach(function(btn) {
        btn.classList.remove('text-emerald-400','bg-emerald-500/10','text-purple-400','bg-purple-500/10');
        btn.classList.add('text-slate-400');
      });
      var activeBtn = document.getElementById('btn-edit-' + tabId);
      if (activeBtn) {
        activeBtn.classList.remove('text-slate-400');
        if (tabId === 'dkn') activeBtn.classList.add('text-purple-400','bg-purple-500/10');
        else activeBtn.classList.add('text-emerald-400','bg-emerald-500/10');
      }

      renderEditNilaiContent();
    }

    function renderEditNilaiContent() {
      var container = document.getElementById('edit-nilai-content');
      if (!container) return;

      var tab = editNilaiState.activeTab;

      if (tab === 'dkn') {
        renderEditDknTab(container);
      } else {
        renderEditSemesterTab(container, tab);
      }
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function renderEditSemesterTab(container, smtId) {
      var mapelList = editNilaiState.semesters[smtId] || [];
      var labelSmt = {
        smt1: 'SEMESTER 1 (Kelas X - Ganjil)',
        smt2: 'SEMESTER 2 (Kelas X - Genap)',
        smt3: 'SEMESTER 3 (Kelas XI - Ganjil)',
        smt4: 'SEMESTER 4 (Kelas XI - Genap)',
        smt5: 'SEMESTER 5 (Kelas XII - Ganjil)',
        smt6: 'SEMESTER 6 (Kelas XII - Genap)'
      }[smtId] || smtId;

      var html = '<div class="border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden">' +
        '<div class="bg-slate-950/80 px-4 py-2 border-b border-slate-800">' +
          '<h4 class="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-400">' + labelSmt + '</h4>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div class="overflow-x-auto max-h-[500px]">' +
          '<table class="w-full text-left text-xs text-slate-300">' +
            '<thead class="bg-slate-900 text-slate-400 font-semibold sticky top-0 z-10">' +
              '<tr>' +
                '<th class="px-3 py-2 w-10 text-center">No</th>' +
                '<th class="px-3 py-2">Mata Pelajaran</th>' +
                '<th class="px-3 py-2 text-center w-32">Nilai Teori</th>' +
                '<th class="px-3 py-2 text-center w-32">Nilai Praktek</th>' +
              '</tr>' +
            '</thead>' +
            '<tbody class="divide-y divide-slate-800">';

      mapelList.forEach(function(item, idx) {
        html += '<tr class="hover:bg-slate-800/40">' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-1.5 text-center text-slate-400">' + (idx + 1) + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-1.5 font-medium text-slate-200">' + (item.mapel || ('Mapel ' + (idx + 1))) + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-1.5 text-center">' +
            '<input type="text" class="edit-teori w-full px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded text-xs text-white text-center focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500" data-smt="' + smtId + '" data-idx="' + idx + '" value="' + (item.teori || '') + '" oninput="onEditNilaiChange(\'' + smtId + '\',' + idx + ',\'teori\',this.value)">' +
          '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-1.5 text-center">' +
            '<input type="text" class="edit-praktek w-full px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded text-xs text-white text-center focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500" data-smt="' + smtId + '" data-idx="' + idx + '" value="' + (item.praktek || '') + '" oninput="onEditNilaiChange(\'' + smtId + '\',' + idx + ',\'praktek\',this.value)">' +
          '</td>' +
        '</tr>';
      });

      html += '</tbody><tfoot class="bg-slate-950/80 text-xs font-bold sticky bottom-0">' +
        '<tr><td colspan="2" class="px-3 py-2 text-right text-slate-300">JUMLAH TEORI:</td>' +
          '<td colspan="2" class="px-3 py-2 text-center text-emerald-400" id="edit-' + smtId + '-jumlah">-</td>' +
        '</tr>' +
        '<tr><td colspan="2" class="px-3 py-2 text-right text-slate-300">RATA-RATA TEORI:</td>' +
          '<td colspan="2" class="px-3 py-2 text-center text-emerald-400" id="edit-' + smtId + '-rata">-</td>' +
        '</tr>' +
      '</tfoot></table></div></div>';

      container.innerHTML = html;
      hitungSemester(smtId);
    }

    function renderEditDknTab(container) {
      var dknList = editNilaiState.dkn || [];

      var html = '<div class="border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden">' +
        '<div class="bg-slate-950/80 px-4 py-2 border-b border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between">' +
          '<h4 class="text-xs font-bold text-purple-400">NILAI IJAZAH (DKN)</h4>' +
          '<span class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 italic">Otomatis dari average 6 semester</span>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div class="overflow-x-auto max-h-[500px]">' +
          '<table class="w-full text-left text-xs text-slate-300">' +
            '<thead class="bg-slate-900 text-slate-400 font-semibold sticky top-0 z-10">' +
              '<tr>' +
                '<th class="px-3 py-2 w-10 text-center">No</th>' +
                '<th class="px-3 py-2">Mata Pelajaran</th>' +
                '<th class="px-3 py-2 text-center w-32">Nilai DKN</th>' +
              '</tr>' +
            '</thead>' +
            '<tbody class="divide-y divide-slate-800">';

      dknList.forEach(function(item, idx) {
        html += '<tr class="hover:bg-slate-800/40">' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-1.5 text-center text-slate-400">' + (idx + 1) + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-1.5 font-medium text-slate-200">' + (item.nama || ('Mapel ' + (idx + 1))) + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-1.5 text-center font-mono-code font-bold text-purple-300">' + (item.nilai || '-') + '</td>' +
        '</tr>';
      });

      var total = 0, count = 0;
      dknList.forEach(function(item) {
        var num = parseFloat(item.nilai);
        if (!isNaN(num)) { total += num; count++; }
      });
      var avg = count > 0 ? total / count : 0;

      html += '</tbody><tfoot class="bg-slate-950/80 text-xs font-bold sticky bottom-0">' +
        '<tr><td colspan="2" class="px-3 py-2 text-right text-slate-300">RATA-RATA IJAZAH:</td>' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-2 text-center text-amber-400" id="edit-rata-ijazah">' + (count > 0 ? potongDuaDesimalJS(avg) : '-') + '</td>' +
        '</tr>' +
      '</tfoot></table></div></div>';

      container.innerHTML = html;
    }

    function onEditNilaiChange(smtId, idx, field, value) {
      if (!editNilaiState.semesters[smtId]) return;
      if (!editNilaiState.semesters[smtId][idx]) return;

      editNilaiState.semesters[smtId][idx][field] = value;
      hitungSemester(smtId);
      hitungUlangDkn();
    }

    function hitungSemester(smtId) {
      var mapelList = editNilaiState.semesters[smtId] || [];
      var total = 0, count = 0;

      mapelList.forEach(function(item) {
        var num = parseFloat(item.teori);
        if (!isNaN(num)) { total += num; count++; }
      });

      var jumlahEl = document.getElementById('edit-' + smtId + '-jumlah');
      var rataEl = document.getElementById('edit-' + smtId + '-rata');

      if (jumlahEl) jumlahEl.textContent = count > 0 ? String(Math.round(total)) : '-';
      if (rataEl) rataEl.textContent = count > 0 ? potongDuaDesimalJS(total / count) : '-';
    }

    function hitungUlangDkn() {
      var jumlahMapel = (editNilaiState.semesters.smt1 || []).length;

      for (var m = 0; m < jumlahMapel; m++) {
        var nilaiArr = [];

        ['smt1','smt2','smt3','smt4','smt5','smt6'].forEach(function(smtId) {
          var item = (editNilaiState.semesters[smtId] || [])[m];
          if (!item) return;
          var tv = parseFloat(item.teori);
          var pv = parseFloat(item.praktek);
          if (!isNaN(tv)) nilaiArr.push(tv);
          if (!isNaN(pv)) nilaiArr.push(pv);
        });

        var avg = 0;
        if (nilaiArr.length > 0) {
          var total = 0;
          for (var k = 0; k < nilaiArr.length; k++) total += nilaiArr[k];
          avg = total / nilaiArr.length;
        }

        if (editNilaiState.dkn[m]) {
          editNilaiState.dkn[m].nilai = nilaiArr.length > 0 ? String(potongDuaDesimalJS(avg)) : '';
        }
      }

      if (editNilaiState.activeTab === 'dkn') {
        var container = document.getElementById('edit-nilai-content');
        if (container) renderEditDknTab(container);
      }
    }

    function simpanEditNilaiDetail() {
      if (!editNilaiState.noInduk) return;

      var payload = {
        noInduk: editNilaiState.noInduk,
        semesters: {},
        dkn: []
      };

      ['smt1','smt2','smt3','smt4','smt5','smt6'].forEach(function(smtId) {
        var mapelList = editNilaiState.semesters[smtId] || [];
        var totalTeori = 0;
        var countTeori = 0;

        payload.semesters[smtId] = mapelList.map(function(item) {
          var tv = parseFloat(item.teori);
          if (!isNaN(tv)) { totalTeori += tv; countTeori++; }
          return {
            teori:   item.teori === '' ? null : item.teori,
            praktek: item.praktek === '' ? null : item.praktek
          };
        });

        payload.semesters[smtId]._jumlah = countTeori > 0 ? Math.round(totalTeori) : null;
        payload.semesters[smtId]._rataRata = countTeori > 0 ? potongDuaDesimalJS(totalTeori / countTeori) : null;
      });

      payload.dkn = (editNilaiState.dkn || []).map(function(item) {
        return item.nilai === '' ? null : item.nilai;
      });

      var totalDkn = 0, countDkn = 0;
      (editNilaiState.dkn || []).forEach(function(item) {
        var num = parseFloat(item.nilai);
        if (!isNaN(num)) { totalDkn += num; countDkn++; }
      });
      payload.rataRataIjazah = countDkn > 0 ? potongDuaDesimalJS(totalDkn / countDkn) : '';

      if (typeof google === 'undefined' || !google.script || !google.script.run) {
        showBanner('❌ Mode offline: tidak bisa simpan', 'error');
        return;
      }

      showBanner('⏳ Menyimpan nilai...', 'info');

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            showBanner('✅ ' + res.message, 'success');
            closeModal('modalEditNilaiDetail');
            refreshDataFromSheets();
          } else {
            showBanner('⚠️ ' + (res.message || 'Gagal simpan'), 'error');
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          showBanner('❌ Gagal simpan: ' + (err.message || err), 'error');
        })
        .saveDetailNilaiDirect(payload);
    }

    function hapusNilai(noInduk) {
      if (!confirm('Yakin ingin menghapus data nilai untuk No Induk ' + noInduk + '?')) return;
      var nama = (appState.nilai.find(function(n) { return n.noInduk === noInduk; }) || {}).nama || noInduk;
      appState.nilai = appState.nilai.filter(function(n) { return n.noInduk !== noInduk; });
      renderAllViews();
      showBanner('Data nilai ' + nama + ' berhasil dihapus.', 'info');
    }


    /* =========================================================
       9. MODUL GURU
       ========================================================= */

    function renderGuruTable() {
      var tbody = document.getElementById('table-guru-body');
      if (!tbody) return;

      var search = (document.getElementById('search-guru') && document.getElementById('search-guru').value || '').toLowerCase();
      var filtered = (appState.guru || []).filter(function(g) {
        if (!search) return true;
        return (g.nama || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
              (g.mapel1 || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
              (g.mapel2 || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
              (g.alamat || '').toLowerCase().includes(search);
      });

      if (filtered.length === 0) {
        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" class="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">Tidak ada data guru yang cocok.</td></tr>';
        refreshIcons();
        return;
      }

      tbody.innerHTML = filtered.map(function(g, idx) {
        var kgEsc = String(g.kg || '').replace(/\\/g, '\\\\').replace(/'/g, "\\'");
        return '<tr class="hover:bg-slate-800/60 transition">' +
          '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center text-slate-400">' + (idx + 1) + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-4 py-3 font-bold text-white">' + (g.nama || '-') + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-slate-300">' + (g.alamat || '-') + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-emerald-400">' + (g.mapel1 || '-') + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-teal-400">' + (g.mapel2 || '-') + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-slate-300">' + (g.kelas_smt1 || '-') + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-slate-300">' + (g.kelas_smt2 || '-') + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center"><div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">' +
            '<button onclick="bukaDetailGuru(\'' + kgEsc + '\')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-blue-400" title="Detail"><i data-lucide="eye" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i></button>' +
            '<button onclick="editGuruModal(\'' + kgEsc + '\')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-amber-400" title="Edit"><i data-lucide="edit-3" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i></button>' +
            '<button onclick="hapusGuru(\'' + kgEsc + '\')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-red-950 text-red-400" title="Hapus"><i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i></button>' +
          '</div></td></tr>';
      }).join('');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function bukaDetailGuru(kg) {
      var guru = appState.guru.find(function(g) { return g.kg === kg; });
      if (!guru) {
        showBanner('Data guru tidak ditemukan!', 'error');
        return;
      }

      var c = document.getElementById('detailGuruContent');
      c.innerHTML =
        '<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">' +
          '<div class="bg-slate-950/60 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-700"><span class="text-slate-400 block text-[10px]">Kode Guru</span><span class="text-white font-bold text-sm">' + (guru.kg || '-') + '</span></div>' +
          '<div class="bg-slate-950/60 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-700"><span class="text-slate-400 block text-[10px]">Nama Lengkap</span><span class="text-white font-bold text-sm">' + (guru.nama || '-') + '</span></div>' +
          '<div class="col-span-2 bg-slate-950/60 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-700"><span class="text-slate-400 block text-[10px]">Alamat</span><span class="text-white">' + (guru.alamat || '-') + '</span></div>' +
          '<div class="bg-slate-950/60 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-700"><span class="text-slate-400 block text-[10px]">Mapel Smt I</span><span class="text-emerald-400 font-semibold">' + (guru.mapel1 || '-') + '</span></div>' +
          '<div class="bg-slate-950/60 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-700"><span class="text-slate-400 block text-[10px]">Mapel Smt II</span><span class="text-teal-400 font-semibold">' + (guru.mapel2 || '-') + '</span></div>' +
          '<div class="bg-slate-950/60 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-700"><span class="text-slate-400 block text-[10px]">Kelas Smt I</span><span class="text-white font-semibold">' + (guru.kelas_smt1 || '-') + '</span></div>' +
          '<div class="bg-slate-950/60 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-700"><span class="text-slate-400 block text-[10px]">Kelas Smt II</span><span class="text-white font-semibold">' + (guru.kelas_smt2 || '-') + '</span></div>' +
        '</div>';
      document.getElementById('detailGuruTitle').textContent = 'Detail Guru: ' + guru.nama;
      openModal('modalDetailGuru');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function editGuruModal(kg) {
      var guru = appState.guru.find(function(g) { return g.kg === kg; });
      if (!guru) {
        showBanner('Data guru tidak ditemukan!', 'error');
        return;
      }

      document.getElementById('eg-index-edit').value = kg;
      document.getElementById('eg-kg').value          = guru.kg || '';
      document.getElementById('eg-nama').value        = guru.nama || '';
      document.getElementById('eg-alamat').value      = guru.alamat || '';
      document.getElementById('eg-mapel1').value      = guru.mapel1 || '';
      document.getElementById('eg-mapel2').value      = guru.mapel2 || '';
      document.getElementById('eg-kelas-smt1').value  = guru.kelas_smt1 || '';
      document.getElementById('eg-kelas-smt2').value  = guru.kelas_smt2 || '';

      document.getElementById('editGuruTitle').textContent = 'Edit Guru: ' + guru.nama;
      openModal('modalEditGuru');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function simpanEditGuru() {
      var kgLama = document.getElementById('eg-index-edit').value;
      var kg     = document.getElementById('eg-kg').value;
      var nama   = document.getElementById('eg-nama').value;
      if (!nama) { showBanner('Nama harus diisi!', 'error'); return; }

      var idx = appState.guru.findIndex(function(g) { return g.kg === kgLama; });
      if (idx === -1) { showBanner('Data guru tidak ditemukan!', 'error'); return; }

      appState.guru[idx] = {
        kg: kg || '',
        nama: nama,
        alamat: document.getElementById('eg-alamat').value || '',
        mapel1: document.getElementById('eg-mapel1').value || '',
        mapel2: document.getElementById('eg-mapel2').value || '',
        kelas_smt1: document.getElementById('eg-kelas-smt1').value || '',
        kelas_smt2: document.getElementById('eg-kelas-smt2').value || ''
      };

      closeModal('modalEditGuru');
      renderGuruTable();
      showBanner('✅ Data guru ' + nama + ' berhasil diperbarui!', 'success');

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function(res) { console.log('Tersimpan:', res); })
          .withFailureHandler(function(err) { console.error('Gagal:', err); })
          .saveGuruBatchDirect(appState.guru);
      }
    }

    function hapusGuru(kg) {
      var guru = appState.guru.find(function(g) { return g.kg === kg; });
      if (!guru) return;
      if (!confirm('Yakin ingin menghapus guru ' + guru.nama + '?')) return;

      appState.guru = appState.guru.filter(function(g) { return g.kg !== kg; });
      renderGuruTable();
      showBanner('Guru ' + guru.nama + ' berhasil dihapus.', 'info');

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function(res) { console.log('Tersimpan:', res); })
          .withFailureHandler(function(err) { console.error('Gagal:', err); })
          .saveGuruBatchDirect(appState.guru);
      }
    }

    function openModalTambahGuru() { openModal('modal-guru-form'); }

    function simpanDataGuruDariForm() {
      var kg = document.getElementById('fg-kg').value;
      var nama = document.getElementById('fg-nama').value;
      if (!nama) { showBanner('Nama harus diisi!', 'error'); return; }

      var g = {
        kg: kg || '', nama: nama,
        alamat: document.getElementById('fg-alamat').value || '',
        mapel1: document.getElementById('fg-mapel1').value || '',
        mapel2: document.getElementById('fg-mapel2').value || '',
        kelas_smt1: document.getElementById('fg-kelas-smt1').value || '',
        kelas_smt2: document.getElementById('fg-kelas-smt2').value || ''
      };

      appState.guru.push(g);
      closeModal('modal-guru-form');
      renderGuruTable();
      showBanner('Dewan guru ' + g.nama + ' berhasil ditambahkan!', 'success');

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function(res) { console.log('Tersimpan:', res); })
          .withFailureHandler(function(err) { console.error('Gagal:', err); })
          .saveGuruBatchDirect(appState.guru);
      }
    }

    function exportGuruToExcel() {
      showBanner('⏳ Export Excel...', 'info');
      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function(result) {
            if (result.success && result.excel) {
              showBanner('✅ ' + result.total + ' data berhasil diexport!', 'success');
              var binary = atob(result.excel);
              var bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
              for (var i = 0; i < binary.length; i++) bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
              var blob = new Blob([bytes], { type: 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet' });
              var link = document.createElement('a');
              link.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
              link.download = result.filename || 'Data_Guru.xlsx';
              document.body.appendChild(link);
              link.click();
              document.body.removeChild(link);
              setTimeout(function() { URL.revokeObjectURL(link.href); }, 100);
            } else {
              showBanner('❌ Gagal: ' + (result.message || 'Unknown error'), 'error');
            }
          })
          .withFailureHandler(function(err) { showBanner('❌ Error: ' + err.message, 'error'); })
          .exportGuruToExcel();
      }
    }


    /* =========================================================
       10. MODUL ABSENSI (Kelas / Nabil / Sorogan)

    /* ----- Populate dropdowns ----- */

    function populateAbsenKelas() {
      var select = document.getElementById('filterAbsenKelas');
      if (!select) return;
      var kelasList = getUniqueKelas();
      select.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Kelas --</option>';
      kelasList.forEach(function(k) {
        var opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = k; opt.textContent = k;
        select.appendChild(opt);
      });
    }

    function populateFilterKelasManual() {
      var select = document.getElementById('filter-absen-kelas');
      if (!select) return;
      var set = new Set();
      (appState.siswa || []).forEach(function(s) {
        if (s.kelas && s.kelas.trim() !== '') set.add(s.kelas.trim());
      });
      var arr = Array.from(set);
      var urutan = { 'X': 1, 'XI': 2, 'XII': 3 };
      arr.sort(function(a, b) {
        var ap = (a.match(/^(X|XI|XII)/) || [''])[0];
        var bp = (b.match(/^(X|XI|XII)/) || [''])[0];
        if (urutan[ap] && urutan[bp]) {
          if (urutan[ap] !== urutan[bp]) return urutan[ap] - urutan[bp];
          return a.localeCompare(b);
        }
        return a.localeCompare(b);
      });

      var html = '<option value="">Semua Kelas</option><option value="TANPA">Tanpa Kelas</option>';
      var htmlCetak = '<option value="">-- Pilih Kelas --</option>';
      arr.forEach(function(k) {
        html += '<option value="' + k + '">' + k + '</option>';
        htmlCetak += '<option value="' + k + '">' + k + '</option>';
      });

      select.innerHTML = html;
      var cetakSelect = document.getElementById('filterAbsenKelasCetak');
      if (cetakSelect) { cetakSelect.innerHTML = htmlCetak; cetakSelect.value = select.value; }
    }

    function populateFilterNabil() {
      var select = document.getElementById('filter-absen-nabil');
      if (!select) return;
      var set = new Set();
      (appState.nabil || []).forEach(function(n) {
        var k = (n.kelasNabil || '').trim();
        if (k) set.add(k);
      });
      var html = '<option value="">Semua Kelas Nabil</option>';
      var htmlCetak = '<option value="">-- Pilih Kelas Nabil --</option>';
      Array.from(set).sort().forEach(function(k) {
        html += '<option value="' + k + '">' + k + '</option>';
        htmlCetak += '<option value="' + k + '">' + k + '</option>';
      });

      select.innerHTML = html;
      var cetak = document.getElementById('filterAbsenNabilCetak');
      if (cetak) { cetak.innerHTML = htmlCetak; cetak.value = select.value; }
    }

    function populateFilterSorogan() {
      var select = document.getElementById('filter-absen-sorogan');
      if (!select) return;
      var set = new Set();
      (appState.sorogan || []).forEach(function(s) {
        var p = (s.pembimbing || '').trim();
        if (p) set.add(p);
      });
      var html = '<option value="">Semua Pembimbing</option>';
      var htmlCetak = '<option value="">-- Pilih Pembimbing --</option>';
      Array.from(set).sort().forEach(function(k) {
        html += '<option value="' + k + '">' + k + '</option>';
        htmlCetak += '<option value="' + k + '">' + k + '</option>';
      });
      select.innerHTML = html;
      var cetak = document.getElementById('filterAbsenSoroganCetak');
      if (cetak) { cetak.innerHTML = htmlCetak; cetak.value = select.value; }
    }

    function populateCetakFilterNabil() {
      var select = document.getElementById('filterAbsenNabil');
      if (!select) return;
      var set = new Set();
      (appState.nabil || []).forEach(function(n) {
        var k = (n.kelasNabil || '').trim();
        if (k) set.add(k);
      });
      select.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Kelas Nabil --</option>';
      Array.from(set).sort().forEach(function(k) {
        var opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = k; opt.textContent = k;
        select.appendChild(opt);
      });
    }

    function populateCetakFilterSorogan() {
      var select = document.getElementById('filterAbsenSorogan');
      if (!select) return;
      var set = new Set();
      (appState.sorogan || []).forEach(function(s) {
        var p = (s.pembimbing || '').trim();
        if (p) set.add(p);
      });
      select.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Pembimbing --</option>';
      Array.from(set).sort().forEach(function(k) {
        var opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = k; opt.textContent = k;
        select.appendChild(opt);
      });
    }

    /* ----- Tabel data siswa (absen) ----- */

    function renderAbsenSiswaTable() {
      var tbody = document.getElementById('table-absen-siswa-body');
      var thead = document.getElementById('absen-table-header');
      if (!tbody || !thead) return;

      var search = (document.getElementById('search-absen-siswa') && document.getElementById('search-absen-siswa').value || '').toLowerCase();

      if (currentDataTab === 'kelas') {
        thead.innerHTML = '<th class="px-4 py-3.5 w-12 text-center">No</th><th class="px-4 py-3.5">No Induk</th><th class="px-4 py-3.5">Nama Lengkap</th><th class="px-4 py-3.5">Kelas</th><th class="px-4 py-3.5">Alamat</th><th class="px-4 py-3.5 text-center">Status</th><th class="px-4 py-3.5 text-center w-32">Aksi</th>';

        var kelasFilter = (document.getElementById('filter-absen-kelas') || {}).value || '';
        var statusFilter = (document.getElementById('filter-absen-status') || {}).value || '';

        var filtered = (appState.siswa || []).filter(function(s) {
          var kelas = (s.kelas || '').trim();
          var status = (s.status || '').trim();
          if (!kelas) return false;
          if (kelas.toLowerCase().includes('lulus')) return false;
          if (status.toLowerCase() === 'lulus') return false;
          if (!/X|XI|XII|Berhenti/i.test(kelas)) return false;

          var matchText = !search || (s.nama || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) || (s.noInduk || '').includes(search);

          var matchKelas = true;
          if (kelasFilter === 'TANPA') matchKelas = false;
          else if (kelasFilter) matchKelas = kelas === kelasFilter || kelas.replace(/^Berhenti\s*/i, '').trim() === kelasFilter || kelas.includes(kelasFilter);

          var matchStatus = true;
          if (statusFilter) {
            var st = s.status || 'Aktif';
            if (kelas.toLowerCase().includes('berhenti')) st = 'Berhenti';
            matchStatus = st === statusFilter;
          }

          return matchText && matchKelas && matchStatus;
        });

        if (filtered.length === 0) {
          tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" class="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">Tidak ada data.</td></tr>';
        } else {
          tbody.innerHTML = filtered.map(function(s, idx) {
            var isBerhenti = s.kelas && s.kelas.toLowerCase().includes('berhenti');
            var statusDisplay = s.status || 'Aktif';
            if (isBerhenti && statusDisplay === 'Aktif') statusDisplay = 'Berhenti';
            return '<tr class="hover:bg-slate-800/60 transition ' + (isBerhenti ? 'bg-red-950/20' : '') + '">' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center text-slate-400">' + (idx + 1) + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3 font-mono-code font-bold text-white">' + (s.noInduk || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-slate-100">' + (s.nama || '-') + (isBerhenti ? ' <span class="ml-2 text-[10px] text-red-400 font-bold">(BERHENTI)</span>' : '') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3">' + (s.kelas || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-slate-400 max-w-[180px] truncate">' + (s.alamat || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center"><span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold ' + getStatusBadgeClass(statusDisplay) + '">' + statusDisplay + '</span></td>' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center"><div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">' +
                '<button onclick="bukaDetailSiswa(\'' + s.noInduk + '\')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-blue-400" title="Detail"><i data-lucide="eye" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i></button>' +
                '<button onclick="editSiswaModal(\'' + s.noInduk + '\')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-amber-400" title="Edit"><i data-lucide="edit-3" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i></button>' +
              '</div></td></tr>';
          }).join('');
        }
      } else if (currentDataTab === 'nabil') {
        thead.innerHTML = '<th class="px-4 py-3.5 w-12 text-center">No</th><th class="px-4 py-3.5">No Induk</th><th class="px-4 py-3.5">Nama</th><th class="px-4 py-3.5">Alamat</th><th class="px-4 py-3.5">Kelas</th><th class="px-4 py-3.5">Kel. Nabil</th><th class="px-4 py-3.5 text-center w-32">Aksi</th>';

        var nabilFilter = (document.getElementById('filter-absen-nabil') || {}).value || '';
        var filteredNabil = (appState.nabil || []).filter(function(n) {
          var mt = !search || (n.nama || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) || (n.noInduk || '').includes(search);
          var mk = true;
          if (nabilFilter) mk = n.kelasNabil === nabilFilter;
          return mt && mk;
        });

        if (filteredNabil.length === 0) {
          tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" class="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">Tidak ada data nabil.</td></tr>';
        } else {
          tbody.innerHTML = filteredNabil.map(function(n, idx) {
            return '<tr class="hover:bg-slate-800/60 transition">' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center text-slate-400">' + (idx + 1) + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3 font-mono-code font-bold text-white">' + (n.noInduk || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-slate-100">' + (n.nama || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-slate-400">' + (n.alamat || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3">' + (n.kelas || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-purple-400 font-semibold">' + (n.kelasNabil || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center"><button onclick="bukaDetailSiswa(\'' + n.noInduk + '\')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-blue-400"><i data-lucide="eye" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i></button></td>' +
            '</tr>';
          }).join('');
        }
      } else if (currentDataTab === 'sorogan') {
        thead.innerHTML = '<th class="px-4 py-3.5 w-12 text-center">No</th><th class="px-4 py-3.5">No Induk</th><th class="px-4 py-3.5">Nama</th><th class="px-4 py-3.5">Alamat</th><th class="px-4 py-3.5">Kelas</th><th class="px-4 py-3.5">Pembimbing</th><th class="px-4 py-3.5 text-center w-32">Aksi</th>';

        var soroganFilter = (document.getElementById('filter-absen-sorogan') || {}).value || '';
        var filteredSorogan = (appState.sorogan || []).filter(function(so) {
          var mt = !search || (so.nama || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) || (so.noInduk || '').includes(search);
          var mp = true;
          if (soroganFilter) mp = so.pembimbing === soroganFilter;
          return mt && mp;
        });

        if (filteredSorogan.length === 0) {
          tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" class="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">Tidak ada data sorogan.</td></tr>';
        } else {
          tbody.innerHTML = filteredSorogan.map(function(so, idx) {
            return '<tr class="hover:bg-slate-800/60 transition">' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center text-slate-400">' + (idx + 1) + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3 font-mono-code font-bold text-white">' + (so.noInduk || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-slate-100">' + (so.nama || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-slate-400">' + (so.alamat || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3">' + (so.kelas || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-amber-400 font-semibold">' + (so.pembimbing || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-4 py-3 text-center"><button onclick="bukaDetailSiswa(\'' + so.noInduk + '\')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-blue-400"><i data-lucide="eye" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i></button></td>' +
            '</tr>';
          }).join('');
        }
      }
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function switchDataTab(tab) {
      currentDataTab = tab;
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-data-btn').forEach(function(btn) {
        btn.classList.remove('bg-emerald-600','text-white','shadow-md','bg-purple-600','bg-amber-600');
        btn.classList.add('text-slate-400','hover:text-white','hover:bg-slate-800');
      });
      var activeBtn = document.getElementById('tab-data-' + tab);
      if (activeBtn) {
        activeBtn.classList.remove('text-slate-400','hover:text-white','hover:bg-slate-800');
        if (tab === 'kelas') activeBtn.classList.add('bg-emerald-600','text-white','shadow-md');
        else if (tab === 'nabil') activeBtn.classList.add('bg-purple-600','text-white','shadow-md');
        else if (tab === 'sorogan') activeBtn.classList.add('bg-amber-600','text-white','shadow-md');
      }

      document.querySelectorAll('.filter-kelas-only').forEach(function(el) { el.classList.toggle('hidden', tab !== 'kelas'); });
      document.querySelectorAll('.filter-nabil-only').forEach(function(el) { el.classList.toggle('hidden', tab !== 'nabil'); });
      document.querySelectorAll('.filter-sorogan-only').forEach(function(el) { el.classList.toggle('hidden', tab !== 'sorogan'); });

      if (tab === 'kelas') populateFilterKelasManual();
      else if (tab === 'nabil') populateFilterNabil();
      else if (tab === 'sorogan') populateFilterSorogan();

      renderAbsenSiswaTable();
      refreshIcons();
    }

    /* ----- Form cetak absen ----- */

    function openFormAbsen() {
      var kelas = (document.getElementById('filterAbsenKelasCetak') || {}).value || '';
      if (!kelas) { showBanner('Pilih kelas terlebih dahulu!', 'error'); return; }

      document.getElementById('f_absenKelas').value = kelas;
      showBanner('Memuat pengaturan...', 'info');

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function(setting) {
            document.getElementById('f_absenJudul').value = setting.judul || 'DAFTAR HADIR SISWA';
            document.getElementById('f_absenSemester').value = setting.semester || 'Ganjil';
            document.getElementById('f_absenTahun').value = setting.tahun || '';
            document.getElementById('f_absenTanggal').value = setting.tanggal || '';
            document.getElementById('f_absenKepalaSekolah').value = setting.kepalaSekolah || "ACH. MA'RUF TAMAM, S.Pd.";
            updateGuruList();
            if (setting.guru) {
              var gs = document.getElementById('f_absenGuru');
              for (var i = 0; i < gs.options.length; i++) {
                if (gs.options[i].value === setting.guru) { gs.selectedIndex = i; updateMapelAndKelas(); break; }
              }
            }
            if (setting.waliKelas) {
              var ws = document.getElementById('f_absenWaliKelas');
              for (var j = 0; j < ws.options.length; j++) {
                if (ws.options[j].value === setting.waliKelas) { ws.selectedIndex = j; break; }
              }
            }
            openModal('modalFormAbsen');
          })
          .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
            updateGuruList();
            openModal('modalFormAbsen');
          })
          .getAbsenSetting(kelas);
      } else {
        updateGuruList();
        openModal('modalFormAbsen');
      }
    }

    function closeFormAbsen() {
      document.getElementById('modalFormAbsen').classList.add('hidden');
    }

    function updateGuruList() {
      var semester = document.getElementById('f_absenSemester').value;
      var guruSelect = document.getElementById('f_absenGuru');
      var mapelInput = document.getElementById('f_absenMapel');
      guruSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Guru --</option>';
      mapelInput.value = '';

      var filtered = (appState.guru || []).filter(function(g) {
        return semester === 'Ganjil' ? (g.mapel1 && g.mapel1.trim() !== '') : (g.mapel2 && g.mapel2.trim() !== '');
      });
      filtered.sort(function(a, b) { return (a.nama || '').localeCompare(b.nama || '', 'id', { sensitivity: 'base' }); });

      filtered.forEach(function(g) {
        var opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = g.nama;
        var mapel = semester === 'Ganjil' ? (g.mapel1 || '') : (g.mapel2 || '');
        var kelas = semester === 'Ganjil' ? (g.kelas_smt1 || '') : (g.kelas_smt2 || '');
        opt.dataset.mapel = mapel;
        opt.dataset.kelas = kelas;
        opt.textContent = g.nama + ' (' + mapel + ')';
        guruSelect.appendChild(opt);
      });

      if (filtered.length === 1) { guruSelect.selectedIndex = 1; updateMapelAndKelas(); }
    }

    function updateMapelAndKelas() {
      var guruSelect = document.getElementById('f_absenGuru');
      var selectedOption = guruSelect.options[guruSelect.selectedIndex];
      var mapelInput = document.getElementById('f_absenMapel');
      if (selectedOption && selectedOption.value) {
        mapelInput.value = selectedOption.dataset.mapel || '';
      } else {
        mapelInput.value = '';
      }
    }

    function openFormAbsenNabil() {
      var kelas = (document.getElementById('filterAbsenNabilCetak') || {}).value || '';
      if (!kelas) { showBanner('Pilih kelas nabil terlebih dahulu!', 'error'); return; }
      document.getElementById('f_absenNabilKelas').value = kelas;

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function(setting) {
            document.getElementById('f_absenNabilJudul').value = setting.judul || 'DAFTAR HADIR KELOMPOK NABIL';
            document.getElementById('f_absenNabilSemester').value = setting.semester || 'Ganjil';
            document.getElementById('f_absenNabilTahun').value = setting.tahun || '';
            document.getElementById('f_absenNabilGuru').value = setting.guru || '';
            document.getElementById('f_absenNabilTanggal').value = setting.tanggal || '';
            document.getElementById('f_absenNabilKepalaSekolah').value = setting.kepalaSekolah || "ACH. MA'RUF TAMAM, S.Pd.";
            openModal('modalFormAbsenNabil');
          })
          .withFailureHandler(function(err) { openModal('modalFormAbsenNabil'); })
          .getAbsenNabilSetting(kelas);
      } else {
        openModal('modalFormAbsenNabil');
      }
    }

    function openFormAbsenSorogan() {
      var filter = (document.getElementById('filterAbsenSoroganCetak') || {}).value || '';
      if (!filter) { showBanner('Pilih Pembimbing terlebih dahulu!', 'error'); return; }

      document.getElementById('f_absenSoroganPembimbing').value = filter;
      var today = new Date();
      var bulanIndo = ['Januari','Februari','Maret','April','Mei','Juni','Juli','Agustus','September','Oktober','November','Desember'];
      var tanggalSurat = today.getDate() + ' ' + bulanIndo[today.getMonth()] + ' ' + today.getFullYear();
      document.getElementById('f_absenSoroganTanggal').value = tanggalSurat;
      document.getElementById('f_absenSoroganTahun').value = today.getFullYear() + '/' + (today.getFullYear() + 1);
      openModal('modalFormAbsenSorogan');
    }

    /* ----- Buka tab cetak absen (via Web App URL) ----- */

    function bukaHalamanAbsen(kelas, jenis, settingData) {
      getWebAppUrl().then(function(webAppUrl) {
        if (!webAppUrl) webAppUrl = window.location.origin + window.location.pathname;
        var params = new URLSearchParams({
          page: 'absen', kelas: kelas, jenis: jenis,
          judul: settingData.judul || 'DAFTAR HADIR SISWA',
          semester: settingData.semester || 'Ganjil',
          tahun: settingData.tahun || '',
          mapel: settingData.mapel || '',
          guru: settingData.guru || '',
          tanggal: settingData.tanggal || '',
          kepalaSekolah: settingData.kepalaSekolah || "ACH. MA'RUF TAMAM, S.Pd.",
          waliKelas: settingData.waliKelas || ''
        });
        window.open(webAppUrl + '?' + params.toString(), '_blank');
        closeFormAbsen();
      });
    }

    function bukaHalamanAbsenNabil(kelas, settingData) {
      getWebAppUrl().then(function(webAppUrl) {
        if (!webAppUrl) webAppUrl = window.location.origin + window.location.pathname;
        var params = new URLSearchParams({
          page: 'absen_nabil', kelas: kelas,
          judul: settingData.judul || 'DAFTAR HADIR KELOMPOK NABIL',
          semester: settingData.semester || 'Ganjil',
          tahun: settingData.tahun || '',
          mapel: 'Nabil',
          guru: settingData.guru || '',
          tanggal: settingData.tanggal || '',
          kepalaSekolah: settingData.kepalaSekolah || "ACH. MA'RUF TAMAM, S.Pd."
        });
        window.open(webAppUrl + '?' + params.toString(), '_blank');
        closeModal('modalFormAbsenNabil');
      });
    }

        /* =========================================================
       11. MODUL VALIDASI ABSEN (Massal)
       ========================================================= */

    function bukaValidasiAbsen() {
      validasiMode = 'validasi';
      validasiCheckedSet = {};

      if (currentDataTab === 'kelas') {
        validasiContext = {
          type: 'kelas', title: 'Validasi Kelas Siswa', subtitle: 'Ubah kelas siswa dalam jumlah banyak',
          filterLabel: 'Kelas', targetLabel: 'Kelas Baru',
          dataSource: appState.siswa, filterField: 'kelas',
          konteks: 'Mengubah kolom KELAS di sheet DataSiswa',
          labelDaftarkan: 'Daftarkan', labelLuluskan: 'Luluskan'
        };
      } else if (currentDataTab === 'nabil') {
        validasiContext = {
          type: 'nabil', title: 'Validasi Kelas Nabil', subtitle: 'Kelola data siswa Nabil',
          filterLabel: 'Kel. Nabil', targetLabel: 'Kel. Nabil Baru',
          dataSource: appState.nabil, filterField: 'kelasNabil',
          konteks: 'Mengubah kolom KELOMPOK_NABIL di sheet Nabil',
          labelDaftarkan: 'Tambah ke Nabil', labelLuluskan: 'Hapuskan'
        };
      } else if (currentDataTab === 'sorogan') {
        validasiContext = {
          type: 'sorogan', title: 'Validasi Pembimbing Sorogan', subtitle: 'Kelola data siswa Sorogan',
          filterLabel: 'Pembimbing', targetLabel: 'Pembimbing Baru',
          dataSource: appState.sorogan, filterField: 'pembimbing',
          konteks: 'Mengubah kolom PEMBIMBING di sheet Sorogan',
          labelDaftarkan: 'Tambah ke Sorogan', labelLuluskan: 'Hapuskan'
        };
      }
      if (!validasiContext) return;

      var lblVal = document.getElementById('labelModeValidasi');
      var lblDaf = document.getElementById('labelModeDaftarkan');
      var lblLul = document.getElementById('labelModeLuluskan');
      if (lblVal) lblVal.textContent = 'Validasi / Edit';
      if (lblDaf) lblDaf.textContent = validasiContext.labelDaftarkan;
      if (lblLul) lblLul.textContent = validasiContext.labelLuluskan;

      var tabs = document.getElementById('validasiModeTabs');
      if (tabs) tabs.classList.remove('hidden');

      document.querySelectorAll('.validasi-mode-btn').forEach(function(btn) {
        btn.classList.remove('text-orange-400','bg-orange-500/10','text-emerald-400','bg-emerald-500/10','text-blue-400','bg-blue-500/10');
        btn.classList.add('text-slate-400');
      });
      var activeBtn = document.getElementById('btnModeValidasi');
      if (activeBtn) { activeBtn.classList.remove('text-slate-400'); activeBtn.classList.add('text-orange-400','bg-orange-500/10'); }

      document.getElementById('validasiTitle').textContent = validasiContext.title;
      document.getElementById('validasiSubtitle').textContent = validasiContext.subtitle;
      document.getElementById('validasiFilterLabel').textContent = validasiContext.filterLabel;
      document.getElementById('validasiTargetLabel').textContent = validasiContext.targetLabel;
      document.getElementById('validasiKonteks').textContent = validasiContext.konteks;
      document.getElementById('validasiTombolText').textContent = 'Simpan Perubahan';

      populateValidasiFilter();
      openModal('modalValidasiAbsen');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function populateValidasiFilter() {
      if (!validasiContext) return;
      var allValues = new Set();
      (validasiContext.dataSource || []).forEach(function(item) {
        var k = (item[validasiContext.filterField] || '').trim();
        if (k) allValues.add(k);
      });
      var sortedValues = Array.from(allValues).sort();

      var filterSelect = document.getElementById('validasiFilterSelect');
      if (filterSelect) {
        filterSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih --</option>';
        sortedValues.forEach(function(k) {
          var opt = document.createElement('option');
          opt.value = k; opt.textContent = k;
          filterSelect.appendChild(opt);
        });
        filterSelect.onchange = function() { updateValidasiPreview(); };
      }

      var nilaiSelect = document.getElementById('validasiNilaiBaruSelect');
      if (nilaiSelect) {
        nilaiSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih dari daftar --</option>';
        sortedValues.forEach(function(k) {
          var opt = document.createElement('option');
          opt.value = k; opt.textContent = k;
          nilaiSelect.appendChild(opt);
        });
