      }

      var inputNilai = document.getElementById('validasiNilaiBaru');
      if (inputNilai) inputNilai.value = '';

      var preview = document.getElementById('validasiPreviewList');
      if (preview) preview.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center text-slate-500 text-[10px] py-2">Pilih filter terlebih dahulu</div>';

      var elJumlah = document.getElementById('validasiJumlah');
      var elTotal = document.getElementById('validasiTotal');
      if (elJumlah) elJumlah.textContent = '0';
      if (elTotal) elTotal.textContent = '0';
    }

    function updateValidasiPreview() {
      var val = document.getElementById('validasiFilterSelect').value;
      var preview = document.getElementById('validasiPreviewList');
      var jumlah = document.getElementById('validasiJumlah');
      var total = document.getElementById('validasiTotal');
      var inputNilai = document.getElementById('validasiNilaiBaru');
      var selectNilai = document.getElementById('validasiNilaiBaruSelect');

      var searchEl = document.getElementById('searchValidasiSiswa');
      if (searchEl) searchEl.value = '';

      if (!val) {
        preview.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center text-slate-500 text-[10px] py-2">Pilih filter terlebih dahulu</div>';
        jumlah.textContent = Object.keys(validasiCheckedSet).length;
        total.textContent = '0';
        inputNilai.value = '';
        selectNilai.value = '';
        validasiPreviewData = [];
        return;
      }

      var filtered = (validasiContext.dataSource || []).filter(function(item) {
        return (item[validasiContext.filterField] || '').trim() === val;
      });
      validasiPreviewData = filtered.slice();
      total.textContent = filtered.length;
      renderValidasiPreviewList(filtered);
    }

    function renderValidasiPreviewList(dataList) {
      var preview = document.getElementById('validasiPreviewList');
      if (dataList.length === 0) {
        preview.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center text-slate-500 text-[10px] py-2">Tidak ada siswa</div>';
        return;
      }
      preview.innerHTML = dataList.map(function(s, idx) {
        var isChecked = validasiCheckedSet[s.noInduk] ? 'checked' : '';
        return '<label class="flex items-start gap-2 px-2 py-1.5 bg-slate-900/60 hover:bg-slate-800/60 rounded-lg text-[11px] cursor-pointer transition">' +
          '<input type="checkbox" class="validasi-siswa-checkbox accent-orange-500 w-3.5 h-3.5 mt-0.5" value="' + s.noInduk + '" ' + isChecked + ' onchange="updateValidasiJumlah()">' +
          '<span class="text-slate-500 w-5 text-right shrink-0">' + (idx + 1) + '.</span>' +
          '<div class="flex-1 min-w-0"><div class="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap"><span class="font-mono-code text-white shrink-0">' + (s.noInduk || '-') + '</span><span class="text-white font-semibold">' + (s.nama || '-') + '</span></div>' +
          '<div class="text-slate-400 text-[10px] mt-0.5 truncate"><i data-lucide="map-pin" class="w-3 h-3 inline mr-1"></i>' + (s.alamat || '-') + '</div></div></label>';
      }).join('');
      refreshIcons();
      updateValidasiJumlah();
    }

    function filterValidasiSiswa() {
      var search = (document.getElementById('searchValidasiSiswa') && document.getElementById('searchValidasiSiswa').value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      if (!validasiPreviewData || validasiPreviewData.length === 0) return;
      var filtered = validasiPreviewData;
      if (search) {
        filtered = validasiPreviewData.filter(function(s) {
          return (s.nama || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) || (s.noInduk || '').toLowerCase().includes(search);
        });
      }
      renderValidasiPreviewList(filtered);
    }

    function updateValidasiJumlah() {
      var checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.validasi-siswa-checkbox');
      checkboxes.forEach(function(cb) {
        if (cb.checked) validasiCheckedSet[cb.value] = true;
        else delete validasiCheckedSet[cb.value];
      });
      var el = document.getElementById('validasiJumlah');
      if (el) el.textContent = Object.keys(validasiCheckedSet).length;
    }

    function validasiPilihSemua(pilih) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.validasi-siswa-checkbox').forEach(function(cb) {
        cb.checked = pilih;
        if (pilih) validasiCheckedSet[cb.value] = true;
        else delete validasiCheckedSet[cb.value];
      });
      updateValidasiJumlah();
    }

    function syncNilaiBaruValidasi() {
      var select = document.getElementById('validasiNilaiBaruSelect');
      var input = document.getElementById('validasiNilaiBaru');
      if (!select.value) { input.value = ''; return; }
      input.value = select.value;
    }

    function switchValidasiMode(mode) {
      validasiMode = mode;
      document.querySelectorAll('.validasi-mode-btn').forEach(function(btn) {
        btn.classList.remove('text-orange-400','bg-orange-500/10','text-emerald-400','bg-emerald-500/10','text-blue-400','bg-blue-500/10');
        btn.classList.add('text-slate-400');
      });
      var activeBtn = document.getElementById('btnMode' + mode.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + mode.slice(1));
      if (activeBtn) {
        activeBtn.classList.remove('text-slate-400');
        if (mode === 'validasi') activeBtn.classList.add('text-orange-400','bg-orange-500/10');
        else if (mode === 'daftarkan') activeBtn.classList.add('text-emerald-400','bg-emerald-500/10');
        else if (mode === 'luluskan') activeBtn.classList.add('text-blue-400','bg-blue-500/10');
      }

      var filterParent = document.getElementById('validasiFilterSelect') && document.getElementById('validasiFilterSelect').parentElement;
      var nilaiContainer = document.getElementById('validasiNilaiBaruContainer');
      var elFilterLabel = document.getElementById('validasiFilterLabel');
      var elTargetLabel = document.getElementById('validasiTargetLabel');
      var elKonteks = document.getElementById('validasiKonteks');
      var elTombol = document.getElementById('validasiTombolText');
      var type = validasiContext.type;

      if (mode === 'validasi') {
        if (filterParent) filterParent.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (nilaiContainer) nilaiContainer.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (elFilterLabel) elFilterLabel.textContent = validasiContext.filterLabel;
        if (elTargetLabel) elTargetLabel.textContent = validasiContext.targetLabel;
        if (elKonteks) elKonteks.textContent = 'Mengubah kolom ' + validasiContext.filterField.toUpperCase();
        if (elTombol) elTombol.textContent = 'Simpan Perubahan';
        populateValidasiFilter();
      } else if (mode === 'daftarkan') {
        if (type === 'kelas') {
          if (filterParent) filterParent.classList.add('hidden');
          if (nilaiContainer) nilaiContainer.classList.remove('hidden');
          if (elKonteks) elKonteks.textContent = 'Memberikan KELAS untuk siswa yang belum punya kelas';
          if (elTombol) elTombol.textContent = 'Daftarkan Siswa';
          showPreviewTanpaKelas();
        } else {
          if (filterParent) filterParent.classList.add('hidden');
          if (nilaiContainer) nilaiContainer.classList.remove('hidden');
          if (elKonteks) elKonteks.textContent = 'Menambahkan siswa ke sheet ' + (type === 'nabil' ? 'Nabil' : 'Sorogan');
          if (elTombol) elTombol.textContent = 'Tambahkan';
          showPreviewSiswaUntukDaftar(type);
        }
      } else if (mode === 'luluskan') {
        if (type === 'kelas') {
          if (filterParent) filterParent.classList.add('hidden');
          if (nilaiContainer) nilaiContainer.classList.add('hidden');
          if (elKonteks) elKonteks.textContent = 'Mengubah STATUS menjadi LULUS dan KELAS menjadi "Lulus [kelas lama]"';
          if (elTombol) elTombol.textContent = 'Luluskan Siswa';
          showPreviewKelasXII();
        } else {
          if (filterParent) filterParent.classList.remove('hidden');
          if (nilaiContainer) nilaiContainer.classList.add('hidden');
          if (elKonteks) elKonteks.textContent = 'Menghapus data dari sheet ' + (type === 'nabil' ? 'Nabil' : 'Sorogan');
          if (elTombol) elTombol.textContent = 'Hapuskan';
          populateValidasiFilter();
          document.getElementById('validasiPreviewList').innerHTML = '<div class="text-center text-slate-500 text-[10px] py-2">Pilih filter terlebih dahulu</div>';
          document.getElementById('validasiJumlah').textContent = '0';
          document.getElementById('validasiTotal').textContent = '0';
        }
      }
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function showPreviewTanpaKelas() {
      var searchEl = document.getElementById('searchValidasiSiswa');
      if (searchEl) searchEl.value = '';
      var filtered = (appState.siswa || []).filter(function(s) {
        var kelas = (s.kelas || '').trim();
        return !kelas;
      });
      validasiPreviewData = filtered.slice();
      document.getElementById('validasiTotal').textContent = filtered.length;
      renderValidasiPreviewList(filtered);
    }

    function showPreviewKelasXII() {
      var searchEl = document.getElementById('searchValidasiSiswa');
      if (searchEl) searchEl.value = '';
      var filtered = (appState.siswa || []).filter(function(s) {
        var kelas = (s.kelas || '').trim();
        return /XII/i.test(kelas) && !kelas.toLowerCase().includes('lulus');
      });
      validasiPreviewData = filtered.slice();
      document.getElementById('validasiTotal').textContent = filtered.length;
      renderValidasiPreviewList(filtered);
    }

    function showPreviewSiswaUntukDaftar(type) {
      var searchEl = document.getElementById('searchValidasiSiswa');
      if (searchEl) searchEl.value = '';
      var existingSet = {};
      (validasiContext.dataSource || []).forEach(function(item) { existingSet[item.noInduk] = true; });
      var filtered = (appState.siswa || []).filter(function(s) {
        var kelas = (s.kelas || '').trim();
        if (!kelas || !/X|XI|XII/i.test(kelas)) return false;
        if (kelas.toLowerCase().includes('lulus')) return false;
        if (existingSet[s.noInduk]) return false;
        return true;
      });
      validasiPreviewData = filtered.slice();
      document.getElementById('validasiTotal').textContent = filtered.length;
      renderValidasiPreviewList(filtered);
    }

    function prosesValidasiAbsen() {
      if (!validasiContext) return;
      var noIndukList = Object.keys(validasiCheckedSet);
      if (noIndukList.length === 0) { showBanner('Pilih minimal 1 siswa!', 'error'); return; }
      var type = validasiContext.type;

      if (validasiMode === 'daftarkan') {
        var newValue = document.getElementById('validasiNilaiBaru').value.trim();
        if (type === 'kelas') {
          if (!newValue) { showBanner('Isi kelas untuk siswa baru!', 'error'); return; }
          if (!confirm('Daftarkan ' + noIndukList.length + ' siswa ke kelas "' + newValue + '"?')) return;
          closeModal('modalValidasiAbsen');
          showBanner('⏳ Mendaftarkan siswa...', 'info');
          google.script.run
            .withSuccessHandler(function(result) {
              if (result.success) {
                showBanner('✅ ' + result.message, 'success');
                var set = {};
                noIndukList.forEach(function(ni) { set[ni] = true; });
                appState.siswa.forEach(function(s) { if (set[s.noInduk]) s.kelas = newValue; });
                renderAbsenSiswaTable();
                populateFilterKelasManual();
              } else showBanner('❌ ' + (result.message || 'Gagal'), 'error');
            })
            .withFailureHandler(function(err) { showBanner('❌ ' + err.message, 'error'); })
            .bulkUpdateField({ type: 'kelas', field: 'kelas', newValue: newValue, noIndukList: noIndukList });
          return;
        } else {
          if (!newValue) { showBanner('Isi nilai terlebih dahulu!', 'error'); return; }
          if (!confirm('Tambahkan ' + noIndukList.length + ' siswa ke ' + (type === 'nabil' ? 'Nabil' : 'Sorogan') + '?')) return;
          closeModal('modalValidasiAbsen');
          showBanner('⏳ Menambahkan siswa...', 'info');
          google.script.run
            .withSuccessHandler(function(result) {
              if (result.success) {
                showBanner('✅ ' + result.message, 'success');
                setTimeout(function() { refreshDataFromSheets(); }, 1000);
              } else showBanner('❌ ' + (result.message || 'Gagal'), 'error');
            })
            .withFailureHandler(function(err) { showBanner('❌ ' + err.message, 'error'); })
            .addSiswaToEkstra({ type: type, newValue: newValue, noIndukList: noIndukList });
          return;
        }
      }

      if (validasiMode === 'luluskan') {
        if (type === 'kelas') {
          if (!confirm('Luluskan ' + noIndukList.length + ' siswa?')) return;
          closeModal('modalValidasiAbsen');
          showBanner('⏳ Meluluskan siswa...', 'info');
          google.script.run
            .withSuccessHandler(function(result) {
              if (result.success) {
                showBanner('✅ ' + result.message, 'success');
                var set = {};
                noIndukList.forEach(function(ni) { set[ni] = true; });
                appState.siswa.forEach(function(s) {
                  if (set[s.noInduk]) {
                    s.status = 'Lulus';
                    if (!s.kelas.toLowerCase().startsWith('lulus')) s.kelas = 'Lulus ' + (s.kelas || '');
                  }
                });
                renderAbsenSiswaTable();
                populateFilterKelasManual();
              } else showBanner('❌ ' + (result.message || 'Gagal'), 'error');
            })
            .withFailureHandler(function(err) { showBanner('❌ ' + err.message, 'error'); })
            .bulkLuluskanSiswa(noIndukList);
          return;
        } else {
          if (!confirm('Hapuskan ' + noIndukList.length + ' siswa dari ' + (type === 'nabil' ? 'Nabil' : 'Sorogan') + '?')) return;
          closeModal('modalValidasiAbsen');
          showBanner('⏳ Menghapus data...', 'info');
          google.script.run
            .withSuccessHandler(function(result) {
              if (result.success) {
                showBanner('✅ ' + result.message, 'success');
                setTimeout(function() { refreshDataFromSheets(); }, 1000);
              } else showBanner('❌ ' + (result.message || 'Gagal'), 'error');
            })
            .withFailureHandler(function(err) { showBanner('❌ ' + err.message, 'error'); })
            .deleteSiswaFromEkstra({ type: type, noIndukList: noIndukList });
          return;
        }
      }

      // Mode validasi
      var filterValue = document.getElementById('validasiFilterSelect').value;
      var newValue2 = document.getElementById('validasiNilaiBaru').value.trim();
      if (!filterValue) { showBanner('Pilih filter terlebih dahulu!', 'error'); return; }
      if (!newValue2) { showBanner('Isi nilai baru terlebih dahulu!', 'error'); return; }
      if (!confirm('Ubah ' + validasiContext.filterLabel + ' menjadi "' + newValue2 + '" untuk ' + noIndukList.length + ' siswa?')) return;
      closeModal('modalValidasiAbsen');
      showBanner('⏳ Menyimpan...', 'info');

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(result) {
          if (result.success) {
            showBanner('✅ ' + result.message, 'success');
            var set = {};
            noIndukList.forEach(function(ni) { set[ni] = true; });
            (validasiContext.dataSource || []).forEach(function(item) {
              if (set[item.noInduk]) item[validasiContext.filterField] = newValue2;
            });
            renderAbsenSiswaTable();
            if (type === 'kelas') populateFilterKelasManual();
            else if (type === 'nabil') populateFilterNabil();
            else if (type === 'sorogan') populateFilterSorogan();
          } else showBanner('❌ ' + (result.message || 'Gagal'), 'error');
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) { showBanner('❌ ' + err.message, 'error'); })
        .bulkUpdateField({
          type: type, field: validasiContext.filterField,
          oldValue: filterValue, newValue: newValue2, noIndukList: noIndukList
        });
    }


    /* =========================================================
       12. MODUL SURAT (SK Aktif & SK Lulus)
       ========================================================= */

    function bukaFormSK() {
      semuaSiswaSK = appState.siswa.slice();
      var select = document.getElementById('selectSK');
      select.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Siswa --</option>';
      appState.siswa.forEach(function(s) {
        var opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = s.noInduk;
        opt.textContent = s.noInduk + ' - ' + s.nama + ' (' + (s.kelas || '-') + ')';
        select.appendChild(opt);
      });
      document.getElementById('searchSK').value = '';
      document.getElementById('skJumlahSiswa').textContent = appState.siswa.length + ' siswa tersedia';

      var today = new Date();
      var bulanIndo = ['Januari','Februari','Maret','April','Mei','Juni','Juli','Agustus','September','Oktober','November','Desember'];
      var tanggalSurat = today.getDate() + ' ' + bulanIndo[today.getMonth()] + ' ' + today.getFullYear();
      document.getElementById('sk-nomor').value = '421.3/SMAI-DUBA/SK/' + (today.getMonth() + 1) + '/' + today.getFullYear();
      document.getElementById('sk-tahun-pelajaran').value = today.getFullYear() + '/' + (today.getFullYear() + 1);
      document.getElementById('sk-tanggal-cetak').value = tanggalSurat;

      updateTabelKehadiran();
      openModal('modalPilihSK');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function bukaFormSKSiswa(noInduk) {
      bukaFormSK();
      setTimeout(function() {
        var select = document.getElementById('selectSK');
        for (var i = 0; i < select.options.length; i++) {
          if (select.options[i].value === noInduk) { select.selectedIndex = i; break; }
        }
      }, 100);
    }

    function filterSiswaSK() {
      var search = document.getElementById('searchSK').value.toLowerCase().trim();
      var select = document.getElementById('selectSK');
      select.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Siswa --</option>';
      var filtered = appState.siswa;
      if (search) {
        filtered = appState.siswa.filter(function(s) {
          return (s.nama || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
                 (s.noInduk || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
                 (s.nisn || '').toLowerCase().includes(search);
        });
      }
      filtered.forEach(function(s) {
        var opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = s.noInduk;
        opt.textContent = s.noInduk + ' - ' + s.nama + ' (' + (s.kelas || '-') + ')';
        select.appendChild(opt);
      });
      document.getElementById('skJumlahSiswa').textContent = filtered.length + ' siswa tersedia';
    }

    function updateTabelKehadiran() {
      var bulanAwal = document.getElementById('sk-bulan-awal').value;
      var bulanAkhir = document.getElementById('sk-bulan-akhir').value;
      var container = document.getElementById('sk-kehadiran-container');
      var bulanIndo = ['Januari','Februari','Maret','April','Mei','Juni','Juli','Agustus','September','Oktober','November','Desember'];
      var idxAwal = bulanIndo.indexOf(bulanAwal);
      var idxAkhir = bulanIndo.indexOf(bulanAkhir);

      if (idxAwal === -1 || idxAkhir === -1) {
        container.innerHTML = '<p class="text-slate-500 text-xs">Pilih bulan awal dan akhir terlebih dahulu</p>';
        return;
      }
      if (idxAwal > idxAkhir) {
        container.innerHTML = '<p class="text-red-400 text-xs">Bulan awal tidak boleh lebih besar dari bulan akhir!</p>';
        return;
      }

      var html = '<div class="grid grid-cols-6 gap-2 items-center bg-slate-800 p-2 rounded-lg mb-2"><div class="text-center text-slate-300 text-[10px] font-bold">NO</div><div class="text-center text-slate-300 text-[10px] font-bold">BULAN</div><div class="text-center text-slate-300 text-[10px] font-bold">HADIR</div><div class="text-center text-slate-300 text-[10px] font-bold">IZIN</div><div class="text-center text-slate-300 text-[10px] font-bold">SAKIT</div><div class="text-center text-slate-300 text-[10px] font-bold">TANPA</div></div>';
      var no = 1;
      for (var i = idxAwal; i <= idxAkhir; i++) {
        var bulan = bulanIndo[i];
        html += '<div class="grid grid-cols-6 gap-2 items-center bg-slate-900/60 p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-700">' +
          '<div class="text-center text-slate-400 text-[10px] font-bold">' + no + '.</div>' +
          '<div class="text-xs text-white font-semibold col-span-1">' + bulan + '</div>' +
          '<div><input type="number" id="sk-hadir-' + i + '" value="0" min="0" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-[10px] text-white text-center"></div>' +
          '<div><input type="number" id="sk-izin-' + i + '" value="0" min="0" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-[10px] text-white text-center"></div>' +
          '<div><input type="number" id="sk-sakit-' + i + '" value="0" min="0" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-[10px] text-white text-center"></div>' +
          '<div><input type="number" id="sk-tanpa-' + i + '" value="0" min="0" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-[10px] text-white text-center"></div>' +
        '</div>';
        no++;
      }
      container.innerHTML = html;
    }

    function bukaFormSKLulus() {
      var select = document.getElementById('selectSKLulus');
      select.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Siswa --</option>';
      appState.siswa.forEach(function(s) {
        var opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = s.noInduk;
        opt.textContent = s.noInduk + ' - ' + s.nama + ' (' + (s.kelas || '-') + ')';
        select.appendChild(opt);
      });
      document.getElementById('searchSKLulus').value = '';
      document.getElementById('sklJumlahSiswa').textContent = appState.siswa.length + ' siswa tersedia';

      var today = new Date();
      var bulanIndo = ['Januari','Februari','Maret','April','Mei','Juni','Juli','Agustus','September','Oktober','November','Desember'];
      document.getElementById('skl-nomor').value = '421.3/SMAI-DUBA/SKL/' + (today.getMonth() + 1) + '/' + today.getFullYear();
      document.getElementById('skl-tanggal-cetak').value = today.getDate() + ' ' + bulanIndo[today.getMonth()] + ' ' + today.getFullYear();
      openModal('modalPilihSKLulus');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function filterSiswaSKLulus() {
      var search = document.getElementById('searchSKLulus').value.toLowerCase().trim();
      var select = document.getElementById('selectSKLulus');
      select.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Siswa --</option>';
      var filtered = appState.siswa;
      if (search) {
        filtered = appState.siswa.filter(function(s) {
          return (s.nama || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
                 (s.noInduk || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
                 (s.nisn || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
                 (s.kelas || '').toLowerCase().includes(search);
        });
      }
      filtered.forEach(function(s) {
        var opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = s.noInduk;
        opt.textContent = s.noInduk + ' - ' + s.nama + ' (' + (s.kelas || '-') + ')';
        select.appendChild(opt);
      });
      document.getElementById('sklJumlahSiswa').textContent = filtered.length + ' siswa tersedia';
    }

    function prosesCetakSKLulus() {
      var noInduk = document.getElementById('selectSKLulus').value;
      if (!noInduk) { showBanner('Pilih siswa terlebih dahulu!', 'error'); return; }
      var nomor = document.getElementById('skl-nomor').value || '';
      var tempat = document.getElementById('skl-tempat').value || 'Pamekasan';
      var tanggalCetak = document.getElementById('skl-tanggal-cetak').value || '';
      closeModal('modalPilihSKLulus');
      showBanner('⏳ Membuka SK Lulus...', 'info');
      getWebAppUrl().then(function(webAppUrl) {
        if (!webAppUrl) webAppUrl = window.location.origin + window.location.pathname;
        var params = new URLSearchParams({
          page: 'sk_lulus', noInduk: noInduk, nomor: nomor,
          tempat: tempat, tanggalSurat: tanggalCetak
        });
        window.open(webAppUrl + '?' + params.toString(), '_blank');
        showBanner('✅ SK Lulus berhasil dibuka!', 'success');
      });
    }

    function getWebAppUrl() {
      return new Promise(function(resolve) {
        if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
          google.script.run
            .withSuccessHandler(function(url) { resolve(url); })
            .withFailureHandler(function() { resolve(window.location.origin + window.location.pathname); })
            .getWebAppUrl();
        } else {
          resolve(window.location.origin + window.location.pathname);
        }
      });
    }

    /* ===== PENERBITAN NOMOR SURAT ===== */

    function bukaModalNomorSurat() {
      if (!currentUser) {
        showBanner('Anda belum login!', 'error');
        return;
      }

      // Reset UI
      document.getElementById('ns-user').value = currentUser.name || currentUser.username;
      document.getElementById('ns-jabatan').value = 'Memuat...';
      document.getElementById('ns-bagian').innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Bagian --</option>';
      document.getElementById('ns-jenis').innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Jenis --</option>';
      document.getElementById('ns-perihal').value = '';
      document.getElementById('ns-hasil-container').classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById('ns-lembaga').textContent = '-';
      document.getElementById('ns-pesantren').textContent = '-';

      openModal('modalNomorSurat');
      refreshIcons();

      if (typeof google === 'undefined' || !google.script || !google.script.run) {
        showBanner('Mode offline', 'error');
        return;
      }

      // Ambil config + jabatan user
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (!res || res.status !== 'success') {
            showBanner('❌ ' + (res.message || 'Gagal memuat konfigurasi'), 'error');
            return;
          }
          _konfigSuratCache = res;

          // Isi dropdown bagian
          var selB = document.getElementById('ns-bagian');
          selB.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Bagian --</option>';
          (res.bagian || []).forEach(function(b) {
            var opt = document.createElement('option');
            opt.value = b.kode;
            opt.textContent = b.kode + ' - ' + b.nama;
            selB.appendChild(opt);
          });

          // Isi dropdown jenis
          var selJ = document.getElementById('ns-jenis');
          selJ.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Jenis --</option>';
          (res.jenis || []).forEach(function(j) {
            var opt = document.createElement('option');
            opt.value = j.kode;
            opt.textContent = j.kode + ' - ' + j.nama;
            selJ.appendChild(opt);
          });

          // Isi kode lembaga & pesantren
          document.getElementById('ns-lembaga').textContent = res.kodeLembaga || '-';
          document.getElementById('ns-pesantren').textContent = res.kodePesantren || '-';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          showBanner('❌ Error: ' + err.message, 'error');
        })
        .getKonfigSurat();

      // Ambil jabatan berdasarkan ROLE
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          var el = document.getElementById('ns-jabatan');
          if (res && res.status === 'success' && res.jabatan) {
            el.value = res.jabatan.kode + ' - ' + res.jabatan.nama;
          } else {
            el.value = '⚠ Role "' + currentUser.role + '" tidak ditemukan di KonfigSurat';
            showBanner('⚠ Role "' + currentUser.role + '" tidak ditemukan di KonfigSurat.', 'warning');
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function() {
          document.getElementById('ns-jabatan').value = '⚠ Gagal memuat';
        })
        .getInfoUserSurat(currentUser.role);
    }

    function prosesTerbitkanNomorSurat() {
      var bagian = document.getElementById('ns-bagian').value;
      var jenis = document.getElementById('ns-jenis').value;
      var perihal = document.getElementById('ns-perihal').value.trim();

      if (!bagian) { showBanner('Pilih Bagian Surat!', 'error'); return; }
      if (!jenis) { showBanner('Pilih Jenis Surat!', 'error'); return; }

      var btn = document.getElementById('btn-terbitkan-nomor');
      if (btn) btn.disabled = true;

      showBanner('⏳ Menerbitkan nomor surat...', 'info');

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            var c = document.getElementById('ns-hasil-container');
            document.getElementById('ns-hasil-nomor').textContent = res.nomorSurat;
            document.getElementById('ns-hasil-info').textContent =
              'Diterbitkan oleh: ' + res.jabatan.nama + ' • ' + res.tanggal;
            c.classList.remove('hidden');
            showBanner('✅ Nomor surat berhasil diterbitkan!', 'success');
            refreshIcons();
          } else {
            showBanner('❌ ' + (res.message || 'Gagal menerbitkan'), 'error');
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
          showBanner('❌ Error: ' + err.message, 'error');
        })
        .terbitkanNomorSurat({
          username: currentUser.name || currentUser.username,
          role: currentUser.role,
          kodeBagian: bagian,
          kodeJenis: jenis,
          perihal: perihal
        });
    }

    function copyNomorSurat() {
      var nomor = document.getElementById('ns-hasil-nomor').textContent;
      if (!nomor || nomor === '-') return;

      if (navigator.clipboard) {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(nomor).then(function() {
          showBanner('✅ Nomor surat disalin!', 'success');
        }).catch(function() {
          showBanner('Gagal menyalin.', 'error');
        });
      } else {
        var ta = document.createElement('textarea');
        ta.value = nomor;
        document.body.appendChild(ta);
        ta.select();
        document.execCommand('copy');
        document.body.removeChild(ta);
        showBanner('✅ Nomor surat disalin!', 'success');
      }
    }

        /* ===== KONFIGURASI SURAT ===== */

    function bukaModalKonfigSurat() {
      if (typeof google === 'undefined' || !google.script || !google.script.run) {
        showBanner('Mode offline', 'error');
        return;
      }

      showBanner('⏳ Memuat konfigurasi...', 'info');
      ['ks-tbody-jabatan','ks-tbody-bagian','ks-tbody-jenis'].forEach(function(id) {
        var el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (el) el.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="3" class="px-2 py-3 text-center text-slate-500 text-[10px]">Memuat...</td></tr>';
      });

      openModal('modalKonfigSurat');
      refreshIcons();

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (!res || res.status !== 'success') {
            showBanner('❌ ' + (res.message || 'Gagal memuat'), 'error');
            return;
          }
          document.getElementById('ks-lembaga').value = res.kodeLembaga || '';
          document.getElementById('ks-pesantren').value = res.kodePesantren || '';

          document.getElementById('ks-tbody-jabatan').innerHTML = '';
          document.getElementById('ks-tbody-bagian').innerHTML = '';
          document.getElementById('ks-tbody-jenis').innerHTML = '';

          (res.jabatanList || []).forEach(function(j) { tambahBarisJabatan(j); });
          (res.bagianList || []).forEach(function(b) { tambahBarisBagian(b); });
          (res.jenisList || []).forEach(function(j) { tambahBarisJenis(j); });

          if (!res.jabatanList || res.jabatanList.length === 0) tambahBarisJabatan();
          if (!res.bagianList || res.bagianList.length === 0) tambahBarisBagian();
          if (!res.jenisList || res.jenisList.length === 0) tambahBarisJenis();
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          showBanner('❌ Error: ' + err.message, 'error');
        })
        .getKonfigSuratRaw();
    }

    function tambahBarisJabatan(data) {
      var tbody = document.getElementById('ks-tbody-jabatan');
      if (!tbody) return;
      var d = data || {};
      var tr = document.createElement('tr');
      tr.className = 'ks-row-jabatan hover:bg-slate-800/40';
      tr.innerHTML =
        '<td class="px-1 py-1"><input type="text" class="ks-kode w-full px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded text-[11px] text-white" value="' + (d.kode || '') + '" placeholder="K.01"></td>' +
        '<td class="px-1 py-1"><input type="text" class="ks-nama w-full px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded text-[11px] text-white" value="' + (d.nama || '') + '" placeholder="Nama Jabatan"></td>' +
        '<td class="px-1 py-1 text-center"><button onclick="hapusBarisKonfigSurat(this)" class="p-1 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-red-950 text-red-400" title="Hapus"><i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-3 h-3"></i></button></td>';
      tbody.appendChild(tr);
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function tambahBarisBagian(data) {
      var tbody = document.getElementById('ks-tbody-bagian');
      if (!tbody) return;
      var d = data || {};
      var tr = document.createElement('tr');
      tr.className = 'ks-row-bagian hover:bg-slate-800/40';
      tr.innerHTML =
        '<td class="px-1 py-1"><input type="text" class="ks-kode w-full px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded text-[11px] text-white" value="' + (d.kode || '') + '" placeholder="B.01"></td>' +
        '<td class="px-1 py-1"><input type="text" class="ks-nama w-full px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded text-[11px] text-white" value="' + (d.nama || '') + '" placeholder="Nama Bagian"></td>' +
        '<td class="px-1 py-1 text-center"><button onclick="hapusBarisKonfigSurat(this)" class="p-1 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-red-950 text-red-400" title="Hapus"><i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-3 h-3"></i></button></td>';
      tbody.appendChild(tr);
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function tambahBarisJenis(data) {
      var tbody = document.getElementById('ks-tbody-jenis');
      if (!tbody) return;
      var d = data || {};
      var tr = document.createElement('tr');
      tr.className = 'ks-row-jenis hover:bg-slate-800/40';
      tr.innerHTML =
        '<td class="px-1 py-1"><input type="text" class="ks-kode w-full px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded text-[11px] text-white" value="' + (d.kode || '') + '" placeholder="J.01"></td>' +
        '<td class="px-1 py-1"><input type="text" class="ks-nama w-full px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded text-[11px] text-white" value="' + (d.nama || '') + '" placeholder="Nama Jenis Surat"></td>' +
        '<td class="px-1 py-1 text-center"><button onclick="hapusBarisKonfigSurat(this)" class="p-1 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-red-950 text-red-400" title="Hapus"><i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-3 h-3"></i></button></td>';
      tbody.appendChild(tr);
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function hapusBarisKonfigSurat(btn) {
      var tr = btn.closest('tr');
      if (tr) tr.remove();
    }

    function bacaListDariTbody_(tbodyId, rowClass) {
      var list = [];
      document.querySelectorAll('#' + tbodyId + ' .' + rowClass).forEach(function(tr) {
        var kode = tr.querySelector('.ks-kode').value.trim();
        var nama = tr.querySelector('.ks-nama').value.trim();
        if (kode || nama) list.push({ kode: kode, nama: nama });
      });
      return list;
    }

    function simpanKonfigSurat() {
      var kodeLembaga = document.getElementById('ks-lembaga').value.trim();
      var kodePesantren = document.getElementById('ks-pesantren').value.trim();

      if (!kodeLembaga || !kodePesantren) {
        showBanner('Kode Lembaga dan Pesantren wajib diisi!', 'error');
        return;
      }

      var jabatanList = bacaListDariTbody_('ks-tbody-jabatan', 'ks-row-jabatan');
      var bagianList  = bacaListDariTbody_('ks-tbody-bagian',  'ks-row-bagian');
      var jenisList   = bacaListDariTbody_('ks-tbody-jenis',   'ks-row-jenis');

      if (jabatanList.length === 0 && bagianList.length === 0 && jenisList.length === 0) {
        showBanner('Minimal isi 1 data!', 'error');
        return;
      }

      var btn = document.getElementById('btn-simpan-konfig-surat');
      if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
      showBanner('⏳ Menyimpan konfigurasi...', 'info');

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            showBanner('✅ ' + res.message, 'success');
            _konfigSuratCache = null;
            setTimeout(function() { closeModal('modalKonfigSurat'); }, 800);
          } else {
            showBanner('❌ ' + (res.message || 'Gagal simpan'), 'error');
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
          showBanner('❌ Error: ' + err.message, 'error');
        })
        .saveKonfigSurat({
          kodeLembaga: kodeLembaga,
          kodePesantren: kodePesantren,
          jabatanList: jabatanList,
          bagianList: bagianList,
          jenisList: jenisList
        });
    }

    /* =========================================================
       13. MODUL EXPORT DATABASE
       ========================================================= */

    function bukaFormExportDatabase() {
      var namaUser = currentUser ? (currentUser.name || currentUser.username) : 'Administrator';
      document.getElementById('exportNamaPengunduh').textContent = namaUser;

      var kelasSet = new Set();
      appState.siswa.forEach(function(s) {
        var k = (s.kelas || '').trim();
        if (!k) return;
        kelasSet.add(k);
        var normal = k.replace(/^Berhenti\s*/i, '').trim();
        if (normal) kelasSet.add(normal);
      });

      var arr = Array.from(kelasSet);
      var order = { 'X': 1, 'XI': 2, 'XII': 3, 'Angkatan': 4 };
      arr.sort(function(a, b) {
        var ap = (a.match(/^(X|XI|XII|Angkatan)/i) || [''])[0];
        var bp = (b.match(/^(X|XI|XII|Angkatan)/i) || [''])[0];
        var ao = order[ap] || 99, bo = order[bp] || 99;
        if (ao !== bo) return ao - bo;
        return a.localeCompare(b, 'id', { sensitivity: 'base' });
      });
      exportKelasOptions = arr.map(function(k) { return { value: k, label: k }; });

      renderExportKelasList('');
      document.getElementById('searchExportKelas').value = '';
      updateExportKelasCount();

      populateExportAngkatan();
      renderExportAngkatanList('');
      document.getElementById('searchExportAngkatan').value = '';
      updateExportAngkatanCount();

      openModal('modalExportDatabase');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function renderExportKelasList(search) {
      var container = document.getElementById('exportKelasList');
      if (!container) return;
      var filtered = exportKelasOptions;
      if (search) {
        var s = search.toLowerCase();
        filtered = exportKelasOptions.filter(function(o) { return o.label.toLowerCase().includes(s); });
      }
      if (filtered.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center text-slate-500 text-[10px] py-2">Tidak ada kelas</div>';
        return;
      }
      container.innerHTML = filtered.map(function(o) {
        var id = 'chk_export_' + o.value.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '_');
        return '<label class="flex items-center gap-2 px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-800/60 cursor-pointer transition"><input type="checkbox" class="export-kelas-checkbox accent-green-500 w-3.5 h-3.5" value="' + o.value + '" id="' + id + '" onchange="updateExportKelasCount()"><span class="text-xs text-slate-200">' + o.label + '</span></label>';
      }).join('');
    }

    function filterExportKelasList() {
      renderExportKelasList(document.getElementById('searchExportKelas').value || '');
    }

    function updateExportKelasCount() {
      var checked = document.querySelectorAll('.export-kelas-checkbox:checked');
      document.getElementById('exportKelasTerpilih').textContent = checked.length;
      document.getElementById('exportKelasTersedia').textContent = exportKelasOptions.length;
    }

    function clearExportKelas() {
      document.querySelectorAll('.export-kelas-checkbox').forEach(function(c) { c.checked = false; });
      updateExportKelasCount();
    }

    function populateExportAngkatan() {
      var set = new Set();
      (appState.siswa || []).forEach(function(s) {
        var m = String(s.masuk || '').trim();
        var match = m.match(/\d{4}/);
        if (match) set.add(match[0]);
      });
      var arr = Array.from(set);
      arr.sort(function(a, b) { return parseInt(b, 10) - parseInt(a, 10); });
      exportAngkatanOptions = arr.map(function(t) { return { value: t, label: t }; });
    }

    function renderExportAngkatanList(search) {
      var container = document.getElementById('exportAngkatanList');
      if (!container) return;
      var filtered = exportAngkatanOptions;
      if (search) {
        var s = search.toLowerCase();
        filtered = exportAngkatanOptions.filter(function(o) { return o.label.toLowerCase().includes(s); });
      }
      if (filtered.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center text-slate-500 text-[10px] py-2">Tidak ada angkatan</div>';
        return;
      }
      container.innerHTML = filtered.map(function(o) {
        var id = 'chk_export_angkatan_' + o.value.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '_');
        return '<label class="flex items-center gap-2 px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-800/60 cursor-pointer transition"><input type="checkbox" class="export-angkatan-checkbox accent-green-500 w-3.5 h-3.5" value="' + o.value + '" id="' + id + '" onchange="updateExportAngkatanCount()"><span class="text-xs text-slate-200">' + o.label + '</span></label>';
      }).join('');
    }

    function filterExportAngkatanList() {
      renderExportAngkatanList(document.getElementById('searchExportAngkatan').value || '');
    }

    function updateExportAngkatanCount() {
      var checked = document.querySelectorAll('.export-angkatan-checkbox:checked');
      document.getElementById('exportAngkatanTerpilih').textContent = checked.length;
      document.getElementById('exportAngkatanTersedia').textContent = exportAngkatanOptions.length;
    }

    function clearExportAngkatan() {
      document.querySelectorAll('.export-angkatan-checkbox').forEach(function(c) { c.checked = false; });
      updateExportAngkatanCount();
    }

    function prosesExportDatabase() {
      var checkedKelas = document.querySelectorAll('.export-kelas-checkbox:checked');
      var filtersKelas = [];
      checkedKelas.forEach(function(c) { filtersKelas.push(c.value); });

      var checkedAngkatan = document.querySelectorAll('.export-angkatan-checkbox:checked');
      var filtersAngkatan = [];
      checkedAngkatan.forEach(function(c) { filtersAngkatan.push(c.value); });

      var namaUser = currentUser ? (currentUser.name || currentUser.username) : 'Administrator';
      var now = new Date();
      var waktuPengunduhan = now.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });

      closeModal('modalExportDatabase');
      showBanner('⏳ Sedang menyiapkan file Excel...', 'info');

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function(result) {
            if (result.success && result.excel) {
              showBanner('✅ ' + result.total + ' data diexport.', 'success');
              var binary = atob(result.excel);
              var bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
              for (var i = 0; i < binary.length; i++) bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
              var blob = new Blob([bytes], { type: 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet' });
              var link = document.createElement('a');
              link.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
              link.download = result.filename || 'Database_Siswa.xlsx';
              document.body.appendChild(link); link.click(); document.body.removeChild(link);
              setTimeout(function() { URL.revokeObjectURL(link.href); }, 100);
            } else {
              showBanner('❌ Gagal: ' + (result.message || 'Unknown error'), 'error');
            }
          })
          .withFailureHandler(function(err) { showBanner('❌ Error: ' + err.message, 'error'); })
          .exportDatabaseExcel({
            filters: filtersKelas.concat(filtersAngkatan),
            filtersKelas: filtersKelas,
            filtersAngkatan: filtersAngkatan,
            namaPengunduh: namaUser,
            waktuPengunduhan: waktuPengunduhan
          });
      }
    }


    /* =========================================================
       14. MODUL CETAK (Raport & Ijazah — tombol PDF locked)
       ========================================================= */

    function bukaModalCetakRaport() {
      if (!currentDetailNilaiData) { showBanner('Tidak ada data nilai!', 'error'); return; }
      var noInduk = currentDetailNilaiData.noInduk;
      var nama = currentDetailNilaiData.nama;
      var santri = appState.siswa.find(function(s) { return s.noInduk === noInduk; });
      if (!santri) { showBanner('Data siswa tidak ditemukan!', 'error'); return; }

      var existing = document.getElementById('modalExportPdf');
      if (existing) existing.remove();

      var today = new Date();
      var kelasSiswa = santri.kelas || '';
      var semesterDefault = 1;
      if (/XII/i.test(kelasSiswa)) semesterDefault = 5;
      else if (/XI/i.test(kelasSiswa)) semesterDefault = 3;

      var isGanjilDefault = (semesterDefault % 2 === 1);
      var hariDefault = isGanjilDefault ? 15 : 31;
      var bulanDefault = isGanjilDefault ? 'Juli' : 'Desember';

      var modalHtml =
        '<div id="modalExportPdf" class="fixed inset-0 z-[60] bg-slate-950/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">' +
          '<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-3xl w-full max-w-md shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">' +
            '<div class="px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between">' +
              '<h3 class="text-sm font-bold text-white font-heading flex items-center gap-2"><i data-lucide="file-pdf" class="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400"></i>Cetak Raport</h3>' +
              '<button onclick="closeModal(\'modalExportPdf\')" class="text-slate-400 hover:text-white"><i data-lucide="x" class="w-5 h-5"></i></button>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="p-6 space-y-4">' +
              '<div><label class="block text-slate-400 mb-1.5 font-semibold text-xs">Siswa:</label><div class="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs text-white"><div class="font-semibold">' + nama + '</div><div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">No Induk: ' + noInduk + ' | Kelas: ' + kelasSiswa + '</div></div></div>' +
              '<div><label class="block text-slate-400 mb-1.5 font-semibold text-xs">Pilih Semester:</label>' +
                '<select id="select-export-semester" onchange="updateTanggalOtomatis()" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs text-white">' +
                  '<option value="1" ' + (semesterDefault === 1 ? 'selected' : '') + '>Semester 1</option>' +
                  '<option value="2" ' + (semesterDefault === 2 ? 'selected' : '') + '>Semester 2</option>' +
                  '<option value="3" ' + (semesterDefault === 3 ? 'selected' : '') + '>Semester 3</option>' +
                  '<option value="4" ' + (semesterDefault === 4 ? 'selected' : '') + '>Semester 4</option>' +
                  '<option value="5" ' + (semesterDefault === 5 ? 'selected' : '') + '>Semester 5</option>' +
                  '<option value="6" ' + (semesterDefault === 6 ? 'selected' : '') + '>Semester 6</option>' +
                '</select></div>' +
              '<div><label class="block text-slate-400 mb-1.5 font-semibold text-xs">Tahun Pelajaran:</label><input type="text" id="export-tahun" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs text-white"></div>' +
              '<div><label class="block text-slate-400 mb-1.5 font-semibold text-xs">Tempat:</label><input type="text" id="export-tanggal-tempat" value="Kabupaten Pamekasan" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs text-white"></div>' +
              '<div><label class="block text-slate-400 mb-1.5 font-semibold text-xs">Tanggal:</label>' +
                '<div class="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2">' +
                  '<input type="text" id="export-tanggal-hari" value="' + hariDefault + '" class="col-span-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white text-center">' +
                  '<select id="export-tanggal-bulan" class="col-span-2 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white">' +
                    ['Januari','Februari','Maret','April','Mei','Juni','Juli','Agustus','September','Oktober','November','Desember'].map(function(b) {
                      return '<option value="' + b + '"' + (b === bulanDefault ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + b + '</option>';
                    }).join('') +
                  '</select>' +
                  '<input type="text" id="export-tanggal-tahun" value="' + today.getFullYear() + '" class="col-span-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white text-center">' +
                '</div></div>' +
              '<div class="flex gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-slate-800">' +
                '<button onclick="closeModal(\'modalExportPdf\')" class="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold">Batal</button>' +
                '<button onclick="prosesCetakRaportModal(\'' + noInduk + '\')" class="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold"><i data-lucide="printer" class="w-3.5 h-3.5 inline mr-1"></i>Cetak Raport</button>' +
              '</div>' +
            '</div>' +
          '</div>' +
        '</div>';

      document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', modalHtml);
      refreshIcons();
      setTimeout(function() {
        updateTanggalOtomatis();
      }, 50);
    }

    function prosesCetakRaportModal(noInduk) {
      var semester = document.getElementById('select-export-semester').value;
      var tahun = document.getElementById('export-tahun').value;
      var tempat = document.getElementById('export-tanggal-tempat').value;
      var hari = document.getElementById('export-tanggal-hari').value;
      var bulan = document.getElementById('export-tanggal-bulan').value;
      var tahunTgl = document.getElementById('export-tanggal-tahun').value;
      var tanggal = tempat + ', ' + hari + ' ' + bulan + ' ' + tahunTgl;

      closeModal('modalExportPdf');
      showBanner('⏳ Membuka raport...', 'info');

      getWebAppUrl().then(function(webAppUrl) {
        if (!webAppUrl) webAppUrl = window.location.origin + window.location.pathname;
        var params = new URLSearchParams({
          page: 'raport', noInduk: noInduk, semester: semester,
          tahunPelajaran: tahun, tanggalCetak: tanggal
        });
        window.open(webAppUrl + '?' + params.toString(), '_blank');
        showBanner('✅ Raport berhasil dibuka!', 'success');
      });
    }

    function updateTanggalOtomatis() {
      var semester = parseInt(document.getElementById('select-export-semester').value);
      var hariInput = document.getElementById('export-tanggal-hari');
      var bulanSelect = document.getElementById('export-tanggal-bulan');
      var tahunInput = document.getElementById('export-tahun');
      if (!hariInput || !bulanSelect) return;

      var isGanjil = (semester % 2 === 1);
      hariInput.value = isGanjil ? '15' : '31';
      bulanSelect.value = isGanjil ? 'Juli' : 'Desember';

      var tahunPelajaran = '';
      if (tahunInput && currentDetailNilaiData && currentDetailNilaiData.semesters) {
        var semData = currentDetailNilaiData.semesters[semester - 1];
        if (semData && semData.tahun && String(semData.tahun).trim() !== '') {
          tahunPelajaran = String(semData.tahun).trim();
          tahunInput.value = tahunPelajaran;
        }
      }

      var tahunTanggalInput = document.getElementById('export-tanggal-tahun');
      if (tahunTanggalInput && tahunPelajaran !== '') {
        var match = tahunPelajaran.match(/[\/\-](\d{4})/);
        if (match) {
          tahunTanggalInput.value = match[1];
        } else {
          var matchSingle = tahunPelajaran.match(/(\d{4})/);
          if (matchSingle) tahunTanggalInput.value = matchSingle[1];
        }
      }
    }

    function cetakIjazahModal() {
      if (!currentDetailNilaiData) { showBanner('Tidak ada data!', 'error'); return; }
      var noInduk = currentDetailNilaiData.noInduk;
      var nama = currentDetailNilaiData.nama;
      if (!noInduk) { showBanner('Data tidak lengkap!', 'error'); return; }

      var santri = appState.siswa.find(function(s) { return s.noInduk === noInduk; });
      var defaultTanggal = santri ? (santri.tgl_kelulusan || '') : '';
      if (!defaultTanggal) defaultTanggal = '31 Desember 2026';

      var modalHtml =
        '<div id="modal-ijazah-cetak" class="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-950/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">' +
          '<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-3xl w-full max-w-md shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">' +
            '<div class="px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between">' +
              '<h3 class="text-sm font-bold text-white font-heading flex items-center gap-2"><i data-lucide="scroll" class="w-4 h-4 text-blue-400"></i>Cetak Ijazah</h3>' +
              '<button onclick="closeModal(\'modal-ijazah-cetak\')" class="text-slate-400 hover:text-white"><i data-lucide="x" class="w-5 h-5"></i></button>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="p-6 space-y-4">' +
              '<div><label class="block text-slate-400 mb-1.5 font-semibold text-xs">Tanggal Kelulusan:</label>' +
                '<div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">' +
                  '<input type="text" id="cetak-ijazah-tanggal-hari" value="' + (defaultTanggal.split(' ')[0] || '31') + '" class="col-span-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white">' +
                  '<select id="cetak-ijazah-tanggal-bulan" class="col-span-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white">' +
                    ['Januari','Februari','Maret','April','Mei','Juni','Juli','Agustus','September','Oktober','November','Desember'].map(function(b) {
                      return '<option value="' + b + '"' + (b === (defaultTanggal.split(' ')[1] || 'Desember') ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + b + '</option>';
                    }).join('') +
                  '</select>' +
                  '<input type="text" id="cetak-ijazah-tanggal-tahun" value="' + (defaultTanggal.split(' ')[2] || '2026') + '" class="col-span-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white">' +
                '</div></div>' +
              '<div class="text-xs text-slate-400 bg-slate-950/60 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800"><p>📜 Cetak Ijazah untuk <strong class="text-white">' + nama + '</strong></p><p class="mt-1">Halaman ijazah akan dibuka di <strong class="text-emerald-400">tab baru</strong>.</p></div>' +
              '<button onclick="prosesCetakIjazahTabBaru()" class="w-full py-2.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-2"><i data-lucide="printer" class="w-4 h-4"></i>Cetak Ijazah</button>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="px-6 py-3 border-t border-slate-800 flex justify-end bg-slate-950">' +
              '<button onclick="closeModal(\'modal-ijazah-cetak\')" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 text-xs transition">Batal</button>' +
            '</div>' +
          '</div>' +
        '</div>';

      var existingModal = document.getElementById('modal-ijazah-cetak');
      if (existingModal) existingModal.remove();
      document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', modalHtml);
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function prosesCetakIjazahTabBaru() {
      var noInduk = currentDetailNilaiData && currentDetailNilaiData.noInduk;
      if (!noInduk) { showBanner('Data tidak lengkap!', 'error'); return; }
      var hari = document.getElementById('cetak-ijazah-tanggal-hari').value || '31';
      var bulan = document.getElementById('cetak-ijazah-tanggal-bulan').value || 'Desember';
      var tahun = document.getElementById('cetak-ijazah-tanggal-tahun').value || '2026';
      var tanggal = hari + ' ' + bulan + ' ' + tahun;
      closeModal('modal-ijazah-cetak');
      showBanner('⏳ Membuka ijazah...', 'info');
      getWebAppUrl().then(function(webAppUrl) {
        if (!webAppUrl) webAppUrl = window.location.origin + window.location.pathname;
        var params = new URLSearchParams({ page: 'ijazah', noInduk: noInduk, tanggalCetak: tanggal });
        window.open(webAppUrl + '?' + params.toString(), '_blank');
        showBanner('✅ Ijazah berhasil dibuka!', 'success');
      });
    }

    function exportRaportPDFLocked() {
      showBanner('🔒 Fitur Export Raport PDF sedang dikunci. Gunakan tombol "Cetak Raport".', 'warning');
    }

    function exportIjazahPDFLocked() {
      showBanner('🔒 Fitur Export Ijazah PDF sedang dikunci. Gunakan tombol "Cetak Ijazah".', 'warning');
    }


    /* =========================================================
       15. MODUL JADWAL (Iframe + postMessage)
       ========================================================= */

    function loadJadwalIframe() {
      var iframe = document.getElementById('iframeJadwal');
      if (!iframe) return;

      if (!window._jadwalLoaded) {
        if (typeof google === 'undefined' || !google.script || !google.script.run) {
          iframe.srcdoc = '<p style="color:orange; padding:20px;">Mode offline.</p>';
          return;
        }
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function(htmlContent) {
            if (!htmlContent) {
              iframe.srcdoc = '<p style="color:red; padding:20px;">Gagal memuat jadwal.</p>';
              return;
            }
            iframe.onload = function() {
              window._jadwalLoaded = true;
              setTimeout(function() { kirimDataKeIframeJadwal(iframe); }, 100);
            };
            iframe.srcdoc = htmlContent;
          })
          .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
            iframe.srcdoc = '<p style="color:red; padding:20px;">Error: ' + err.message + '</p>';
          })
          .getJadwalHTML();
        return;
      }
      kirimDataKeIframeJadwal(iframe);
    }

    function kirimDataKeIframeJadwal(iframe, _retry) {
      if (!iframe || !iframe.contentWindow) return;
      _retry = _retry || 0;

      var jadwal = window.appState ? (window.appState.jadwal || []) : [];
      var guru   = window.appState ? (window.appState.guru   || []) : [];

      if ((jadwal.length === 0 || guru.length === 0) && _retry < 20) {
        setTimeout(function() { kirimDataKeIframeJadwal(iframe, _retry + 1); }, 500);
        return;
      }

      try {
        iframe.contentWindow.postMessage({
          type: 'UPDATE_JADWAL_DATA', jadwal: jadwal, guru: guru
        }, '*');
      } catch (e) { console.error('Gagal postMessage:', e); }
    }

    function cetakJadwalLangsung() {
      var iframe = document.getElementById('iframeJadwal');
      if (!iframe) { showBanner('Jadwal belum dimuat!', 'error'); return; }
      try {
        iframe.contentWindow.focus();
        iframe.contentWindow.print();
      } catch (e) { showBanner('Gagal print: ' + e.message, 'error'); }
    }


    /* =========================================================
       16. MODUL PKKS
       ========================================================= */

    var _pkksFolderData = [];
    var _pkksCurrentFolder = null;
    var _pkksFileData = [];
    var _pkksUploadMode = 'file';
    var _pkksFolderLoaded = false;
    var _pkksCurrentFolderLoaded = false;
    var _pkksFileCache = {};
     var _pkksFolderCache = {};
    var _pkksFolderPath = [];
    var _pkksViewMode = 'card';

    /* ----- HALAMAN 1: MENU FOLDER ----- */

    function muatDaftarFolderPKKS() {
      var grid = document.getElementById('pkks-folder-grid');
      if (!grid) return;
      grid.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-full text-center py-8 text-slate-500">Memuat folder...</div>';

      if (typeof google === 'undefined' || !google.script || !google.script.run) {
        grid.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-full text-center py-8 text-slate-500">Mode offline</div>';
        return;
      }

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            _pkksFolderData = res.data || [];
            _pkksFolderLoaded = true;
            updateViewToggleButtons();
            renderFolderPKKSGrid();
          } else {
            grid.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-full text-center py-8 text-red-400">' + (res.message || 'Gagal memuat') + '</div>';
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          grid.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-full text-center py-8 text-red-400">Error: ' + (err.message || err) + '</div>';
        })
        .getDaftarFolderPKKS();
    }

    function renderFolderPKKSGrid() {
      var grid = document.getElementById('pkks-folder-grid');
      if (!grid) return;

      var search = (document.getElementById('pkks-folder-search') && document.getElementById('pkks-folder-search').value || '').toLowerCase().trim();

      var filtered = _pkksFolderData;
      if (search) {
        filtered = filtered.filter(function(f) {
          return (f.nama || '').toLowerCase().includes(search);
        });
      }

      var totalEl = document.getElementById('pkks-folder-total');
      if (totalEl) totalEl.textContent = filtered.length;

      if (filtered.length === 0) {
        grid.className = 'grid';
        grid.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-12 text-slate-500">' +
          '<i data-lucide="folder-open" class="w-12 h-12 mx-auto mb-3 opacity-40"></i>' +
          '<p class="text-sm">Belum ada folder. Klik <b>Folder Baru</b> untuk membuat.</p>' +
        '</div>';
        refreshIcons();
        return;
      }

      if (_pkksViewMode === 'table') {
        grid.className = 'overflow-x-auto';
        grid.innerHTML = '<table class="w-full text-left text-xs text-slate-300">' +
          '<thead class="bg-slate-950 text-slate-400 font-semibold text-[11px] border-b border-slate-800">' +
            '<tr><th class="px-3 py-2 w-10"></th><th class="px-3 py-2">Nama Folder</th><th class="px-3 py-2 w-40">Tanggal Dibuat</th><th class="px-3 py-2 text-center w-24">Aksi</th></tr>' +
          '</thead><tbody class="divide-y divide-slate-800">' +
          filtered.map(function(f) {
            var namaEsc = (f.nama || '').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
            return '<tr class="hover:bg-slate-800/40 cursor-pointer" onclick="bukaFolderPKKS(\'' + f.id + '\', \'' + namaEsc + '\')">' +
              '<td class="px-3 py-2 text-amber-400"><i data-lucide="folder" class="w-4 h-4"></i></td>' +
              '<td class="px-3 py-2 font-semibold text-white">' + (f.nama || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-3 py-2 text-slate-500">' + (f.tanggal || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-3 py-2 text-center text-slate-500"><i data-lucide="chevron-right" class="w-4 h-4 inline"></i></td>' +
            '</tr>';
          }).join('') +
          '</tbody></table>';
      } else {
        grid.className = 'grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 2xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4';
        grid.innerHTML = filtered.map(function(f) {
          var namaEsc = (f.nama || '').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
          return '<div onclick="bukaFolderPKKS(\'' + f.id + '\', \'' + namaEsc + '\')" class="cursor-pointer bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 hover:border-amber-500/60 rounded-2xl p-4 transition shadow-md hover:shadow-lg group flex flex-col items-center text-center">' +
            '<div class="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center mb-3 group-hover:scale-110 transition">' +
              '<i data-lucide="folder" class="w-8 h-8"></i>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="text-xs font-bold text-white truncate w-full" title="' + namaEsc + '">' + (f.nama || '-') + '</div>' +
            '<div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1">' + (f.tanggal || '') + '</div>' +
          '</div>';
        }).join('');
      }

      refreshIcons();
    }

    function bukaFolderPKKS(folderId, namaFolder, dariBack) {
      if (!folderId) return;

      if (!dariBack) {
        var top = _pkksFolderPath[_pkksFolderPath.length - 1];
        if (!top || top.id !== folderId) {
          _pkksFolderPath.push({ id: folderId, nama: namaFolder });
        }
      }

      _pkksCurrentFolder = { id: folderId, nama: namaFolder };
      _pkksCurrentFolderLoaded = !!_pkksFileCache[folderId];

      document.getElementById('pkks-menu-view').classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById('pkks-folder-view').classList.remove('hidden');

      document.getElementById('pkks-folder-title').textContent = namaFolder || 'Folder';

      var sel = document.getElementById('pkks-folder-tujuan');
      if (sel) sel.value = folderId;

      renderBreadcrumbPKKS();
      muatIsiFolderPKKS();
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function kembaliKeMenuPKKS() {
      if (_pkksFolderPath.length > 1) {
        _pkksFolderPath.pop();
        var parent = _pkksFolderPath[_pkksFolderPath.length - 1];

        _pkksCurrentFolder = { id: parent.id, nama: parent.nama };
        _pkksCurrentFolderLoaded = !!_pkksFileCache[parent.id];

        document.getElementById('pkks-menu-view').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('pkks-folder-view').classList.remove('hidden');
        document.getElementById('pkks-folder-title').textContent = parent.nama || 'Folder';

        var sel = document.getElementById('pkks-folder-tujuan');
        if (sel) sel.value = parent.id;

        renderBreadcrumbPKKS();
        muatIsiFolderPKKS();
        refreshIcons();
        return;
      }

      _pkksFolderPath = [];
      _pkksCurrentFolder = null;
      _pkksCurrentFolderLoaded = false;

      document.getElementById('pkks-menu-view').classList.remove('hidden');
      document.getElementById('pkks-folder-view').classList.add('hidden');
      renderFolderPKKSGrid();
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function hapusFolderPKKSKonfirmasi() {
      if (!_pkksCurrentFolder) return;
      var nama = _pkksCurrentFolder.nama || '';
      var folderId = _pkksCurrentFolder.id;
      if (!confirm('Hapus folder "' + nama + '" beserta semua isinya?\n\nFolder akan dipindah ke Trash Drive.')) return;

      var diDalamFolder = (_pkksFolderPath.length > 1);

      if (diDalamFolder) {
        _pkksFolderPath.pop();
        var parent = _pkksFolderPath[_pkksFolderPath.length - 1];
        _pkksCurrentFolder = { id: parent.id, nama: parent.nama };

        if (_pkksFolderCache[parent.id]) {
          _pkksFolderCache[parent.id] = _pkksFolderCache[parent.id].filter(function(f) {
            return f.id !== folderId;
          });
          _pkksSubfolderData = _pkksFolderCache[parent.id];
        }

        document.getElementById('pkks-folder-title').textContent = parent.nama || 'Folder';
        renderBreadcrumbPKKS();
        renderSubfolderPKKSGrid();
        renderFilePKKSGrid();
      } else {
        _pkksFolderPath = [];
        _pkksCurrentFolder = null;
        _pkksFolderData = _pkksFolderData.filter(function(f) { return f.id !== folderId; });

        document.getElementById('pkks-menu-view').classList.remove('hidden');
        document.getElementById('pkks-folder-view').classList.add('hidden');
        renderFolderPKKSGrid();
      }

      showBanner('⏳ Menghapus folder ' + nama + '...', 'info');

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            showBanner('✅ ' + res.message, 'success');
            delete _pkksFileCache[folderId];
            delete _pkksFolderCache[folderId];
          } else {
            showBanner('❌ ' + (res.message || 'Gagal hapus folder'), 'error');
            _pkksFolderLoaded = false;
            if (diDalamFolder) {
              _pkksCurrentFolderLoaded = false;
              muatIsiFolderPKKS();
            } else {
              muatDaftarFolderPKKS();
            }
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          showBanner('❌ Error: ' + (err.message || err), 'error');
          _pkksFolderLoaded = false;
          if (diDalamFolder) {
            _pkksCurrentFolderLoaded = false;
            muatIsiFolderPKKS();
          } else {
            muatDaftarFolderPKKS();
          }
        })
        .hapusFolderPKKS(folderId);
    }

    function renderBreadcrumbPKKS() {
      var el = document.getElementById('pkks-breadcrumb');
      if (!el) return;

      var html = '<span class="hover:text-emerald-400 cursor-pointer" onclick="kembaliKeMenuPKKS()">PKKS</span>';

      for (var i = 0; i < _pkksFolderPath.length; i++) {
        var f = _pkksFolderPath[i];
        var isLast = (i === _pkksFolderPath.length - 1);

        html += '<i data-lucide="chevron-right" class="w-3 h-3"></i>';

        if (isLast) {
          html += '<span class="text-slate-200 font-semibold truncate">' + (f.nama || 'Folder') + '</span>';
        } else {
          html += '<span class="hover:text-emerald-400 cursor-pointer" onclick="bukaFolderDariBreadcrumb(' + i + ')">' + (f.nama || 'Folder') + '</span>';
        }
      }

      el.innerHTML = html;
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function bukaFolderDariBreadcrumb(index) {
      if (index < 0 || index >= _pkksFolderPath.length) return;

      _pkksFolderPath = _pkksFolderPath.slice(0, index + 1);

      var target = _pkksFolderPath[index];
      _pkksCurrentFolder = { id: target.id, nama: target.nama };
      _pkksCurrentFolderLoaded = !!_pkksFileCache[target.id];

      document.getElementById('pkks-folder-title').textContent = target.nama || 'Folder';

      renderBreadcrumbPKKS();
      muatIsiFolderPKKS();
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function toggleViewPKKS() {
      _pkksViewMode = (_pkksViewMode === 'card') ? 'table' : 'card';
      updateViewToggleButtons();
      renderFolderPKKSGrid();
      renderSubfolderPKKSGrid();
      renderFilePKKSGrid();
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function updateViewToggleButtons() {
      var icons = document.querySelectorAll('.pkks-view-icon');
      var labels = document.querySelectorAll('.pkks-view-label');
      icons.forEach(function(el) { el.setAttribute('data-lucide', _pkksViewMode === 'card' ? 'layout-grid' : 'list'); });
      labels.forEach(function(el) { el.textContent = _pkksViewMode === 'card' ? 'Tabel' : 'Card'; });
    }

    function resetViewModePKKS() {
      _pkksViewMode = 'card';
      var icon = document.querySelector('#pkks-view-toggle i');
      var label = document.getElementById('pkks-view-label');
      if (icon) icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'layout-grid');
      if (label) label.textContent = 'Tabel';
    }

    function formatFileIconPKKS(nama) {
      var ext = (nama || '').split('.').pop().toLowerCase();
      if (ext === 'pdf') return { name: 'file-text', color: 'text-red-400' };
      if (['doc','docx'].includes(ext)) return { name: 'file-text', color: 'text-blue-400' };
      if (['xls','xlsx'].includes(ext)) return { name: 'file-spreadsheet', color: 'text-emerald-400' };
      if (['ppt','pptx'].includes(ext)) return { name: 'presentation', color: 'text-orange-400' };
      if (['jpg','jpeg','png','gif','webp'].includes(ext)) return { name: 'image', color: 'text-purple-400' };
      if (['zip','rar'].includes(ext)) return { name: 'file-archive', color: 'text-amber-400' };
      return { name: 'file', color: 'text-slate-400' };
    }

    /* ----- HALAMAN 2: DETAIL FOLDER ----- */

    function muatIsiFolderPKKS() {
      if (!_pkksCurrentFolder) return;

      var folderId = _pkksCurrentFolder.id;

      // Kalau ada di cache, langsung render
      if (_pkksFileCache[folderId] && _pkksFolderCache[folderId]) {
        _pkksFileData = _pkksFileCache[folderId];
        _pkksSubfolderData = _pkksFolderCache[folderId];
        _pkksCurrentFolderLoaded = true;
        renderSubfolderPKKSGrid();
        renderFilePKKSGrid();
        return;
      }

      // Loading state
      var fileGrid = document.getElementById('pkks-file-grid');
      var subfolderGrid = document.getElementById('pkks-subfolder-grid');
      if (fileGrid) fileGrid.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-full text-center py-8 text-slate-500">Memuat file...</div>';
      if (subfolderGrid) subfolderGrid.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-full text-center py-8 text-slate-500">Memuat subfolder...</div>';

      if (typeof google === 'undefined' || !google.script || !google.script.run) return;

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            _pkksFileData = res.files || [];
            _pkksSubfolderData = res.folders || [];
            _pkksFileCache[folderId] = _pkksFileData;
            _pkksFolderCache[folderId] = _pkksSubfolderData;
            _pkksCurrentFolderLoaded = true;

            // Update breadcrumb — parent info
            if (res.folder && res.folder.parent && !res.folder.parent.isRoot) {
              // Ada parent di luar root, tapi kita belum handle multi-level breadcrumb.
              // Minimal: update nama folder yang sedang aktif
            }

            renderSubfolderPKKSGrid();
            renderFilePKKSGrid();
          } else {
            if (fileGrid) fileGrid.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-full text-center py-8 text-red-400">' + (res.message || 'Gagal memuat') + '</div>';
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          if (fileGrid) fileGrid.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-full text-center py-8 text-red-400">Error: ' + (err.message || err) + '</div>';
        })
        .getIsiFolderPKKS(folderId);
    }

    function renderSubfolderPKKSGrid() {
      var grid = document.getElementById('pkks-subfolder-grid');
      var section = document.getElementById('pkks-subfolder-section');
      var countEl = document.getElementById('pkks-subfolder-count');
      if (!grid || !section) return;

      var total = _pkksSubfolderData.length;
      if (countEl) countEl.textContent = total;

      if (total === 0) {
        section.classList.add('hidden');
        return;
      }

      section.classList.remove('hidden');

      if (_pkksViewMode === 'table') {
        grid.className = 'overflow-x-auto';
        grid.innerHTML = '<table class="w-full text-left text-xs text-slate-300">' +
          '<thead class="bg-slate-950 text-slate-400 font-semibold text-[11px] border-b border-slate-800">' +
            '<tr><th class="px-3 py-2 w-10"></th><th class="px-3 py-2">Nama</th><th class="px-3 py-2 w-40">Tanggal Dibuat</th><th class="px-3 py-2 text-center w-24">Aksi</th></tr>' +
          '</thead><tbody class="divide-y divide-slate-800">' +
          _pkksSubfolderData.map(function(f) {
            var namaEsc = (f.nama || '').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
            return '<tr class="hover:bg-slate-800/40 cursor-pointer" onclick="bukaFolderPKKS(\'' + f.id + '\', \'' + namaEsc + '\')">' +
              '<td class="px-3 py-2 text-amber-400"><i data-lucide="folder" class="w-4 h-4"></i></td>' +
              '<td class="px-3 py-2 font-semibold text-white">' + (f.nama || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-3 py-2 text-slate-500">' + (f.tanggal || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-3 py-2 text-center text-slate-500"><i data-lucide="chevron-right" class="w-4 h-4 inline"></i></td>' +
            '</tr>';
          }).join('') +
          '</tbody></table>';
      } else {
        grid.className = 'grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 2xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4';
        grid.innerHTML = _pkksSubfolderData.map(function(f) {
          var namaEsc = (f.nama || '').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
          return '<div onclick="bukaFolderPKKS(\'' + f.id + '\', \'' + namaEsc + '\')" class="cursor-pointer bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 hover:border-amber-500/60 rounded-2xl p-4 transition shadow-md hover:shadow-lg group flex flex-col items-center text-center">' +
            '<div class="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center mb-3 group-hover:scale-110 transition">' +
              '<i data-lucide="folder" class="w-8 h-8"></i>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="text-xs font-bold text-white truncate w-full" title="' + namaEsc + '">' + (f.nama || '-') + '</div>' +
            '<div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1">' + (f.tanggal || '') + '</div>' +
          '</div>';
        }).join('');
      }

      refreshIcons();
    }
