    function renderFilePKKSGrid() {
      var grid = document.getElementById('pkks-file-grid');
      if (!grid) return;

      var search = (document.getElementById('pkks-file-search') && document.getElementById('pkks-file-search').value || '').toLowerCase().trim();

      var filtered = _pkksFileData;
      if (search) {
        filtered = filtered.filter(function(f) {
          return (f.nama || '').toLowerCase().includes(search);
        });
      }

      var totalEl = document.getElementById('pkks-file-total');
      if (totalEl) totalEl.textContent = filtered.length;
      var countEl = document.getElementById('pkks-file-count');
      if (countEl) countEl.textContent = filtered.length;

      if (filtered.length === 0) {
        grid.className = 'grid';
        grid.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-12 text-slate-500">' +
          '<i data-lucide="file-x" class="w-12 h-12 mx-auto mb-3 opacity-40"></i>' +
          '<p class="text-sm">Folder ini kosong. Klik <b>Upload</b> untuk menambah file.</p>' +
        '</div>';
        refreshIcons();
        return;
      }

      if (_pkksViewMode === 'table') {
        grid.className = 'overflow-x-auto';
        grid.innerHTML = '<table class="w-full text-left text-xs text-slate-300">' +
          '<thead class="bg-slate-950 text-slate-400 font-semibold text-[11px] border-b border-slate-800">' +
            '<tr><th class="px-3 py-2 w-10"></th><th class="px-3 py-2">Nama File</th><th class="px-3 py-2 w-24">Ukuran</th><th class="px-3 py-2 w-40">Tanggal</th><th class="px-3 py-2 text-center w-48">Aksi</th></tr>' +
          '</thead><tbody class="divide-y divide-slate-800">' +
          filtered.map(function(f) {
            var namaEsc = (f.nama || '').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
            var icon = formatFileIconPKKS(f.nama);
            return '<tr class="hover:bg-slate-800/40">' +
              '<td class="px-3 py-2 ' + icon.color + '"><i data-lucide="' + icon.name + '" class="w-4 h-4"></i></td>' +
              '<td class="px-3 py-2 font-semibold text-white">' + (f.nama || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-3 py-2 text-slate-400">' + (f.ukuran || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-3 py-2 text-slate-500">' + (f.tanggal || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="px-3 py-2 text-center"><div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1">' +
                '<a href="' + f.url + '" target="_blank" class="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-blue-400 text-[10px] font-semibold" title="Buka">Buka</a>' +
                '<button onclick="downloadFilePKKSById(\'' + f.id + '\', \'' + namaEsc + '\')" class="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-emerald-400 text-[10px] font-semibold" title="Download">Unduh</button>' +
                '<button onclick="bukaModalPindahFile(\'' + f.id + '\', \'' + namaEsc + '\')" class="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-amber-400 text-[10px] font-semibold" title="Pindah">Pindah</button>' +
                '<button onclick="hapusFilePKKSById(\'' + f.id + '\', \'' + namaEsc + '\')" class="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-red-950 text-red-400 text-[10px] font-semibold" title="Hapus">Hapus</button>' +
              '</div></td>' +
            '</tr>';
          }).join('') +
          '</tbody></table>';
      } else {
        grid.className = 'grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 2xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4';
        grid.innerHTML = filtered.map(function(f) {
          var namaEsc = (f.nama || '').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
          var icon = formatFileIconPKKS(f.nama);
          return '<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 hover:border-emerald-500/60 rounded-2xl p-4 transition shadow-md group flex flex-col items-center text-center">' +
            '<div class="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/60 ' + icon.color + ' flex items-center justify-center mb-3 group-hover:scale-105 transition">' +
              '<i data-lucide="' + icon.name + '" class="w-8 h-8"></i>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="text-xs font-bold text-white truncate w-full mb-1" title="' + namaEsc + '">' + (f.nama || '-') + '</div>' +
            '<div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mb-3">' + (f.ukuran || '') + ' • ' + (f.tanggal || '') + '</div>' +
            '<div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1 w-full">' +
              '<a href="' + f.url + '" target="_blank" class="flex-1 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-blue-400 text-[10px] font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1" title="Buka">' +
                '<i data-lucide="external-link" class="w-3 h-3"></i>Buka' +
              '</a>' +
              '<button onclick="downloadFilePKKSById(\'' + f.id + '\', \'' + namaEsc + '\')" class="flex-1 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-emerald-400 text-[10px] font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1" title="Download">' +
                '<i data-lucide="download" class="w-3 h-3"></i>Unduh' +
              '</button>' +
              '<button onclick="bukaModalPindahFile(\'' + f.id + '\', \'' + namaEsc + '\')" class="py-1.5 px-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-amber-400 text-[10px] font-semibold flex items-center justify-center" title="Pindah ke Folder Lain">' +
                '<i data-lucide="folder-symlink" class="w-3 h-3"></i>' +
              '</button>' +
              '<button onclick="hapusFilePKKSById(\'' + f.id + '\', \'' + namaEsc + '\')" class="py-1.5 px-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-red-950 text-red-400 text-[10px] font-semibold flex items-center justify-center" title="Hapus">' +
                '<i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-3 h-3"></i>' +
              '</button>' +
            '</div>' +
          '</div>';
        }).join('');
      }

      refreshIcons();
    }

    /* ----- BUAT FOLDER BARU ----- */

    function bukaModalBuatFolder() {
      document.getElementById('pkks-nama-folder-baru').value = '';
      openModal('modalBuatFolderPKKS');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function simpanFolderPKKSBaru() {
      var nama = document.getElementById('pkks-nama-folder-baru').value.trim();
      if (!nama) { showBanner('Nama folder harus diisi!', 'error'); return; }

      // ★ Kalau sedang di dalam folder → parent = folder aktif
      //   Kalau di menu utama → parent = root PKKS
      var parentFolderId = _pkksCurrentFolder ? _pkksCurrentFolder.id : '';

      showBanner('⏳ Membuat folder...', 'info');

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            showBanner('✅ ' + res.message, 'success');
            closeModal('modalBuatFolderPKKS');

            if (_pkksCurrentFolder) {
              // Refresh isi folder yang sedang dibuka
              delete _pkksFolderCache[_pkksCurrentFolder.id];
              _pkksCurrentFolderLoaded = false;
              muatIsiFolderPKKS();
            } else {
              // Refresh menu folder utama
              _pkksFolderLoaded = false;
              muatDaftarFolderPKKS();
            }
          } else {
            showBanner('❌ ' + (res.message || 'Gagal buat folder'), 'error');
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          showBanner('❌ Error: ' + (err.message || err), 'error');
        })
        .buatFolderPKKS(nama, parentFolderId);
    }

    /* ----- RENAME FOLDER ----- */

    function bukaModalRenameFolder() {
      if (!_pkksCurrentFolder) {
        showBanner('Tidak ada folder aktif!', 'error');
        return;
      }

      document.getElementById('pkks-nama-folder-rename').value = _pkksCurrentFolder.nama || '';
      openModal('modalRenameFolderPKKS');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function simpanRenameFolderPKKS() {
      if (!_pkksCurrentFolder) return;

      var namaBaru = document.getElementById('pkks-nama-folder-rename').value.trim();
      if (!namaBaru) { showBanner('Nama folder harus diisi!', 'error'); return; }

      var folderId = _pkksCurrentFolder.id;
      var namaLama = _pkksCurrentFolder.nama;

      if (namaBaru === namaLama) {
        closeModal('modalRenameFolderPKKS');
        return;
      }

      showBanner('⏳ Mengubah nama folder...', 'info');

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            showBanner('✅ ' + res.message, 'success');
            closeModal('modalRenameFolderPKKS');

            _pkksCurrentFolder.nama = namaBaru;

            _pkksFolderData.forEach(function(f) {
              if (f.id === folderId) f.nama = namaBaru;
            });

            document.getElementById('pkks-breadcrumb-folder').textContent = namaBaru;
            document.getElementById('pkks-folder-title').textContent = namaBaru;

            loadFolderTujuanDropdown();
          } else {
            showBanner('❌ ' + (res.message || 'Gagal ubah nama folder'), 'error');
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          showBanner('❌ Error: ' + (err.message || err), 'error');
        })
        .renameFolderPKKS(folderId, namaBaru);
    }

    /* ----- MODAL UPLOAD ----- */

    function bukaModalUploadPKKS() {
      document.getElementById('pkks-multi-file').value = '';
      document.getElementById('pkks-folder-input').value = '';
      document.getElementById('pkks-file-preview').classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById('pkks-folder-preview').classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById('pkks-jenis').value = 'Lainnya';
      document.getElementById('pkks-keterangan').value = '';
      document.getElementById('pkks-progress').classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById('pkks-progress-bar').style.width = '0%';
      document.getElementById('pkks-progress-count').textContent = '0/0';
      document.getElementById('pkks-btn-upload').disabled = false;

      loadFolderTujuanDropdown();

      switchUploadMode('file');
      openModal('modalUploadPKKS');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function loadFolderTujuanDropdown() {
      var sel = document.getElementById('pkks-folder-tujuan');
      if (!sel) return;

      sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Root PKKS (tanpa folder) --</option>';

      if (_pkksCurrentFolder) {
        var opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = _pkksCurrentFolder.id;
        opt.textContent = '📁 ' + _pkksCurrentFolder.nama;
        sel.appendChild(opt);
        sel.value = _pkksCurrentFolder.id;
      }

      _pkksFolderData.forEach(function(f) {
        if (_pkksCurrentFolder && f.id === _pkksCurrentFolder.id) return;
        var opt2 = document.createElement('option');
        opt2.value = f.id;
        opt2.textContent = '📁 ' + f.nama;
        sel.appendChild(opt2);
      });
    }

    function switchUploadMode(mode) {
      _pkksUploadMode = mode;

      var btnFile = document.getElementById('btn-upload-file');
      var btnFolder = document.getElementById('btn-upload-folder');
      var modeFile = document.getElementById('upload-mode-file');
      var modeFolder = document.getElementById('upload-mode-folder');

      if (mode === 'file') {
        btnFile.classList.add('text-emerald-400', 'bg-emerald-500/10');
        btnFile.classList.remove('text-slate-400');
        btnFolder.classList.remove('text-blue-400', 'bg-blue-500/10');
        btnFolder.classList.add('text-slate-400');
        modeFile.classList.remove('hidden');
        modeFolder.classList.add('hidden');
      } else {
        btnFolder.classList.add('text-blue-400', 'bg-blue-500/10');
        btnFolder.classList.remove('text-slate-400');
        btnFile.classList.remove('text-emerald-400', 'bg-emerald-500/10');
        btnFile.classList.add('text-slate-400');
        modeFile.classList.add('hidden');
        modeFolder.classList.remove('hidden');
      }
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function formatBytesJS(bytes) {
      if (bytes < 1024) return bytes + ' B';
      if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return (bytes / 1024).toFixed(1) + ' KB';
      return (bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2) + ' MB';
    }

    /* ----- PROSES UPLOAD ----- */

    function prosesUploadPKKS() {
      if (_pkksUploadMode === 'file') {
        prosesUploadMultiFile();
      } else {
        prosesUploadFolder();
      }
    }

    function prosesUploadMultiFile() {
      var input = document.getElementById('pkks-multi-file');
      var files = Array.from(input.files || []);
      if (files.length === 0) { showBanner('Pilih file terlebih dahulu!', 'error'); return; }

      var folderId = document.getElementById('pkks-folder-tujuan').value || '';
      var jenis = document.getElementById('pkks-jenis').value || 'Lainnya';
      var keterangan = document.getElementById('pkks-keterangan').value.trim();

      uploadFileSequential(files, folderId, jenis, keterangan);
    }

    function prosesUploadFolder() {
      var input = document.getElementById('pkks-folder-input');
      var files = Array.from(input.files || []);
      if (files.length === 0) { showBanner('Pilih folder terlebih dahulu!', 'error'); return; }

      var path = files[0].webkitRelativePath || '';
      var folderName = path.split('/')[0] || 'Folder Baru';

      var parentFolderId = document.getElementById('pkks-folder-tujuan').value || '';
      var jenis = document.getElementById('pkks-jenis').value || 'Lainnya';
      var keterangan = document.getElementById('pkks-keterangan').value.trim();

      showBanner('⏳ Membuat folder "' + folderName + '"...', 'info');

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (res && res.status === 'success' && res.data) {
            var newFolderId = res.data.id;
            showBanner('✅ Folder dibuat. Mengupload file...', 'success');
            uploadFileSequential(files, newFolderId, jenis, keterangan, folderName);
          } else {
            showBanner('❌ Gagal buat folder: ' + (res.message || 'Unknown'), 'error');
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          showBanner('❌ Error buat folder: ' + (err.message || err), 'error');
        })
        .buatFolderPKKS(folderName, parentFolderId);
    }

    function uploadFileSequential(files, folderId, jenis, keterangan, folderName) {
      var total = files.length;
      var sukses = 0;
      var gagal = 0;
      var gagalList = [];

      document.getElementById('pkks-progress').classList.remove('hidden');
      document.getElementById('pkks-progress-bar').style.width = '0%';
      document.getElementById('pkks-progress-label').textContent = 'Mengupload...';
      document.getElementById('pkks-progress-count').textContent = '0/' + total;
      document.getElementById('pkks-btn-upload').disabled = true;

      var index = 0;

      function uploadNext() {
        if (index >= total) {
          document.getElementById('pkks-progress-bar').style.width = '100%';
          document.getElementById('pkks-progress-label').textContent = 'Selesai!';
          document.getElementById('pkks-btn-upload').disabled = false;

          var msg = '✅ ' + sukses + ' berhasil, ' + gagal + ' gagal.';
          if (gagal > 0) {
            showBanner(msg + ' File gagal: ' + gagalList.slice(0, 3).join(', ') + (gagalList.length > 3 ? '...' : ''), 'error');
          } else {
            showBanner(msg, 'success');
          }

          setTimeout(function() {
            closeModal('modalUploadPKKS');
            if (_pkksCurrentFolder) {
              delete _pkksFileCache[_pkksCurrentFolder.id];
              delete _pkksFolderCache[_pkksCurrentFolder.id];
              _pkksCurrentFolderLoaded = false;
              muatIsiFolderPKKS();
            } else {
              _pkksFolderLoaded = false;
              muatDaftarFolderPKKS();
            }
          }, 1000);
          return;
        }

        var file = files[index];
        var reader = new FileReader();

        reader.onload = function(e) {
          var base64 = e.target.result.split(',')[1];
          var mimeType = file.type || 'application/octet-stream';

          google.script.run
            .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
              if (res && res.status === 'success') {
                sukses++;
              } else {
                gagal++;
                gagalList.push(file.name);
              }

              index++;
              var percent = Math.round((index / total) * 100);
              document.getElementById('pkks-progress-bar').style.width = percent + '%';
              document.getElementById('pkks-progress-count').textContent = index + '/' + total;
              document.getElementById('pkks-progress-label').textContent = 'Mengupload: ' + file.name;

              uploadNext();
            })
            .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
              gagal++;
              gagalList.push(file.name);
              index++;
              var percent = Math.round((index / total) * 100);
              document.getElementById('pkks-progress-bar').style.width = percent + '%';
              document.getElementById('pkks-progress-count').textContent = index + '/' + total;
              uploadNext();
            })
            .uploadFilePKKS({
              nama: file.name,
              mimeType: mimeType,
              base64: base64,
              jenis: jenis,
              keterangan: keterangan,
              folderId: folderId,
              uploader: currentUser ? (currentUser.name || currentUser.username) : 'admin'
            });
        };

        reader.onerror = function() {
          gagal++;
          gagalList.push(file.name);
          index++;
          uploadNext();
        };

        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
      }

      uploadNext();
    }

    /* ----- DOWNLOAD / PINDAH / HAPUS FILE ----- */

    function downloadFilePKKSById(fileId, namaFile) {
      if (!fileId) return;
      showBanner('⏳ Mengunduh ' + namaFile + '...', 'info');
      var url = 'https://drive.google.com/uc?export=download&id=' + fileId;

      fetch(url)
        .then(function(response) {
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + response.status);
          return response.blob();
        })
        .then(function(blob) {
          var urlBlob = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
          var a = document.createElement('a');
          a.href = urlBlob; a.download = namaFile;
          document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); document.body.removeChild(a);
          setTimeout(function() { window.URL.revokeObjectURL(urlBlob); }, 1000);
          showBanner('✅ File berhasil diunduh.', 'success');
        })
        .catch(function(err) {
          showBanner('⚠️ Mengunduh via tab baru...', 'info');
          window.open(url, '_blank');
        });
    }

    function bukaModalPindahFile(fileId, namaFile) {
      if (!fileId) return;

      _pindahFileId = fileId;

      document.getElementById('pindah-file-nama').textContent = namaFile || '-';

      var sel = document.getElementById('pindah-folder-tujuan');
      sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Root PKKS (tanpa folder) --</option>';

      (_pkksFolderData || []).forEach(function(f) {
        if (_pkksCurrentFolder && f.id === _pkksCurrentFolder.id) return;
        var opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = f.id;
        opt.textContent = '📁 ' + f.nama;
        sel.appendChild(opt);
      });

      openModal('modalPindahFilePKKS');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function prosesPindahFilePKKS() {
      if (!_pindahFileId) return;

      var targetFolderId = document.getElementById('pindah-folder-tujuan').value || '';
      var fileId = _pindahFileId;

      closeModal('modalPindahFilePKKS');
      showBanner('⏳ Memindah file...', 'info');

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            showBanner('✅ ' + res.message, 'success');

            if (_pkksCurrentFolder) {
              _pkksFileData = _pkksFileData.filter(function(f) { return f.id !== fileId; });
              _pkksFileCache[_pkksCurrentFolder.id] = _pkksFileData;
              renderFilePKKSGrid();
            }

            if (targetFolderId && _pkksFileCache[targetFolderId]) {
              delete _pkksFileCache[targetFolderId];
            }
          } else {
            showBanner('❌ ' + (res.message || 'Gagal pindah file'), 'error');
          }
          _pindahFileId = null;
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          showBanner('❌ Error: ' + (err.message || err), 'error');
          _pindahFileId = null;
        })
        .pindahFilePKKS(fileId, targetFolderId);
    }

    function hapusFilePKKSById(fileId, namaFile) {
      if (!fileId) return;
      if (!confirm('Hapus file "' + namaFile + '"?\n\nFile akan dipindah ke Trash Drive.')) return;

      _pkksFileData = _pkksFileData.filter(function(f) { return f.id !== fileId; });
      if (_pkksCurrentFolder) {
        _pkksFileCache[_pkksCurrentFolder.id] = _pkksFileData;
      }
      renderFilePKKSGrid();

      showBanner('⏳ Menghapus ' + namaFile + '...', 'info');

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            showBanner('✅ ' + namaFile + ' dihapus.', 'success');
          } else {
            showBanner('❌ Gagal hapus: ' + (res.message || 'Unknown'), 'error');
            _pkksCurrentFolderLoaded = false;
            muatIsiFolderPKKS();
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          showBanner('❌ Error: ' + (err.message || err), 'error');
          _pkksCurrentFolderLoaded = false;
          muatIsiFolderPKKS();
        })
        .hapusFilePKKSById(fileId);
    }

    function refreshFileDalamFolderPKKS() {
      if (!_pkksCurrentFolder) return;
      delete _pkksFileCache[_pkksCurrentFolder.id];
      delete _pkksFolderCache[_pkksCurrentFolder.id];
      _pkksCurrentFolderLoaded = false;
      muatIsiFolderPKKS();
    }

    function bukaModalBuatSubfolder() {
      if (!_pkksCurrentFolder) {
        showBanner('Tidak ada folder aktif!', 'error');
        return;
      }
      document.getElementById('pkks-nama-folder-baru').value = '';
      document.getElementById('pkks-nama-folder-baru').placeholder = 'Contoh: Subfolder 1';
      openModal('modalBuatFolderPKKS');
      refreshIcons();
    }


    /* =========================================================
       17. MODUL MAPPING (Raport / Ijazah / Buku Induk)
       ========================================================= */

    function settingMappingRaport() {
      showBanner('Memuat Mapping Raport...', 'info');
      if (typeof google === 'undefined' || !google.script || !google.script.run) return;

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(data) {
          var html = '<div class="mb-3"><input id="cariMappingRaport" oninput="filterMappingRaport()" placeholder="Cari mata pelajaran..." class="w-full px-3.5 py-2 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"></div>' +
            '<div class="overflow-x-auto max-h-96 overflow-y-auto"><table class="w-full text-left text-xs text-slate-300"><thead class="bg-slate-950 text-slate-400 font-semibold text-[11px] sticky top-0 z-10 border-b border-slate-800"><tr><th class="px-4 py-2.5">Mata Pelajaran</th><th class="px-4 py-2.5">Cell Teori</th><th class="px-4 py-2.5">Cell Praktek</th><th class="px-4 py-2.5">Terbilang Teori</th><th class="px-4 py-2.5">Terbilang Praktek</th></tr></thead><tbody class="divide-y divide-slate-800">';

          if (data && data.length > 0) {
            data.forEach(function(r) {
              html += '<tr class="row-mapping hover:bg-slate-800/50 transition" data-key="' + r[0] + '">' +
                '<td class="px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-white">' + r[0] + '</td>' +
                '<td class="px-4 py-2.5"><input class="cell-teori w-full px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg text-xs text-white" data-key="' + r[0] + '" value="' + (r[1] || '') + '"></td>' +
                '<td class="px-4 py-2.5"><input class="cell-praktek w-full px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg text-xs text-white" data-key="' + r[0] + '" value="' + (r[2] || '') + '"></td>' +
                '<td class="px-4 py-2.5"><input class="cell-terbilang-teori w-full px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg text-xs text-white" data-key="' + r[0] + '" value="' + (r[3] || '') + '"></td>' +
                '<td class="px-4 py-2.5"><input class="cell-terbilang-praktek w-full px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg text-xs text-white" data-key="' + r[0] + '" value="' + (r[4] || '') + '"></td>' +
              '</tr>';
            });
          } else {
            html += '<tr><td colspan="5" class="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">Belum ada data mapping.</td></tr>';
          }

          html += '</tbody></table></div><div class="flex justify-end gap-2 mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-slate-800">' +
            '<button onclick="closeMappingModal()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 text-xs"><i data-lucide="x" class="w-3.5 h-3.5 inline mr-1"></i>Tutup</button>' +
            '<button onclick="simpanMappingRaport()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white text-xs font-semibold"><i data-lucide="save" class="w-3.5 h-3.5 inline mr-1"></i>Simpan</button></div>';

          showMappingModal('Mapping Raport', html);
          refreshIcons();
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) { showBanner('Gagal memuat mapping: ' + err.message, 'error'); })
        .getMappingRaport();
    }

    function settingMappingIjazah() {
      showBanner('Memuat Mapping Ijazah...', 'info');
      if (typeof google === 'undefined' || !google.script || !google.script.run) return;

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(data) {
          var html = '<div class="mb-3"><input id="cariMappingIjazah" oninput="filterMappingIjazah()" placeholder="Cari KEY..." class="w-full px-3.5 py-2 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"></div>' +
            '<div class="overflow-x-auto max-h-96 overflow-y-auto"><table class="w-full text-left text-xs text-slate-300"><thead class="bg-slate-950 text-slate-400 font-semibold text-[11px] sticky top-0 z-10 border-b border-slate-800"><tr><th class="px-4 py-2.5">KEY</th><th class="px-4 py-2.5">CELL TEORI</th></tr></thead><tbody class="divide-y divide-slate-800">';

          if (data && data.length > 0) {
            data.forEach(function(r) {
              html += '<tr class="row-mapping-ijazah hover:bg-slate-800/50 transition" data-key="' + r[0] + '">' +
                '<td class="px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-white">' + r[0] + '</td>' +
                '<td class="px-4 py-2.5"><input class="cell-ijazah w-full px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg text-xs text-white" data-key="' + r[0] + '" value="' + (r[1] || '') + '"></td>' +
              '</tr>';
            });
          } else {
            html += '<tr><td colspan="2" class="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">Belum ada data mapping ijazah.</td></tr>';
          }

          html += '</tbody></table></div><div class="flex justify-end gap-2 mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-slate-800">' +
            '<button onclick="closeMappingModal()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 text-xs">Tutup</button>' +
            '<button onclick="simpanMappingIjazah()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white text-xs font-semibold">Simpan</button></div>';

          showMappingModal('Mapping Ijazah', html);
          refreshIcons();
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) { showBanner('Gagal memuat: ' + err.message, 'error'); })
        .getMappingIjazah();
    }

    function showMappingModal(title, content) {
      var modal = document.getElementById('mappingModalContainer');
      if (!modal) {
        modal = document.createElement('div');
        modal.id = 'mappingModalContainer';
        modal.className = 'fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-950/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4';
        document.body.appendChild(modal);
      }
      modal.innerHTML = '<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-3xl w-full max-w-2xl max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">' +
        '<div class="px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between flex-shrink-0">' +
        '<h3 class="text-sm font-bold text-white font-heading flex items-center gap-2"><i data-lucide="map" class="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400"></i>' + title + '</h3>' +
        '<button onclick="closeMappingModal()" class="text-slate-400 hover:text-white"><i data-lucide="x" class="w-5 h-5"></i></button></div>' +
        '<div class="p-6 overflow-y-auto flex-1">' + content + '</div></div>';
      modal.style.display = 'flex';
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function closeMappingModal() {
      var modal = document.getElementById('mappingModalContainer');
      if (modal) { modal.style.display = 'none'; document.body.style.overflow = ''; }
    }

    function filterMappingRaport() {
      var keyword = document.getElementById('cariMappingRaport').value.toLowerCase().trim();
      document.querySelectorAll('.row-mapping').forEach(function(row) {
        row.style.display = (keyword === '' || row.dataset.key.toLowerCase().includes(keyword)) ? '' : 'none';
      });
    }

    function filterMappingIjazah() {
      var keyword = document.getElementById('cariMappingIjazah').value.toLowerCase().trim();
      document.querySelectorAll('.row-mapping-ijazah').forEach(function(row) {
        row.style.display = (keyword === '' || row.dataset.key.toLowerCase().includes(keyword)) ? '' : 'none';
      });
    }

    function simpanMappingRaport() {
      showBanner('Menyimpan Mapping Raport...', 'info');
      var data = [];
      document.querySelectorAll('.cell-teori').forEach(function(teori) {
        var key = teori.dataset.key;
        var praktek = document.querySelector('.cell-praktek[data-key="' + key + '"]');
        var tt = document.querySelector('.cell-terbilang-teori[data-key="' + key + '"]');
        var tp = document.querySelector('.cell-terbilang-praktek[data-key="' + key + '"]');
        data.push({
          key: key, teori: teori.value.trim(),
          praktek: praktek ? praktek.value.trim() : '',
          terbilangTeori: tt ? tt.value.trim() : '',
          terbilangPraktek: tp ? tp.value.trim() : ''
        });
      });

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function() {
            showBanner('Mapping Raport berhasil disimpan!', 'success');
            setTimeout(closeMappingModal, 1000);
          })
          .withFailureHandler(function(err) { showBanner('Gagal menyimpan: ' + err.message, 'error'); })
          .saveMappingRaport(data);
      }
    }

    function simpanMappingIjazah() {
      showBanner('Menyimpan Mapping Ijazah...', 'info');
      var data = [];
      document.querySelectorAll('.cell-ijazah').forEach(function(el) {
        data.push({ key: el.dataset.key, nilai: el.value.trim() });
      });

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function() {
            showBanner('Mapping Ijazah berhasil disimpan!', 'success');
            setTimeout(closeMappingModal, 1000);
          })
          .withFailureHandler(function(err) { showBanner('Gagal menyimpan: ' + err.message, 'error'); })
          .saveMappingIjazah(data);
      }
    }

    function settingMappingBukuInduk() {
      showBanner('Memuat Mapping Buku Induk...', 'info');
      if (typeof google === 'undefined' || !google.script || !google.script.run) return;

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(result) {
          var data = result.data || [];
          var headers = result.headers || ['KEY','CELL TEORI SMT 1','CELL PRAKTEK SMT 1','CELL TEORI SMT 2','CELL PRAKTEK SMT 2','CELL TEORI SMT 3','CELL PRAKTEK SMT 3','CELL TEORI SMT 4','CELL PRAKTEK SMT 4','CELL TEORI SMT 5','CELL PRAKTEK SMT 5','CELL TEORI SMT 6','CELL PRAKTEK SMT 6'];

          var headerHtml = '<tr>';
          headers.forEach(function(h) {
            headerHtml += '<th class="px-2 py-2 border border-slate-700 text-center text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-300 whitespace-nowrap">' + h + '</th>';
          });
          headerHtml += '</tr>';

          var rowsHtml = '';
          if (data && data.length > 0) {
            data.forEach(function(row, i) {
              var key = row[0] || '';
              rowsHtml += '<tr class="hover:bg-slate-800/50 transition" data-key="' + key + '">';
              for (var j = 0; j < 13; j++) {
                var val = row[j] || '';
                rowsHtml += '<td class="px-1 py-1 border border-slate-700">';
                if (j === 0) rowsHtml += '<span class="text-xs text-white block px-1">' + val + '</span>';
                else rowsHtml += '<input id="cell_' + i + '_' + j + '" value="' + val + '" class="w-full px-1 py-0.5 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded text-[10px] text-white" style="min-width:50px;">';
                rowsHtml += '</td>';
              }
              rowsHtml += '</tr>';
            });
          } else {
            rowsHtml = '<tr><td colspan="13" class="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">Belum ada data mapping.</td></tr>';
          }

          var html = '<div class="mb-3"><input id="cariMappingBukuInduk" oninput="filterMappingBukuInduk()" placeholder="Cari KEY..." class="w-full px-3.5 py-2 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-white"></div>' +
            '<div class="overflow-x-auto max-h-96 overflow-y-auto"><table class="w-full text-left text-xs text-slate-300 border-collapse"><thead class="bg-slate-950 text-slate-400 font-semibold text-[10px] sticky top-0 z-10 border-b border-slate-800">' + headerHtml + '</thead><tbody class="divide-y divide-slate-800" id="bodyMappingBukuInduk">' + rowsHtml + '</tbody></table></div>' +
            '<div class="flex justify-end gap-2 mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-slate-800">' +
              '<button onclick="closeMappingModal()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 text-xs">Tutup</button>' +
              '<button onclick="simpanMappingBukuIndukBaru()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white text-xs font-semibold">Simpan</button>' +
            '</div>';

          showMappingModal('Mapping Buku Induk', html);
          refreshIcons();
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) { showBanner('Gagal memuat: ' + err.message, 'error'); })
        .settingMappingBukuInduk();
    }

    function simpanMappingBukuIndukBaru() {
      showBanner('Menyimpan Mapping Buku Induk...', 'info');
      var data = [];
      var rows = document.querySelectorAll('#bodyMappingBukuInduk tr');
      rows.forEach(function(row) {
        if (row.dataset.key === undefined) return;
        var rowData = [row.dataset.key];
        var inputs = row.querySelectorAll('input');
        inputs.forEach(function(input) { rowData.push(input.value.trim().toUpperCase()); });
        while (rowData.length < 13) rowData.push('');
        data.push(rowData);
      });

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function(result) {
            if (result.success) {
              showBanner('Mapping Buku Induk berhasil disimpan!', 'success');
              setTimeout(closeMappingModal, 1000);
            } else showBanner('Gagal menyimpan!', 'error');
          })
          .withFailureHandler(function(err) { showBanner('Error: ' + err.message, 'error'); })
          .saveMappingBukuInduk(data);
      }
    }

    function filterMappingBukuInduk() {
      var keyword = document.getElementById('cariMappingBukuInduk').value.toLowerCase().trim();
      document.querySelectorAll('#bodyMappingBukuInduk tr').forEach(function(row) {
        var key = row.dataset.key || '';
        row.style.display = (keyword === '' || key.toLowerCase().includes(keyword)) ? '' : 'none';
      });
    }


    /* =========================================================
       18. MODUL BUKU INDUK
       ========================================================= */

    function bukaFormBukuInduk() {
      // Kalau sedang di halaman detail siswa → langsung cetak siswa itu
      if (currentDetailSiswaNoInduk) {
        cetakBukuInduk(currentDetailSiswaNoInduk);
        return;
      }

      // Kalau tidak → buka modal pilih siswa
      semuaSiswaBukuInduk = appState.siswa.slice();
      var search = document.getElementById('searchBukuIndukSiswa');
      if (search) search.value = '';
      populateBukuIndukSelect('');
      openModal('modalPilihBukuInduk');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function cetakBukuInduk(noInduk) {
      if (!noInduk) return;
      closeModal('modalPilihBukuInduk');
      showBanner('⏳ Membuka Buku Induk...', 'info');

      getWebAppUrl().then(function(webAppUrl) {
        if (!webAppUrl) webAppUrl = window.location.origin + window.location.pathname;
        var params = new URLSearchParams({ page: 'buku_induk', noInduk: noInduk });
        window.open(webAppUrl + '?' + params.toString(), '_blank');
        showBanner('✅ Buku Induk berhasil dibuka!', 'success');
      });
    }

    function filterBukuIndukSiswa() {
      populateBukuIndukSelect(document.getElementById('searchBukuIndukSiswa').value.toLowerCase().trim());
    }

    function populateBukuIndukSelect(search) {
      var select = document.getElementById('selectBukuIndukSiswa');
      if (!select) return;
      var filtered = semuaSiswaBukuInduk;
      if (search) {
        filtered = semuaSiswaBukuInduk.filter(function(s) {
          return (s.nama || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
                 (s.noInduk || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
                 (s.nisn || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
                 (s.kelas || '').toLowerCase().includes(search);
        });
      }
      filtered.sort(function(a, b) { return (a.nama || '').localeCompare(b.nama || '', 'id', { sensitivity: 'base' }); });

      select.innerHTML = '';
      if (filtered.length === 0) {
        var opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = ''; opt.textContent = '-- Tidak ada siswa --';
        select.appendChild(opt);
      } else {
        filtered.forEach(function(s) {
          var option = document.createElement('option');
          option.value = s.noInduk;
          option.textContent = s.noInduk + ' - ' + s.nama + ' (' + (s.kelas || '-') + ')';
          select.appendChild(option);
        });
      }
      var info = document.getElementById('bukuIndukJumlahSiswa');
      if (info) info.textContent = filtered.length + ' siswa tersedia';
    }

    function prosesBukuInduk() {
      var noInduk = document.getElementById('selectBukuIndukSiswa').value;
      if (!noInduk) { showBanner('Pilih siswa terlebih dahulu!', 'error'); return; }
      cetakBukuInduk(noInduk);
    }

        /* =========================================================
       19. MODUL MUADALAH
       ========================================================= */

    function initMuadalahTab() {
      muatFilterMuadalah();
    }

    function muatFilterMuadalah() {
      if (typeof google === 'undefined' || !google.script || !google.script.run) return;

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          var sel = document.getElementById('muadalah-filter-tahun');
          if (!sel || !res || res.status !== 'success') return;
          var current = sel.value;
          sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Tahun --</option>';
          (res.data || []).forEach(function(t) {
            var opt = document.createElement('option');
            opt.value = t; opt.textContent = t;
            sel.appendChild(opt);
          });
          if (current) sel.value = current;
        })
        .getDaftarTahunPelajaranMuadalah();

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          var sel = document.getElementById('muadalah-filter-kelas');
          if (!sel || !res || res.status !== 'success') return;
          var current = sel.value;
          sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Kelas --</option>';
          (res.data || []).forEach(function(k) {
            var opt = document.createElement('option');
            opt.value = k; opt.textContent = k;
            sel.appendChild(opt);
          });
          if (current) sel.value = current;
        })
        .getDaftarKelasMuadalah();
    }

    function muatNilaiMuadalah() {
      var tahun = document.getElementById('muadalah-filter-tahun').value;
      var kelas = document.getElementById('muadalah-filter-kelas').value;
      var smtAktif = document.getElementById('muadalah-filter-semester').value;

      if (!tahun || !kelas || !smtAktif) {
        showBanner('Pilih Tahun Pelajaran, Kelas, dan Semester terlebih dahulu!', 'error');
        return;
      }

      if (typeof google === 'undefined' || !google.script || !google.script.run) {
        showBanner('Mode offline: tidak bisa memuat data.', 'error');
        return;
      }

      showBanner('⏳ Memuat data nilai...', 'info');
      document.getElementById('muadalah-empty').classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById('muadalah-table-wrapper').classList.add('hidden');

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            muadalahState.konfigAktif = res.konfig;
            muadalahState.siswa = res.siswa || [];
            populateFilterRombelMuadalah();
            renderMuadalahTable();
            showBanner('✅ ' + muadalahState.siswa.length + ' siswa dimuat.', 'success');
          } else {
            muadalahState.konfigAktif = null;
            muadalahState.siswa = [];
            document.getElementById('muadalah-table-wrapper').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('muadalah-empty').classList.remove('hidden');
            showBanner('❌ ' + (res.message || 'Konfigurasi tidak ditemukan'), 'error');
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          showBanner('❌ Error: ' + (err.message || err), 'error');
        })
        .getNilaiMuadalah(kelas, tahun, smtAktif);
    }

    function renderMuadalahTable() {
      var wrapper = document.getElementById('muadalah-table-wrapper');
      var thead = document.getElementById('muadalah-table-head');
      var tbody = document.getElementById('muadalah-table-body');
      var info = document.getElementById('muadalah-info-konfig');

      if (!wrapper || !thead || !tbody) return;

      if (!muadalahState.konfigAktif) {
        wrapper.classList.add('hidden');
        return;
      }

      wrapper.classList.remove('hidden');

      var konfig = muadalahState.konfigAktif;
      info.classList.remove('hidden');
      document.getElementById('muadalah-info-judul').textContent =
        'Kelas ' + konfig.kelas + ' • ' + konfig.tahunPelajaran + ' • Semester ' + konfig.semester;
      document.getElementById('muadalah-info-detail').textContent =
        konfig.jumlahMapel + ' mapel: ' + konfig.mapel.join(', ');

      var headerHtml = '<tr>' +
        '<th class="px-3 py-3 text-center w-10">No</th>' +
        '<th class="px-3 py-3 font-mono-code">No Induk</th>' +
        '<th class="px-3 py-3 min-w-[180px]">Nama</th>' +
        '<th class="px-3 py-3 text-center w-16">Kelas</th>';

      // Tampilkan kolom per mapel
      for (var m = 0; m < konfig.jumlahMapel; m++) {
        headerHtml += '<th class="px-3 py-3 text-center min-w-[70px]" title="' + konfig.mapel[m] + '">' + (m + 1) + '</th>';
      }

      headerHtml +=
        '<th class="px-3 py-3 text-center w-20 bg-emerald-950/40">Jumlah</th>' +
        '<th class="px-3 py-3 text-center w-24 bg-emerald-950/40">Rata-rata</th>' +
        '<th class="px-3 py-3 text-center w-20 bg-amber-950/40">Rank</th>' +
        '<th class="px-3 py-3 text-center w-32">Aksi</th>' +
        '</tr>';

      thead.innerHTML = headerHtml;

      renderMuadalahBody(tbody);
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function renderMuadalahBody(tbodyEl) {
      if (!tbodyEl) return;
      var konfig = muadalahState.konfigAktif;
      if (!konfig) return;

      var search = (document.getElementById('muadalah-search') && document.getElementById('muadalah-search').value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      var rombelFilter = (document.getElementById('muadalah-filter-rombel') || {}).value || '';

      var filtered = muadalahState.siswa;
      if (rombelFilter) {
        filtered = filtered.filter(function(s) {
          return String(s.kelas || '').trim() === rombelFilter;
        });
      }
      if (search) {
        filtered = filtered.filter(function(s) {
          return (s.nama || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(search) !== -1 ||
                (s.noInduk || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(search) !== -1;
        });
      }

      if (filtered.length === 0) {
        tbodyEl.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="' + (konfig.jumlahMapel + 7) + '" class="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">Tidak ada siswa.</td></tr>';
        return;
      }

      tbodyEl.innerHTML = filtered.map(function(s, idx) {
        var noIndukEsc = String(s.noInduk).replace(/\\/g, '\\\\').replace(/'/g, "\\'");

        var html = '<tr class="hover:bg-slate-800/40" data-row-index="' + idx + '">' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-2 text-center text-slate-400">' + (idx + 1) + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-2 font-mono-code font-bold text-white">' + s.noInduk + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-2 font-semibold text-slate-100">' + s.nama + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-2 text-center text-slate-300">' + (s.kelas || '-') + '</td>';

        // Kolom nilai per mapel
        for (var m = 0; m < konfig.jumlahMapel; m++) {
          var v = s.nilai[m];
          var val = (v === '' || v === null || v === undefined) ? '-' : String(v);
          html += '<td class="px-3 py-2 text-center font-mono-code text-slate-200">' + val + '</td>';
        }

        // Jumlah / Rata / Ranking
        var jml = parseFloat(s.jumlah);
        var rr = parseFloat(s.rataRata);
        var rk = s.ranking;

        html += '<td class="px-3 py-2 text-center font-mono-code text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-950/20">' +
          (!isNaN(jml) && jml > 0 ? Math.round(jml) : '-') + '</td>';
        html += '<td class="px-3 py-2 text-center font-mono-code font-bold text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-950/20">' +
          (!isNaN(rr) && rr > 0 ? potongDuaDesimalJS(rr) : '-') + '</td>';
        html += '<td class="px-3 py-2 text-center font-mono-code text-amber-400 bg-amber-950/20">' +
          (rk && rk !== '' ? rk : '-') + '</td>';

        // Aksi
        html += '<td class="px-3 py-2 text-center"><div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">' +
          '<button onclick="lihatMuadalahSiswa(\'' + noIndukEsc + '\')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-blue-400" title="Lihat"><i data-lucide="eye" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i></button>' +
          '<button onclick="editMuadalahSiswa(\'' + noIndukEsc + '\')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-amber-400" title="Edit"><i data-lucide="edit-3" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i></button>' +
        '</div></td>';

        html += '</tr>';
        return html;
      }).join('');

      updateMuadalahCounter();
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function filterMuadalahTable() {
      var tbody = document.getElementById('muadalah-table-body');
      renderMuadalahBody(tbody);
    }

    function populateFilterRombelMuadalah() {
      var sel = document.getElementById('muadalah-filter-rombel');
      if (!sel) return;

      var set = {};
      (muadalahState.siswa || []).forEach(function(s) {
        var k = String(s.kelas || '').trim();
        if (k) set[k] = true;
      });

      var arr = Object.keys(set);
      arr.sort(function(a, b) { return a.localeCompare(b, 'id', { sensitivity: 'base' }); });

      var current = sel.value;
      sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Semua Rombel</option>';
      arr.forEach(function(k) {
        var opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = k; opt.textContent = k;
        sel.appendChild(opt);
      });
      if (current && set[current]) sel.value = current;
    }

    function updateMuadalahCounter() {
      var total = muadalahState.siswa.length;
      var elTotal = document.getElementById('muadalah-total-siswa');
      var elBottom = document.getElementById('muadalah-total-bottom');
      if (elTotal) elTotal.textContent = total;
      if (elBottom) elBottom.textContent = total;
    }

    /* ----- Lihat / Edit / Hapus Muadalah ----- */

    function lihatMuadalahSiswa(noInduk) {
      var siswa = muadalahState.siswa.find(function(s) { return s.noInduk === noInduk; });
      if (!siswa) return;
      var konfig = muadalahState.konfigAktif;

      var html = '<div class="p-3 bg-slate-950/60 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 mb-3">' +
        '<div class="text-sm font-bold text-white">' + siswa.nama + '</div>' +
        '<div class="text-[11px] text-slate-400">No Induk: ' + siswa.noInduk + ' • Kelas: ' + (siswa.kelas || '-') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="text-[11px] text-slate-400">' + konfig.tahunPelajaran + ' • Semester ' + konfig.semester + '</div>' +
      '</div>';

      html += '<div class="border border-slate-800 rounded-xl overflow-hidden">' +
        '<table class="w-full text-xs text-slate-300">' +
          '<thead class="bg-slate-950 text-slate-400 font-semibold">' +
            '<tr><th class="px-3 py-2 text-center w-10">No</th><th class="px-3 py-2">Mapel</th><th class="px-3 py-2 text-center w-20">Nilai</th></tr>' +
          '</thead><tbody class="divide-y divide-slate-800">';

      var total = 0, count = 0;
      konfig.mapel.forEach(function(m, i) {
        var v = siswa.nilai[i];
        var display = (v === '' || v === null || v === undefined) ? '-' : String(v);
        if (v !== '' && v !== null && v !== undefined && !isNaN(parseFloat(v))) {
          total += parseFloat(v); count++;
        }
        html += '<tr><td class="px-3 py-2 text-center text-slate-400">' + (i + 1) + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-2 font-medium text-white">' + m + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-2 text-center font-mono-code font-bold text-emerald-400">' + display + '</td></tr>';
      });

      var rata = count > 0 ? total / count : 0;
      html += '</tbody><tfoot class="bg-slate-950/60 text-xs font-bold">' +
        '<tr><td colspan="2" class="px-3 py-2 text-right text-slate-300">JUMLAH:</td><td class="px-3 py-2 text-center text-emerald-400">' + (count > 0 ? Math.round(total) : '-') + '</td></tr>' +
        '<tr><td colspan="2" class="px-3 py-2 text-right text-slate-300">RATA-RATA:</td><td class="px-3 py-2 text-center text-emerald-400">' + (count > 0 ? potongDuaDesimalJS(rata) : '-') + '</td></tr>' +
      '</tfoot></table></div>';

      showInfoMuadalahModal('Detail Nilai: ' + siswa.nama, html);
    }

    function editMuadalahSiswa(noInduk) {
      var siswa = muadalahState.siswa.find(function(s) { return s.noInduk === noInduk; });
      if (!siswa) return;
      var konfig = muadalahState.konfigAktif;

      var html = '<div class="p-3 bg-slate-950/60 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 mb-3">' +
        '<div class="text-sm font-bold text-white">' + siswa.nama + '</div>' +
        '<div class="text-[11px] text-slate-400">No Induk: ' + siswa.noInduk + ' • Kelas: ' + (siswa.kelas || '-') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="text-[11px] text-slate-400">' + konfig.tahunPelajaran + ' • Semester ' + konfig.semester + '</div>' +
      '</div>';

      html += '<div class="space-y-2">';

      konfig.mapel.forEach(function(m, i) {
        var v = siswa.nilai[i];
        var val = (v === '' || v === null || v === undefined) ? '' : v;
        html += '<div class="flex items-center gap-3">' +
          '<label class="flex-1 text-xs text-slate-300">' + (i + 1) + '. ' + m + '</label>' +
          '<input type="text" inputmode="decimal" class="edit-muadalah-nilai w-24 px-3 py-2 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg text-xs text-white text-center focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500" data-idx="' + i + '" value="' + val + '">' +
        '</div>';
      });

      html += '</div>';

      html += '<div class="flex justify-end gap-2 mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-slate-800">' +
        '<button onclick="closeModal(\'modalInfoMuadalah\')" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 text-xs">Batal</button>' +
        '<button onclick="simpanEditMuadalah(\'' + siswa.noInduk + '\')" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-semibold text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5">' +
          '<i data-lucide="save" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i><span>Simpan</span>' +
        '</button>' +
      '</div>';

      showInfoMuadalahModal('Edit Nilai: ' + siswa.nama, html);
    }

    function simpanEditMuadalah(noInduk) {
      var siswa = muadalahState.siswa.find(function(s) { return s.noInduk === noInduk; });
      if (!siswa) return;
      var konfig = muadalahState.konfigAktif;

      var inputs = document.querySelectorAll('#modalInfoMuadalah .edit-muadalah-nilai');
      var nilaiArr = [];
      inputs.forEach(function(inp) {
        var v = String(inp.value || '').trim();
        if (v === '') nilaiArr.push('');
        else {
          var num = parseFloat(v);
          nilaiArr.push(isNaN(num) ? '' : num);
        }
      });

      var payload = {
        kelas: konfig.kelas,
        tahunPelajaran: konfig.tahunPelajaran,
        semester: konfig.semester,
        siswa: [{
          noInduk: siswa.noInduk,
          nama: siswa.nama,
          kelas: siswa.kelas || konfig.kelas,
          nilai: nilaiArr
        }]
      };

      showBanner('⏳ Menyimpan...', 'info');
      closeModal('modalInfoMuadalah');

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            showBanner('✅ ' + res.message, 'success');
            setTimeout(function() { muatNilaiMuadalah(); }, 800);
          } else {
            showBanner('❌ ' + (res.message || 'Gagal simpan'), 'error');
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          showBanner('❌ Error: ' + (err.message || err), 'error');
        })
        .saveNilaiMuadalah(payload);
    }

    function showInfoMuadalahModal(title, content) {
      var modal = document.getElementById('modalInfoMuadalah');
      if (!modal) {
        modal = document.createElement('div');
        modal.id = 'modalInfoMuadalah';
        modal.className = 'hidden fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-950/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4';
        document.body.appendChild(modal);
      }
      modal.innerHTML = '<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-3xl w-full max-w-lg max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">' +
        '<div class="px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between flex-shrink-0">' +
          '<h3 class="text-sm font-bold text-white font-heading">' + title + '</h3>' +
          '<button onclick="closeModal(\'modalInfoMuadalah\')" class="text-slate-400 hover:text-white">✕</button>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div class="p-5 overflow-y-auto flex-1">' + content + '</div>' +
      '</div>';
      modal.classList.remove('hidden');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    /* =========================================================
       MODAL KONFIGURASI MUADALAH
       ========================================================= */

    var _konfigMuadalahList = [];
    var _konfigMapelRows = 0;
    var _konfigEditOriginal = null;

    function bukaModalKonfigMuadalah() {
      if (typeof google === 'undefined' || !google.script || !google.script.run) {
        showBanner('Mode offline', 'error');
        return;
      }

      showBanner('⏳ Memuat konfigurasi...', 'info');

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (!res || res.status !== 'success') {
            showBanner('❌ ' + (res.message || 'Gagal'), 'error');
            return;
          }
          _konfigMuadalahList = res.data || [];
          renderKonfigListModal();
          openModal('modalKonfigMuadalah');
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          showBanner('❌ Error: ' + (err.message || err), 'error');
        })
        .getAllKonfigMuadalah();
    }

    function renderKonfigListModal() {
      var tbody = document.getElementById('konfig-muadalah-list');
      if (!tbody) return;

      if (_konfigMuadalahList.length === 0) {
        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="6" class="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">Belum ada konfigurasi.</td></tr>';
        return;
      }

      tbody.innerHTML = _konfigMuadalahList.map(function(k, idx) {
        var tahunEsc = String(k.tahunPelajaran).replace(/'/g, "\\'");
        var kelasEsc = String(k.kelas).replace(/'/g, "\\'");
        return '<tr class="hover:bg-slate-800/40">' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-2.5 text-center text-slate-400">' + (idx + 1) + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-2.5 font-semibold text-white">' + k.tahunPelajaran + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-2.5 text-center"><span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-800 text-slate-200">' + k.kelas + '</span></td>' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-2.5 text-center font-bold text-emerald-400">' + k.jumlahMapel + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-2.5 text-slate-300">' + k.mapel.join(', ') + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-3 py-2.5 text-center"><div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">' +
            '<button onclick="editKonfigMuadalah(\'' + tahunEsc + '\', \'' + kelasEsc + '\')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-amber-400" title="Edit">' +
              '<i data-lucide="edit-3" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>' +
            '</button>' +
            '<button onclick="hapusKonfigMuadalah(\'' + tahunEsc + '\', \'' + kelasEsc + '\')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-red-950 text-red-400" title="Hapus">' +
              '<i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>' +
            '</button>' +
          '</div></td>' +
        '</tr>';
      }).join('');

      refreshIcons();
    }

    function bukaModalTambahKonfig() {
      _konfigEditOriginal = null;
      document.getElementById('modalKonfigTitle').textContent = 'Tambah Konfigurasi Baru';
      document.getElementById('konfig-tahun').value = '';
      document.getElementById('konfig-kelas').value = '';

      document.getElementById('konfig-mapel-list').innerHTML = '';
      _konfigMapelRows = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < 5; i++) tambahBarisMapelKonfig();

      openModal('modalTambahKonfig');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function tambahBarisMapelKonfig(namaAwal) {
      var container = document.getElementById('konfig-mapel-list');
      if (!container) return;

      _konfigMapelRows++;
      var idx = _konfigMapelRows;

      var div = document.createElement('div');
      div.className = 'flex items-center gap-2 konfig-mapel-row';
      div.dataset.idx = idx;

      div.innerHTML =
        '<span class="text-slate-500 text-xs w-6 text-right shrink-0">' + idx + '.</span>' +
        '<input type="text" class="konfig-mapel-input flex-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500" placeholder="Nama mapel (kosongkan jika tidak dipakai)" value="' + (namaAwal || '') + '">' +
        '<button onclick="hapusBarisMapelKonfig(this)" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-red-950 text-red-400 shrink-0" title="Hapus baris">' +
          '<i data-lucide="x" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>' +
        '</button>';

      container.appendChild(div);
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function hapusBarisMapelKonfig(btn) {
      var row = btn.closest('.konfig-mapel-row');
      if (row) row.remove();
      var rows = document.querySelectorAll('#konfig-mapel-list .konfig-mapel-row');
      rows.forEach(function(r, i) {
        var span = r.querySelector('span');
        if (span) span.textContent = (i + 1) + '.';
      });
      _konfigMapelRows = rows.length;
    }

    function editKonfigMuadalah(tahun, kelas) {
      var konfig = _konfigMuadalahList.find(function(k) {
        return k.tahunPelajaran === tahun && k.kelas === kelas;
      });
      if (!konfig) return;

      _konfigEditOriginal = {
        tahun: konfig.tahunPelajaran,
        kelas: konfig.kelas
      };

      document.getElementById('modalKonfigTitle').textContent = 'Edit Konfigurasi: ' + kelas + ' • ' + tahun;
      document.getElementById('konfig-tahun').value = konfig.tahunPelajaran;
      document.getElementById('konfig-kelas').value = konfig.kelas;

      document.getElementById('konfig-mapel-list').innerHTML = '';
      _konfigMapelRows = 0;

      konfig.mapel.forEach(function(m) {
        tambahBarisMapelKonfig(m);
      });

      for (var i = 0; i < 2; i++) tambahBarisMapelKonfig();

      closeModal('modalKonfigMuadalah');
      openModal('modalTambahKonfig');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function simpanKonfigMuadalahUI() {
      var tahun = document.getElementById('konfig-tahun').value.trim();
      var kelas = document.getElementById('konfig-kelas').value.trim();

      if (!tahun || !kelas) {
        showBanner('Tahun Pelajaran dan Kelas harus diisi!', 'error');
        return;
      }

      var mapelInputs = document.querySelectorAll('#konfig-mapel-list .konfig-mapel-input');
      var mapel = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < mapelInputs.length; i++) {
        var v = mapelInputs[i].value.trim();
        if (!v) break;
        mapel.push(v);
      }

      if (mapel.length === 0) {
        showBanner('Isi minimal 1 mapel!', 'error');
        return;
      }

      var payload = {
        tahunPelajaran: tahun,
        kelas: kelas,
        mapel: mapel
      };

      if (_konfigEditOriginal) {
        payload.originalTahun = _konfigEditOriginal.tahun;
        payload.originalKelas = _konfigEditOriginal.kelas;
      }

      showBanner('⏳ Menyimpan konfigurasi...', 'info');

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            showBanner('✅ ' + res.message, 'success');
            closeModal('modalTambahKonfig');
            _konfigEditOriginal = null;
            bukaModalKonfigMuadalah();
            muatFilterMuadalah();
          } else {
            showBanner('❌ ' + (res.message || 'Gagal simpan'), 'error');
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          showBanner('❌ Error: ' + (err.message || err), 'error');
        })
        .saveKonfigMuadalah(payload);
    }

    function batalKonfigMuadalah() {
      _konfigEditOriginal = null;
      closeModal('modalTambahKonfig');
    }

    function hapusKonfigMuadalah(tahun, kelas) {
      if (!confirm('Hapus konfigurasi ' + kelas + ' • ' + tahun + '?\n\nCatatan: Data nilai siswa di sheet Muadalah TIDAK ikut terhapus.')) return;

      showBanner('⏳ Menghapus konfigurasi...', 'info');

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            showBanner('✅ ' + res.message, 'success');
            _konfigMuadalahList = _konfigMuadalahList.filter(function(k) {
              return !(k.tahunPelajaran === tahun && k.kelas === kelas);
            });
            renderKonfigListModal();
            muatFilterMuadalah();
          } else {
            showBanner('❌ ' + (res.message || 'Gagal hapus'), 'error');
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          showBanner('❌ Error: ' + (err.message || err), 'error');
        })
        .hapusKonfigMuadalah(tahun, kelas);
    }

    /* =========================================================
       HALAMAN EDIT MASSAL MUADALAH
       ========================================================= */

    function bukaHalamanEditMassal() {
      if (!muadalahState.konfigAktif) {
        showBanner('Muat data terlebih dahulu.', 'error');
        return;
      }
      if (muadalahState.siswa.length === 0) {
        showBanner('Tidak ada siswa untuk diedit.', 'error');
        return;
      }

      // ★ Terapkan filter rombel yang aktif
      var rombelFilter = (document.getElementById('muadalah-filter-rombel') || {}).value || '';
      var dataEdit = muadalahState.siswa;
      if (rombelFilter) {
        dataEdit = dataEdit.filter(function(s) {
          return String(s.kelas || '').trim() === rombelFilter;
        });
      }

      if (dataEdit.length === 0) {
        showBanner('Tidak ada siswa yang cocok dengan filter.', 'error');
        return;
      }

      // ★ Simpan data yang akan diedit ke state
      muadalahState.siswaMassal = dataEdit.slice();

      document.getElementById('view-muadalah').classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById('view-muadalah-massal').classList.remove('hidden');

      renderHalamanMassal();
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function kembaliKeMuadalah() {
      muadalahState.siswaMassal = [];
      document.getElementById('view-muadalah-massal').classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById('view-muadalah').classList.remove('hidden');
      refreshIcons();
    }

    function renderHalamanMassal() {
      var konfig = muadalahState.konfigAktif;
      if (!konfig) return;

      document.getElementById('massal-info-konfig').textContent =
        'Kelas ' + konfig.kelas + ' • ' + konfig.tahunPelajaran + ' • Semester ' + konfig.semester +
        ' • ' + konfig.jumlahMapel + ' mapel';

      var thead = document.getElementById('massal-table-head');
      var headerHtml = '<tr>' +
        '<th class="py-3 text-center bg-slate-950 sticky left-0 top-0 z-40" style="width:50px;min-width:50px;max-width:50px;">No</th>' +
        '<th class="py-3 font-mono-code bg-slate-950 sticky left-[50px] top-0 z-40" style="width:110px;min-width:110px;max-width:110px;">No Induk</th>' +
        '<th class="py-3 bg-slate-950 sticky left-[160px] top-0 z-40" style="width:200px;min-width:200px;max-width:200px;">Nama</th>';

      konfig.mapel.forEach(function(m) {
        headerHtml += '<th class="px-3 py-3 text-center min-w-[80px]" title="' + m + '">' + m + '</th>';
      });

      headerHtml +=
        '<th class="px-3 py-3 text-center w-20 bg-emerald-950/40">Jumlah</th>' +
        '<th class="px-3 py-3 text-center w-24 bg-emerald-950/40">Rata-rata</th>' +
        '</tr>';

      thead.innerHTML = headerHtml;

      var tbody = document.getElementById('massal-table-body');
      renderMassalBody(tbody);

      document.getElementById('massal-total').textContent = muadalahState.siswa.length;
      document.getElementById('massal-total-mapel').textContent = konfig.jumlahMapel;

      refreshIcons();
    }

    function renderMassalBody(tbodyEl) {
      if (!tbodyEl) return;
      var konfig = muadalahState.konfigAktif;
      if (!konfig) return;

      var siswaList = muadalahState.siswaMassal || [];

      tbodyEl.innerHTML = siswaList.map(function(s, idx) {
        var html = '<tr data-row-index="' + idx + '" class="hover:bg-slate-800/30">' +
          '<td class="px-2 py-1.5 text-center text-slate-400 bg-slate-900 sticky left-0 z-20" style="width:50px;min-width:50px;max-width:50px;">' + (idx + 1) + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-2 py-1.5 font-mono-code font-bold text-white bg-slate-900 sticky left-[50px] z-20" style="width:110px;min-width:110px;max-width:110px;">' + (s.noInduk || '-') + '</td>' +
          '<td class="px-2 py-1.5 font-semibold text-slate-100 bg-slate-900 sticky left-[160px] z-20" style="width:200px;min-width:200px;max-width:200px;">' + (s.nama || '-') + '</td>';

        for (var m = 0; m < konfig.jumlahMapel; m++) {
          var v = s.nilai[m];
          var val = (v === '' || v === null || v === undefined) ? '' : v;

          html += '<td class="px-1 py-1 text-center">' +
            '<input type="text" inputmode="decimal" ' +
            'class="massal-input-nilai w-full max-w-[70px] mx-auto px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded text-xs text-white text-center focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-500" ' +
            'data-idx="' + m + '" ' +
            'data-row="' + idx + '" ' +
            'oninput="onMassalInputChange(this)" ' +
            'onkeydown="handleEnterMassal(event, ' + idx + ', ' + m + ')" ' +
            'value="' + val + '">' +
          '</td>';
        }

        html += '<td class="px-3 py-1.5 text-center font-mono-code font-bold text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-950/20 massal-jumlah">-</td>';
        html += '<td class="px-3 py-1.5 text-center font-mono-code font-bold text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-950/20 massal-rata">-</td>';

        html += '</tr>';
        return html;
      }).join('');

      setTimeout(function() {
        hitungSemuaMassal();
        var firstInput = document.querySelector('#massal-table-body .massal-input-nilai');
        if (firstInput) {
          firstInput.focus();
          firstInput.select();
        }
      }, 50);
    }

    function handleEnterMassal(e, rowIdx, mapelIdx) {
      if (e.key !== 'Enter') return;
      e.preventDefault();

      var goUp = e.shiftKey;
      var targetRow = goUp ? rowIdx - 1 : rowIdx + 1;

      var rows = document.querySelectorAll('#massal-table-body tr[data-row-index]');
      if (targetRow < 0 || targetRow >= rows.length) return;

      var input = rows[targetRow].querySelector('.massal-input-nilai[data-idx="' + mapelIdx + '"]');
      if (input) {
        input.focus();
        input.select();
      }
    }

    function onMassalInputChange(input) {
      var tr = input.closest('tr');
      if (!tr) return;

      var inputs = tr.querySelectorAll('.massal-input-nilai');
      var total = 0;
      var count = 0;

      inputs.forEach(function(inp) {
        var v = String(inp.value || '').trim();
        if (v === '') return;
        var num = parseFloat(v);
        if (isNaN(num)) return;
        total += num;
        count++;
      });

      var rata = count > 0 ? total / count : 0;

      var elJumlah = tr.querySelector('.massal-jumlah');
      var elRata = tr.querySelector('.massal-rata');

      if (elJumlah) elJumlah.textContent = count > 0 ? String(Math.round(total * 100) / 100) : '-';
      if (elRata) elRata.textContent = count > 0 ? potongDuaDesimalJS(rata) : '-';

      hitungRataKelasMassal();
    }

    function hitungSemuaMassal() {
      var rows = document.querySelectorAll('#massal-table-body tr[data-row-index]');
      rows.forEach(function(tr) {
        var inputs = tr.querySelectorAll('.massal-input-nilai');
        var total = 0, count = 0;

        inputs.forEach(function(inp) {
          var v = String(inp.value || '').trim();
          if (v === '') return;
          var num = parseFloat(v);
          if (isNaN(num)) return;
          total += num;
          count++;
        });

        var rata = count > 0 ? total / count : 0;
        var elJumlah = tr.querySelector('.massal-jumlah');
        var elRata = tr.querySelector('.massal-rata');
        if (elJumlah) elJumlah.textContent = count > 0 ? String(Math.round(total * 100) / 100) : '-';
        if (elRata) elRata.textContent = count > 0 ? potongDuaDesimalJS(rata) : '-';
      });

      hitungRataKelasMassal();
    }

    function hitungRataKelasMassal() {
      var rows = document.querySelectorAll('#massal-table-body tr[data-row-index]');
      var totalRata = 0, count = 0;

      rows.forEach(function(tr) {
        var elRata = tr.querySelector('.massal-rata');
        if (!elRata) return;
        var v = elRata.textContent.trim();
        if (v === '-' || v === '') return;
        var num = parseFloat(v);
        if (isNaN(num)) return;
        totalRata += num;
        count++;
      });

      var el = document.getElementById('massal-avg-kelas');
      if (el) el.textContent = count > 0 ? potongDuaDesimalJS(totalRata / count) : '-';
    }

    function isiCepatKosongMassal() {
      if (!confirm('Kosongkan semua nilai di halaman ini?\n\n(Tidak akan tersimpan sampai klik "Simpan Semua")')) return;
      document.querySelectorAll('#massal-table-body .massal-input-nilai').forEach(function(inp) {
        inp.value = '';
      });
      hitungSemuaMassal();
    }

    function simpanMassalMuadalah() {
      var konfig = muadalahState.konfigAktif;
      if (!konfig) {
        showBanner('Konfigurasi tidak ditemukan!', 'error');
        return;
      }

      if (!muadalahState.siswaMassal || muadalahState.siswaMassal.length === 0) {
        showBanner('Data siswa kosong. Muat ulang data terlebih dahulu.', 'error');
        return;
      }
      var rows = document.querySelectorAll('#massal-table-body tr[data-row-index]');
      if (rows.length === 0) {
        showBanner('Tabel tidak memiliki baris. Muat ulang halaman.', 'error');
        return;
      }

      var siswaList = [];

      rows.forEach(function(tr) {
        var idx = parseInt(tr.dataset.rowIndex, 10);
        if (isNaN(idx)) return;

        var siswa = muadalahState.siswaMassal[idx];
        if (!siswa) return;

        var inputs = tr.querySelectorAll('.massal-input-nilai');
        var nilaiArr = [];
        for (var m = 0; m < konfig.jumlahMapel; m++) {
          var v = inputs[m] ? String(inputs[m].value || '').trim() : '';
          if (v === '') nilaiArr.push('');
          else {
            var num = parseFloat(v);
            nilaiArr.push(isNaN(num) ? '' : num);
          }
        }

        siswaList.push({
          noInduk: String(siswa.noInduk || '').trim(),
          nama: siswa.nama,
          kelas: siswa.kelas || konfig.kelas,
          nilai: nilaiArr
        });
      });

      if (siswaList.length === 0) {
        showBanner('Tidak ada siswa yang bisa disimpan!', 'error');
        return;
      }

      var payload = {
        kelas: konfig.kelas,
        tahunPelajaran: konfig.tahunPelajaran,
        semester: konfig.semester,
        siswa: siswaList
      };

      var btn = document.getElementById('btn-simpan-massal');
      if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
      showBanner('⏳ Menyimpan ' + siswaList.length + ' siswa...', 'info');

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            showBanner('✅ ' + res.message, 'success');
            setTimeout(function() {
              kembaliKeMuadalah();
              muatNilaiMuadalah();
            }, 800);
          } else {
            showBanner('❌ ' + (res.message || 'Gagal simpan'), 'error');
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
          showBanner('❌ Error: ' + (err.message || err), 'error');
        })
        .saveNilaiMuadalah(payload);
    }


    /* =========================================================
       20. SINKRONISASI DATA
       ========================================================= */

    function refreshDataFromSheets() {
      var icon = document.getElementById('refresh-icon');
      if (icon) icon.classList.add('animate-spin');

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
            if (icon) icon.classList.remove('animate-spin');
            if (res && res.status === 'success') {
              applyDataFromServer_(res);

              renderAllViews();

              setTimeout(function() {
                populateFilterKelasManual();
                populateFilterNabil();
                populateFilterSorogan();
              }, 100);

              setTimeout(function() {
                if (window._jadwalLoaded) {
                  var iframeJadwal = document.getElementById('iframeJadwal');
                  if (iframeJadwal && typeof kirimDataKeIframeJadwal === 'function') {
                    kirimDataKeIframeJadwal(iframeJadwal);
                  }
                }
              }, 500);

              // Reset cache PKKS
              _pkksFileCache = {};
              _pkksFolderCache = {};
              if (_pkksFolderLoaded) {
                _pkksFolderLoaded = false;
                if (_pkksCurrentFolder) {
                  _pkksCurrentFolderLoaded = false;
                  muatIsiFolderPKKS();
                } else {
                  muatDaftarFolderPKKS();
                }
              }

              showBanner('Sinkronisasi Berhasil!', 'success');
            } else {
              showBanner('Gagal sinkron data: ' + (res.message || 'Unknown error'), 'error');
            }
          })
          .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
            if (icon) icon.classList.remove('animate-spin');
            showBanner('Gagal sinkron data: ' + (err.message || 'Unknown error'), 'error');
            renderAllViews();
          })
          .getInitialDataDirect();
      } else {
        setTimeout(function() {
          if (icon) icon.classList.remove('animate-spin');
          renderAllViews();
          showBanner('Mode offline: Data lokal digunakan.', 'info');
        }, 600);
      }
    }


    /* =========================================================
